const text = v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const dt=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ko-KR',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',hour12:false,hourCycle:'h23'});
const clockFmt=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',hourCycle:'h23'});
const fmt=v=>{if(!v||!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(v)))return '—';const p=Object.fromEntries(dt.formatToParts(new Date(v)).map(x=>[x.type,x.value]));return p.month+'.'+p.day+' '+p.hour+':'+p.minute;};
function clock(){ $('#kst').innerHTML=clockFmt.format(new Date())+' <small>KST</small>'; }

let chatTerminal=null;
const openingMessages=[];
function showChat(message,mine){
  if(message.terminal!==chatTerminal)return;
  if(world?.available){world.sayChat?.(message.avatarId,message.text,mine||selfAvatarId===message.avatarId);return;}
  if(openingMessages.length>=64)openingMessages.shift();
  openingMessages.push({message,mine});
}
const chat=mountHarborChat(hud,{api,onClose:focusWorld,onMessage:showChat});
function setChatTerminal(id){
  id='busan';
  if(chatTerminal!==id){openingMessages.length=0;chatTerminal=id;}
  chat.setTerminal(id);
}
setChatTerminal('busan');
function focusWorld(){if(playing&&!dialog.open)$('#world canvas[tabindex], #world [tabindex]')?.focus({preventScroll:true});}
function dockMode(id){return id==='arrival'?'pull':id==='departure'?'push':null;}
function dockCall(kind){
  if(scheduleFailed || state.source?.stale || !hasSession || (terminal && terminal!==state.terminal))return null;
  return kind==='pull'||kind==='push'?invocationCall():null;
}
function callName(call,kind){const voy=kind==='pull'?call.inboundVoy:call.outboundVoy;return [call.name,voy||call.voy||call.carrierVoy||call.terminalVoy].filter(Boolean).join(' ');}
function timing(call,kind){return call.atd?'ATD '+fmt(call.atd):kind==='pull'?'ETB '+fmt(call.etb):'ETD '+fmt(call.etd);}
function setNearby(id){
  if(nearby!==id){
    dialogue.hide();nearby=id;presence?.wake();
    const next=dockMode(id);
    if(next&&mode!==next){
      if(next==='pull'&&!selectedId&&state.selected)selectedId=state.selected.id;
      mode=next;refresh().catch(()=>{});
    }
  }
  renderHud();
}
function shrineDirection(){return mode==='pull'?'import':'export';}
function shrineGuardian(){
  if(shrineNearby?.kind==='guardian'||shrineNearby?.kind==='court')return shrineNearby.id||'yi';
  if(shrineNearby?.kind==='wish')return shrineNearby.id==='left'?'poseidon':'njord';
  return 'yi';
}
function personalCall(){
  const call=state.selected;
  return call?.id===invocationCallId && call.terminal===state.terminal && (!terminal||call.terminal===terminal)?call:null;
}
function invocationCall(){
  const call=personalCall();
  return call&&!call.atd&&call.berthPhase!=='departed'?call:null;
}
function shrineCallLabel(call){
  if(call.inboundVoy && call.outboundVoy && call.inboundVoy!==call.outboundVoy){
    return call.name+' · 입항 '+call.inboundVoy+' / 출항 '+call.outboundVoy;
  }
  return callName(call,mode);
}
function renderHud(){
  const item=places[nearby], kind=dockMode(nearby), call=kind?dockCall(kind):state.selected;
  let title='내 모선 찾기',schedule='모선 관제소',detail='';
  if(kind){
    title='내 모선 선택';
    schedule='부산항 공동 부두';
    detail='내 모선을 선택하고 모두와 같은 배를 응원해요.';
  }
  if(call){
    title=callName(call,kind);
    schedule=(call.isDemo?'예시 일정':'운항 일정')+' · '+timing(call,kind);
    if(kind==='pull')detail='다른 모선을 기다리는 사람들과 함께 당겨요.';
    if(kind==='push')detail='선체를 누르거나 가까이 걸어가세요.';
  }
  if(scheduleFailed){schedule='일정 연결 대기';detail='터미널 일정에 연결할 수 없어요.';}
  else if(!hasSession||(terminal&&terminal!==state.terminal)){schedule='일정 확인 중';detail='터미널 일정을 확인하고 있어요.';}
  else if(kind&&state.source?.stale){schedule='최신 일정 확인 대기';detail='터미널의 최신 접안 상태를 기다리고 있어요.';}
  $('#hud-vessel').textContent=title;$('#hud-schedule').textContent=schedule;
  const busy=cheerInFlight||Boolean(activeCheer&&activeCheer.until>Date.now());
  const atShrine=Boolean(shrineNearby)||nearby==='shrine'||busy;
  const own=atShrine?invocationCall():null,direction=shrineDirection();
  $('#context-action').classList.toggle('off',(!kind&&!item?.[1]&&!atShrine)||dialogue.isOpen()||!!dialog.open);
  $('#interact').hidden=atShrine?false:!item?.[1]||(kind==='pull'&&!call);
  $('#interact').disabled=atShrine?busy:actionBusy||dockAnimating;
  const actionLabel=atShrine?(direction==='import'?'입항 삼창':'출항 삼창'):item?.[1]||'';
  const iconOnly=!atShrine&&(nearby==='smoking'||nearby==='eomuk');
  $('#interact').setAttribute('aria-label',actionLabel);
  $('#interact').dataset.iconOnly=String(iconOnly);
  $('#interact-label').textContent=actionLabel;
  $('#interact-label').hidden=iconOnly;
  $('#interact-key').hidden=iconOnly;
  $('#smoke-icon').hidden=!iconOnly||nearby!=='smoking';
  $('#eomuk-icon').hidden=!iconOnly||nearby!=='eomuk';
  $('#shrine-alternate').hidden=!atShrine;
  $('#shrine-alternate').disabled=busy;
  $('#shrine-alternate').textContent=direction==='import'?'출항 삼창':'입항 삼창';
  $('#shrine-shortcuts').hidden=!atShrine||busy;
  $('#loc-detail').textContent=atShrine?(cheerStage||(own?'내 모선 · '+shrineCallLabel(own):'삼창할 내 모선을 먼저 선택해 주세요.')):kind?detail:'';
  $('#quay-details').hidden=!kind||!call;
  const seat=$('#seat-toggle');
  if(seat){seat.hidden=!(seatState.seated||seatState.available)||!!dialog.open;seat.textContent=seatState.seated?'일어나기':'벤치에 앉기';seat.disabled=actionBusy||dockAnimating||busy;}
}
async function ensureWorld(){
 if(world)return world;if(worldReady)return worldReady;
 worldReady=(async()=>{
  const m=await import('./walk3d.js?v=24');
  world=m.mountWalk3D($('#world'),{
    profile:state.profile||{name:'나',style:'cardigan'},active:true,onHint:toast,
    onNearby:setNearby,
    getSelfId:()=>selfAvatarId,
    onSeatChange:next=>{seatState=next;presence?.wake();renderHud();},
    onDepartureAnnouncement:speakDeparture,
    onShrineNearby:target=>{shrineNearby=target;renderHud();},
    onShrineInteraction:(kind,id)=>{
      if(!playing||dialog.open)return;
      if(kind==='wish')openPanel('shrine',null,{focus:'wall',wallSide:id});
      else if(kind==='guardian'){if(dialogue.show('shrine',id))renderHud();}
    },
    onTerminalBoard:()=>openPanel('board'),
    onCheerEnd:()=>{activeCheer=null;presence?.wake();renderHud();},
    onOwnCheerBeat:speakCheer,
    onDockContact:performWorldAction,
    onDockGestureEnd:finishDockGesture,
    onZone:id=>{if(playing&&!dialog.open)encounter(id);}
  });
  if(!world?.available)throw Error('3D renderer unavailable');
  updateBase();for(const {message,mine} of openingMessages.splice(0))showChat(message,mine);return world;
 })().catch(e=>{worldReady=null;throw e;});return worldReady;
}
async function startWalking(){
 const retry=$('#retry-world');retry.hidden=true;
 try{
  await ensureWorld();playing=true;app.dataset.playing='true';loading.hidden=true;hud.hidden=false;
  world.setActive(!dialog.open);startPresence();renderHud();focusWorld();return true;
 }catch(error){
  console.error('Busan harbor startup',error);
  $('#loading-status').textContent='터미널을 불러오지 못했어요.';retry.hidden=false;return false;
 }
}
$('#retry-world').onclick=startWalking;
async function travel(id){
 if(!places[id])return;const token=++lookToken;dialogue.hide();if(dialog.open)close();
 if(!playing&&!(await startWalking()))return;if(token!==lookToken)return;
 world.setActive?.(true);
 try{const r=await world.goTo(id,false);if(token!==lookToken||!r?.arrived||!playing||dialog.open)return;
 setNearby(id);if(id==='control')encounter(id);else focusWorld();}
 catch(e){toast('산책로를 다시 선택해 주세요.');}
}
function encounter(id=nearby){
 if(!playing||dialog.open)return;
 if(id==='shark'){
   world?.watchShark?.();focusWorld();return;
 }
 if(id==='eomuk'){
   if(world?.eatFishcake?.())presence?.wake();
   focusWorld();return;
 }
 if(id==='departureOffice'){
   if(!world?.replayDeparture?.())toast('실제 출항이 확인된 모선의 공표를 기다리고 있어요.');
   focusWorld();return;
 }
 if(id==='smoking'){
   if(world?.smoke?.())presence?.wake();
   focusWorld();return;
 }
 if(id==='shrine')return;
 if(!id||id==='yard')return;
 if(dockMode(id))return;
 if(dialogue.show(id))renderHud();
}
function open(id){if(['arrival','departure'].includes(id))travel(id);else openPanel(id);}
$('#guide-map').onclick=()=>openPanel('map');

 if(shrineNearby||nearby==='shrine'){cheerGuardian(shrineGuardian());return;}
 if(world?.interactNearShrine?.())return;
 if(nearby==='arrival'){const call=dockCall('pull');if(call)performWorldAction('pull',call.id);return;}
 encounter();
}

function syncSound(){const b=$('#sound');b.innerHTML=ico(sound?'sound':'mute');b.setAttribute('aria-label',sound?'효과음 끄기':'효과음 켜기');b.title=b.getAttribute('aria-label');}
function playSound(id='step'){
  const hz=({pull:220,celebrate:523,wish:440,coffee:347,step:345})[id];
  if(!sound||!hz)return;try{
  audio||=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();audio.resume().catch(()=>{});
  const osc=audio.createOscillator(),g=audio.createGain(),t=audio.currentTime;osc.type='sine';
  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(hz,t);osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(hz*1.35,t+.14);
  g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.10,t+.012);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+.21);
  osc.connect(g);g.connect(audio.destination);osc.start(t);osc.stop(t+.22);
}catch{}}
function speakCheer(call,beat,direction){
  const speech=window.speechSynthesis;
  if(!sound || !speech || !window.SpeechSynthesisUtterance)return;
  const voice=speech.getVoices().find(v=>v.localService && /^ko([_-]|$)/i.test(v.lang));
  if(!voice)return;
  if(beat===0)speech.cancel();
  const words=new window.SpeechSynthesisUtterance(beat===0?[call.name,call.voy].filter(Boolean).join(' '):direction==='import'?'입항!':'출항!');
  words.voice=voice;words.lang=voice.lang;words.rate=1.12;words.volume=.75;speech.speak(words);
}
function speakDeparture(call){
  const speech=window.speechSynthesis;
  if(!sound||!speech||!window.SpeechSynthesisUtterance)return;
  const voice=speech.getVoices().find(v=>v.localService&&/^ko([_-]|$)/i.test(v.lang));
  if(!voice)return;
  const words=new window.SpeechSynthesisUtterance(callName(call,'push')+'. 출항 확인! 수고하셨습니다.');
  words.voice=voice;words.lang=voice.lang;words.rate=.98;words.volume=.8;speech.speak(words);
}
function toggleSound(){sound=!sound;if(!sound)window.speechSynthesis?.cancel();try{localStorage.setItem('atd.sound',sound?'on':'off');}catch{}syncSound();playSound('step');toast(sound?'효과음을 켰습니다.':'효과음을 껐습니다.');}
syncSound();$('#sound').onclick=toggleSound;
function toast(msg){
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);const out=$('#toast');out.textContent=msg;out.classList.toggle('on',!dialog.open);
  dialog.querySelector('.toast-panel')?.remove();
  if(dialog.open){const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='toast-panel';e.setAttribute('role','status');e.textContent=msg;dialog.append(e);}
  toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>{out.classList.remove('on');dialog.querySelector('.toast-panel')?.remove();},2800);
}
async function api(path, payload){
  const headers=sessionHeaders();
  if(payload!==undefined)headers['Content-Type']='application/json';
  const res=await fetch('/api/'+path,{
    method:payload===undefined?'GET':'POST',credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',headers,
    body:payload===undefined?undefined:JSON.stringify(payload)
  });
  const result=await res.json().catch(()=>({error:'항구 서버에 연결할 수 없습니다.'}));
  if(!res.ok||result.error)throw new Error(result.error||'잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.');return result;
}
let boardRequest=0, firstBoard=null;
function getBoardState(){
  return firstBoard?{...state,terminal:firstBoard.terminal,terminalStatus:firstBoard.data}:state;
}
function primeBoard(id){
  const token=++boardRequest;
  firstBoard={terminal:id,data:{calls:[],source:{kind:'unavailable'}}};
  world?.setBoard?.(getBoardState());
  api('terminal-board?terminal='+encodeURIComponent(id)).then(data=>{
    if(token!==boardRequest || !Array.isArray(data.calls) || !data.source)return;
    firstBoard={terminal:id,data};world?.setBoard?.(getBoardState());
    if(active==='board')cleanup?.refresh?.();
  }).catch(()=>{});
}
function terminalName(id){return state.terminals.find(t=>t.id===id)?.name||id?.toUpperCase()||'—';}
function refresh(){const seq=++serial,profileAtRequest=state.profile;const params=new URLSearchParams({mode});if(terminal)params.set('terminal',terminal);if(selectedId)params.set('call',selectedId);
  const req=api('state?'+params).then(res=>{if(seq!==serial)return state;
    const old=state.selected;
    if(state.profile && state.profile!==profileAtRequest)res.profile=state.profile;
    state=res;hasSession=true;++boardRequest;firstBoard=null;scheduleFailed=false;
    if(previousTerminal!==state.terminal){previousTerminal=state.terminal;setChatTerminal(state.terminal);recentGesture=null;presence?.wake();}
    updateBase();cleanup?.refresh?.();
    if(old&&state.selected?.id===old.id&&!old.atd&&state.selected.atdVerified&&state.selected.atd&&!state.selected.isDemo&&state.room.selfJoined){
      if(playing)celebrateConfirmed(state.selected);
    }return state;
    }).catch(err=>{if(seq===serial){scheduleFailed=true;updateBase();}throw err;});
  pendingRefresh=req;req.then(()=>{if(pendingRefresh===req)pendingRefresh=null;},()=>{if(pendingRefresh===req)pendingRefresh=null;});return req;
}
function updateBase(){
 world?.setBoard?.(getBoardState());
 if(Array.isArray(state.confirmedDepartures))world?.setDepartures?.(state.confirmedDepartures,state.terminal);
 world?.setWishes?.(state.selected,state.wishes);
 const p=state.profile, portrait=crewPortrait(p?.style,true);
 const image=$('.my-id img');if(!image.src.endsWith(portrait))image.src=portrait;
 const identity=crewIdentity(p?.style),label=identity.department+' '+identity.name+' · 나의 사원증';
 image.alt=identity.name;$('.my-id').title=label;$('.my-id').setAttribute('aria-label',label);
 world?.setProfile?.(p||{name:'나',style:'cardigan'});world?.setWeather?.(state.weather?.code||null);
 const departure=dockCall('push'),arrival=dockCall('pull');
 world?.bindVessels?.({departure,arrival,personal:personalCall()});
 renderHud();if(active==='log')updateDockLog();
}
function celebrateConfirmed(s){
 if(!s?.atdVerified||!s.atd||s.isDemo||lastVictory===s.id)return;lastVictory=s.id;
 world?.celebrate?.(s);toast('모선이 무사히 출항했습니다.');playSound('celebrate');
}
async function selectCall(id,term){
 selectedId=id;terminal=term||'';updateBase();await refresh();
 const selected=state.selected;
 if(selected?.id!==id){toast('이 항차를 찾을 수 없어요. 모선을 다시 선택해 주세요.');return state;}
 if(pendingInvocation && (selected.atd||selected.berthPhase==='departed')){
   toast('이미 출항한 모선이에요. 삼창할 다른 항차를 선택해 주세요.');return state;
 }
 invocationCallId=id;try{localStorage.setItem('atd.own-call',id);}catch{}
 const u=new URL(location.href);u.searchParams.set('call',id);history.replaceState(null,'',u);updateBase();
 if(pendingInvocation&&dialog.open&&active==='control'){
   const intent=pendingInvocation;pendingInvocation=null;close();
   await cheerGuardian(intent.guardianId,intent.direction);
 }
 return state;
}
async function setTerminal(id){primeBoard(id);selectedId='';invocationCallId='';try{localStorage.removeItem('atd.own-call');}catch{}terminal=id;updateBase();const u=new URL(location.href);u.searchParams.delete('call');history.replaceState(null,'',u);return refresh();}
async function participationProfile(){
  if(state.profile)return state.profile;
  if(!guestRequest){
    // Only an explicit participation action creates a guest card. The server
    // uses the existing browser identity and preserves a concurrently saved name.
    guestRequest=api('guest',{}).then(({profile})=>{
      if(!profile)throw new Error('참여 정보를 불러오지 못했어요.');
      if(!state.profile){state.profile=profile;updateBase();}
      return state.profile;
    }).finally(()=>{guestRequest=null;});
  }
  return guestRequest;
}
async function refreshAfterWrite(){
  // A confirmed write stays successful if the read-back is temporarily offline.
  try{await refresh();}catch{}
}
async function join(){
  const call=state.selected;
  if(!call){toast('모선을 먼저 찾아주세요.');return false;}
  if(call.atd)return false;
  if(state.room.selfJoined)return true;
  await participationProfile();
  if(state.selected?.id!==call.id)return false;
  await api('join',{callId:call.id});await refreshAfterWrite();return state.selected?.id===call.id;
}
async function favorite(id){
  const profile=await participationProfile();
  const value=!profile.favorites?.includes(id);
  await api('favorite',{callId:id,value});await refreshAfterWrite();
  toast(value?'즐겨찾기에 담았습니다.':'즐겨찾기에서 뺐습니다.');return true;
}
async function postWish(note,guardian,side='left',kind='wish'){
  const id=state.selected?.id;if(!id){toast('기원을 남길 모선을 먼저 골라주세요.');return false;}
  if(state.selected.atd)return false;
  try{
    const requestId=crypto.randomUUID();
    await participationProfile();
    await api('wish',{callId:id,text:note,guardian,side,kind,requestId});
    await refreshAfterWrite();return true;
  }catch(err){toast(err.message);return false;}
}
let cheerInFlight=false, activeCheer=null,cheerStage='';
async function cheerGuardian(guardianId=shrineGuardian(),direction=shrineDirection()){
  if(cheerInFlight || activeCheer?.until>Date.now())return false;
  const selected=invocationCall();
  if(!selected){
    pendingInvocation={guardianId,direction};openPanel('control');
    toast(state.selected?.atd||state.selected?.berthPhase==='departed'?'이미 출항한 모선이에요. 삼창할 항차를 골라 주세요.':'삼창할 모선명과 항차를 골라 주세요.');
    return false;
  }
  const scope=++lookToken;
  const canBegin=()=>{
    if(scope!==lookToken||dialog.open)return false;
    if(invocationCall()?.id!==selected.id){toast('내 모선의 상태가 바뀌었어요. 삼창할 항차를 다시 선택해 주세요.');return false;}
    if(!world.canCheerHere()){toast('수호자 앞에서 삼창을 다시 눌러 주세요.');return false;}
    return true;
  };
  cheerInFlight=true;cheerStage='수호자 앞으로 가는 중…';renderHud();
  try{
    dialogue.hide();close();
    if(!playing && !(await startWalking()))return false;
    world.setActive(true);
    if(!await world.approachGuardian(guardianId)){
      if(scope===lookToken&&!dialog.open)toast('수호자 앞으로 가는 길이 끊겼어요. 삼창을 다시 눌러 주세요.');
      return false;
    }
    if(!canBegin())return false;
    cheerStage='모선의 출항 상태를 확인하고 있어요.';renderHud();
    await participationProfile();
    if(!canBegin())return false;
    const requestId=crypto.randomUUID();
    await api('action',{mode:'cheer',callId:selected.id,requestId,direction});
    if(scope!==lookToken||dialog.open||invocationCall()?.id!==selected.id||!world.canCheerHere()){
      if(scope===lookToken&&!dialog.open)toast('기원은 기록됐어요. 수호자 앞에서 다시 삼창할 수 있어요.');
      return false;
    }
    const ritualCall={...selected,voy:(direction==='import'?selected.inboundVoy:selected.outboundVoy)||selected.voy||selected.carrierVoy||selected.terminalVoy};
    if(!await world.cheer(ritualCall,{requestId,guardianId,direction})){
      toast('기원은 기록됐어요. 수호자 앞에서 삼창을 다시 눌러 주세요.');return false;
    }
    activeCheer={id:requestId,callId:selected.id,until:Date.now()+3900};
    presence?.wake();focusWorld();return true;
  }catch(err){toast(err.message||'삼창을 시작하지 못했어요. 다시 눌러 주세요.');return false;}
  finally{cheerInFlight=false;cheerStage='';renderHud();}
}

  if(!await world.goToWish(side)||lookToken!==scope||dialog.open)return false;
  openPanel('shrine',null,{focus:'wall',wallSide:side});return true;
}

async function shareCall(){

 try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(copy);toast('응원 기록과 모선 링크를 복사했습니다.');}catch{

}
function presenceCallId(){
 if(activeCheer?.until>Date.now())return activeCheer.callId;
 if(recentGesture?.terminal===state.terminal && recentGesture.until>Date.now())return recentGesture.callId;
 const kind=dockMode(nearby);
 return kind?dockCall(kind)?.id||null:state.selected?.id||null;
}
function startPresence(){
 if(presence)return;
 presence=connectPresence({
  getRoom:()=>hasSession?{terminal:state.terminal,callId:presenceCallId()}:null,
  getPose:()=>{
    const pose=world?.getPresence?.();if(!pose)return null;
    if(['smoke','sit','sit-smoke','eat'].includes(pose.motion))return pose;
    if(activeCheer && activeCheer.callId===state.selected?.id && activeCheer.until>Date.now() && pose.motion==='cheer')
      return {...pose,motion:'cheer',actionId:activeCheer.id};
    if(recentGesture?.callId===presenceCallId()&&recentGesture?.until>Date.now()&&pose.motion!=='walk'){
      return {...pose,motion:recentGesture.motion,actionId:recentGesture.actionId};
    }
    return {...pose,motion:['push','pull'].includes(pose.motion)?'idle':pose.motion,actionId:null};
  },
  onPeers:(rows,serverTime)=>world?.setLivePeers?.(rows,serverTime),
  onStatus:status=>{selfAvatarId=status.selfId;}
 });
}
function showMap(){
 const map=Object.entries(places).map(([id,[name,,icon]])=>{
   const point=world?.getZonePosition?.(id);
   if(!point)return '';
   // The chart runs along the waterfront. Its east axis points down the page.
   const left=(288-3.82*point.z)/766*100,top=(156+2.66*point.x)/320*100;
   return '<button class="atlas-pin" style="left:'+left+'%;top:'+top+'%" aria-label="'+name+'까지 걷기" data-travel="'+id+'">'+ico(icon)+'</button>';
 }).join('');
 const lines=Object.entries(places).map(([id,[name,,icon]])=>'<button data-travel="'+id+'">'+ico(icon)+name+'<small>걸어가기</small></button>').join('');
 body.innerHTML='<div class="atlas"><div class="atlas-image">'+map+'</div><div class="atlas-list">'+lines+'</div><div class="row-actions"><button class="secondary" data-menu="control">모선 검색</button><button class="secondary" data-menu="board">선적 현황</button></div></div>';
 body.querySelectorAll('[data-travel]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>travel(b.dataset.travel));
 body.querySelectorAll('[data-menu]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>openPanel(b.dataset.menu));
}

async function updateDockLog(){

 target.pending=true;

   if(data.selected?.id!==target.id||data.terminal!==target.terminal)throw Error('이 항차의 기록을 찾을 수 없어요.');
   target.data=data;target.error='';

 finally{target.pending=false;if(active==='log'&&dockLog===target)logPanel();}
}

}

/* ===== FILE: /conversations.js ===== */
import {ico} from './icons.js';
import {guardianHistory} from './guardian-history.js?v=19';

const portrait=file=>'/assets/'+file+'.webp';
const guardians=Object.fromEntries([
  ['yi','이순신 장군'],['jang','해상왕 장보고'],['poseidon','포세이돈'],['njord','뇨르드']
].map(([id,name])=>[id,{name,art:'guardian-'+id,...guardianHistory[id]}]));
const encounters={
  shrine:{place:'해상 수호 신전'},
  control:{place:'항만 관제소',label:'선박 일정 보기',name:'강 주임',role:'관제소 안내',art:'crew-vest-face',line:'배 이름이랑 항차만 불러 주세요. 어느 터미널에 뜨는지 같이 찾아볼까요?'}
};

function drawStory(deck,entries){
  if(!deck.remaining.length){
    const next=entries.slice();
    for(let i=next.length-1;i>0;i--){
      const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));
      [next[i],next[j]]=[next[j],next[i]];
    }
    // The next story is taken from the end of the shuffled array.
    const end=next.length-1;
    if(end>0&&next[end]===deck.last){
      const other=Math.floor(Math.random()*end);
      [next[other],next[end]]=[next[end],next[other]];
    }
    deck.remaining=next;
  }
  deck.last=deck.remaining.pop();
  return deck.last;
}

export function mountConversations(container,{onOpen,onClose}={}) {
  const root=document.createElement('section');
  root.className='atd-conversation';root.hidden=true;
  root.setAttribute('aria-label','항구 사람들과 대화');
  root.innerHTML=`
    <div class="atd-talk-card">
      <div class="atd-talk-portrait"><img alt=""></div>
      <div class="atd-talk-content">
        <header class="atd-talk-header"><h3></h3><span class="atd-talk-role"></span><small class="atd-talk-place"></small></header>
        <div aria-live="polite" aria-atomic="true">
          <p class="atd-talk-kind" hidden></p>
          <p class="atd-talk-line"></p>
          <p class="atd-talk-context" hidden></p>
          <a class="atd-talk-source" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" hidden></a>
          <div class="atd-talk-encouragement" hidden>
            <span>오늘의 응원 · 게임 창작</span>
            <p class="atd-talk-message"></p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="atd-talk-actions">
          <button type="button" class="atd-talk-enter"><kbd>E</kbd><span></span>${ico('next')}</button>
          <button type="button" class="atd-talk-next" hidden>다른 이야기</button>
          <button type="button" class="atd-talk-dismiss">계속 걷기</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  container.append(root);
  const find=key=>root.querySelector('.atd-talk-'+key);
  const name=root.querySelector('h3'),img=root.querySelector('img');
  const entrance=find('enter'),next=find('next'),line=find('line'),kind=find('kind'),context=find('context');
  const source=find('source'),encouragement=find('encouragement'),message=find('message');
  const decks=new Map();
  const hud=container.closest('#walk-hud');
  const chat=hud?.querySelector('.harbor-chat'),actions=hud?.querySelector('.context-action');
  let zone='',speaker='',destroyed=false;

  function placeCard(){
    if(!hud||root.hidden)return;
    const bottom=hud.getBoundingClientRect().bottom;
    const occupied=[chat,zone==='shrine'?actions:null].filter(Boolean)
      .map(el=>el.getBoundingClientRect()).filter(rect=>rect.height>0);
    if(occupied.length)root.style.setProperty('--talk-bottom',Math.ceil(bottom-Math.min(...occupied.map(rect=>rect.top))+8)+'px');
    else root.style.removeProperty('--talk-bottom');
  }
  const observer=hud&&typeof ResizeObserver!=='undefined'?new ResizeObserver(placeCard):null;
  for(const el of [hud,chat,actions])if(el)observer?.observe(el);

  function write(){
    const shrine=zone==='shrine',scene=encounters[zone],person=shrine?guardians[speaker]:scene;
    root.dataset.zone=zone;root.dataset.passive=String(shrine);
    img.src=portrait(person.art);name.textContent=person.name;
    find('role').textContent=person.role;find('place').textContent=scene.place;
    let record=null;
    if(shrine){
      if(!decks.has(speaker))decks.set(speaker,{remaining:[],last:null});
      record=drawStory(decks.get(speaker),person.entries);
    }
    line.textContent=shrine?(record.spoken?'“'+record.line+'”':record.line):person.line;
    kind.hidden=!shrine;kind.textContent=record?.kind||'';
    context.hidden=!shrine;context.textContent=record?.context||'';
    source.hidden=!shrine;
    if(shrine){source.href=record.url;source.textContent='출처 · '+record.source+' ↗';}
    else{source.removeAttribute('href');source.textContent='';}
    encouragement.hidden=!shrine;message.textContent=record?.message||'';
    entrance.hidden=shrine;next.hidden=!shrine||person.entries.length<2;
    if(!shrine)entrance.querySelector('span').textContent=scene.label;
    placeCard();
  }
  function show(id,guardianId){
    if(destroyed||!Object.hasOwn(encounters,id)){hide();return false;}
    if(id==='control'&&zone===id)return true;
    if(id==='shrine')speaker=Object.hasOwn(guardians,guardianId)?guardianId:(speaker||'yi');
    zone=id;root.hidden=false;write();return true;
  }
  function hide(){root.hidden=true;zone='';}
  function dismiss(){
    if(!zone)return;
    hide();onClose?.();
  }
  function enter(){
    if(zone!=='control')return;
    const chosen=zone;hide();onOpen?.(chosen);
  }
  function onKey(event){
    if(!zone||event.isComposing||event.defaultPrevented||event.altKey||event.ctrlKey||event.metaKey)return;
    if(event.target?.closest?.('input,textarea,select,[contenteditable="true"],dialog[open]'))return;
    if(event.code==='Escape'){
      event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();dismiss();
    }else if(event.code==='KeyE'&&zone==='control'){
      event.preventDefault();event.stopPropagation();if(!event.repeat)enter();
    }else if(['KeyW','KeyA','KeyS','KeyD','ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight'].includes(event.code)){
      dismiss(); // The same key remains available to the walk controls.
    }
  }
  function onWalkStick(event){
    if(zone&&event.target?.closest?.('[aria-label="캐릭터 이동 조이스틱"]'))dismiss();
  }
  entrance.addEventListener('click',enter);
  next.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(zone==='shrine')write();});
  find('dismiss').addEventListener('click',dismiss);
  window.addEventListener('keydown',onKey,true);window.addEventListener('pointerdown',onWalkStick,true);
  return {show,hide,isOpen:()=>Boolean(zone)&&zone!=='shrine',destroy(){
    if(destroyed)return;
    destroyed=true;hide();observer?.disconnect();window.removeEventListener('keydown',onKey,true);window.removeEventListener('pointerdown',onWalkStick,true);root.remove();
  }};
}


/* ===== FILE: /harbor-chat.js ===== */
export function mountHarborChat(host,{api,onMessage,onClose}){
  const panel=host.querySelector('#harbor-chat'),form=panel.querySelector('form');
  const input=form.querySelector('input'),send=form.querySelector('button'),status=panel.querySelector('[role=status]');
  // When an older shell meets this module, keep the existing draft and input.
  host.querySelector('button[aria-controls="harbor-chat"]')?.remove();
  for(const obsolete of panel.querySelectorAll('header, ol, [role="log"]'))obsolete.remove();
  panel.hidden=false;
  send.disabled=true;
  input.placeholder='부산항에 연결 중…';
  const app=host.closest('#harbor-app');
  if(app && typeof ResizeObserver!=='undefined'){
    new ResizeObserver(()=>{
      const height=panel.getBoundingClientRect().height;
      if(height>0)app.style.setProperty('--chat-footprint',(Math.ceil(height)+10)+'px');
    }).observe(panel);
  }

  const drafts=new Map(),spoken=new Set();
  let room=null,generation=0,cursor=0,enteredAt=0,firstRead=true,readFailed=false,pollTimer,errorTimer;
  function notice(text=''){
    clearTimeout(errorTimer);status.textContent=text;
    if(text)errorTimer=setTimeout(()=>{status.textContent='';},4500);
  }
  function speak(message,mine){
    if(message.terminal!==room?.terminal || spoken.has(message.id))return;
    spoken.add(message.id);
    if(spoken.size>128)spoken.delete(spoken.values().next().value);
    onMessage(message,mine);
  }
  async function poll(version){
    if(version!==generation||!room)return;
    try{
      if(!document.hidden){
        const data=await api('chat?'+new URLSearchParams({terminal:room.terminal,after:String(cursor)}));
        if(version!==generation)return;
        // A GET can see our write while its POST reply is still in flight.
        // Deliver the confirmed local bubble first and skip its matching ID here.
        if(room.posting)await room.posting;
        if(version!==generation)return;
        for(const m of data.messages){
          if(m.terminal!==room.terminal||m.id<=cursor)continue;
          if((!firstRead||m.sentAt>enteredAt) && Date.now()-m.sentAt<5000)speak(m,false);
          cursor=m.id;
        }
        firstRead=false;readFailed=false;
      }
    }catch{
      if(version===generation && !readFailed){
        readFailed=true;
        if(!room.posting)notice('채팅 연결을 다시 확인하고 있어요.');
      }
    }finally{
      if(version===generation)pollTimer=setTimeout(()=>poll(version),document.hidden?5000:1550);
    }
  }

  function fitKeyboard(){
    const viewport=window.visualViewport;
    const gap=viewport&&document.activeElement===input ? Math.max(0,host.getBoundingClientRect().bottom-viewport.offsetTop-viewport.height) : 0;
    panel.style.setProperty('--chat-keyboard-gap',Math.ceil(gap)+'px');
  }
  input.addEventListener('focus',fitKeyboard);
  input.addEventListener('blur',()=>panel.style.removeProperty('--chat-keyboard-gap'));
  window.visualViewport?.addEventListener('resize',fitKeyboard);
  window.visualViewport?.addEventListener('scroll',fitKeyboard);
  function close(){
    if(form.contains(document.activeElement)){
      document.activeElement.blur();
      onClose?.();
    }
  }
  let composing=false,commitKey=false;
  input.addEventListener('compositionstart',()=>{composing=true;});
  input.addEventListener('compositionend',()=>{composing=false;});
  form.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
    event.stopPropagation();
    if(composing||event.isComposing||event.keyCode===229){
      if(event.key==='Enter'){commitKey=true;setTimeout(()=>{commitKey=false;},0);}
      return;
    }
    if(event.key==='Escape'){event.preventDefault();close();}
  });
  // Key releases still reach the walk controls when focus changes mid-stride.
  form.onsubmit=async event=>{
    event.preventDefault();
    const channel=room,draft=input.value.trim();
    if(composing||commitKey||channel?.posting||!draft)return;
    if(!channel){notice('항구에 연결된 뒤 다시 보내 주세요.');return;}
    if(channel.pending?.text!==draft)channel.pending={terminal:channel.terminal,text:draft,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()};
    if(document.activeElement===send)input.focus({preventScroll:true});
    send.disabled=true;notice();
    channel.posting=api('chat',channel.pending).then(({message})=>{
      if(channel===room){
        speak(message,true);
        if(input.value.trim()===draft)input.value='';
      }
      if(channel.draft.trim()===draft)channel.draft='';
      channel.pending=null;
    }).catch(error=>{
      if(room===channel)notice(error.message);
    }).finally(()=>{
      channel.posting=null;
      if(room===channel)send.disabled=false;
    });
    await channel.posting;
  };
  return {close,setTerminal(next){
    if(!next||room?.terminal===next)return;
    if(room)room.draft=input.value;
    if(!drafts.has(next))drafts.set(next,{terminal:next,draft:room?'':input.value,pending:null,posting:null});
    room=drafts.get(next);generation++;clearTimeout(pollTimer);
    cursor=0;enteredAt=Date.now();firstRead=true;readFailed=false;spoken.clear();composing=false;commitKey=false;notice();
    input.value=room.draft;input.placeholder='메시지 입력…';
    form.setAttribute('aria-label','부산항 모두에게 메시지 보내기');
    send.disabled=Boolean(room.posting);
    poll(generation);
  }};
}


/* ===== FILE: /browser-session.js ===== */
// Origin-scoped private credential. Presence stream IDs and public avatar IDs
// are separate values and must never be used to authenticate a browser.
const storageKey = 'atd.browser-session.v1';
const validKey = value => typeof value === 'string' && /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(value);
let documentKey;

function currentKey() {
  if (!documentKey) {
    documentKey = Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)),
      byte => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  }
  return documentKey;
}

export function sessionHeaders() {
  try {
    const storage = window.localStorage;
    let saved = storage.getItem(storageKey);
    if (!validKey(saved)) {
      const candidate = currentKey();
      // Another tab can win the first write. Read before and after writing,
      // then consult the shared value again before every request.
      saved = storage.getItem(storageKey);
      if (!validKey(saved)) storage.setItem(storageKey, candidate);
      saved = storage.getItem(storageKey);
    }
    if (validKey(saved)) documentKey = saved;
  } catch {
    // Some embedded contexts deny even access to the localStorage getter.
  }
  return {'X-ATD-Browser': currentKey()};
}


/* ===== FILE: /presence-client.js ===== */
import {sessionHeaders} from './browser-session.js';

// Everyone stays in the same Busan harbor. Personal schedule metadata still
// invalidates pending writes without removing the people already in view.
const MOVING_MS = 400;
const REST_MS = 900;
const TTL_MS = 10_000;
const REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 5_000;

function roomFrom(getRoom) {
  const room = getRoom();
  if (!room || typeof room.terminal !== 'string' || !room.terminal) return null;
  const callId = typeof room.callId === 'string' && room.callId ? room.callId : null;
  return {terminal:room.terminal,callId,key:JSON.stringify([room.terminal,callId])};
}

function validPose(value) {
  return value && Number.isFinite(value.x) && Number.isFinite(value.z) && Number.isFinite(value.yaw);
}

function isMoving(pose, previous) {
  if (!validPose(pose)) return false;
  return ['walk','walking','run','push','pull','cheer','smoke'].includes(pose.motion) ||
    (previous && (Math.abs(pose.x-previous.x) + Math.abs(pose.z-previous.z) > .025 || Math.abs(pose.yaw-previous.yaw) > .065));
}

export function connectPresence({getPose,getRoom,onPeers,onStatus}) {
  const instanceId = crypto.randomUUID?.() ?? Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)),
    byte=>byte.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
  let room = null, generation = 0, seq = 0, destroyed = false, suspended = false;
  let inFlight = null, timer = null, deadline = 0, expiryTimer = null;
  let lastRequestAt = -Infinity, lastPose = null, departureSent = false;
  let records = [], selfId = null, selfName = null, online = 0, connected = false;
  let serverOffset = 0, previousPeers = null, previousStatus = null;

  function clearPoll() {
    clearTimeout(timer);timer=null;deadline=0;
  }
  function report(alwaysSendPeers=false) {
    clearTimeout(expiryTimer);expiryTimer=null;
    const now=Date.now(), remaining=records.filter(entry=>entry.expires>now);
    if(remaining.length!==records.length){
      online=Math.max(0,online-(records.length-remaining.length));records=remaining;
    }
    const peers=records.map(entry=>entry.peer);
    const peerKey=JSON.stringify(peers);
    if(alwaysSendPeers||peerKey!==previousPeers) {
      previousPeers=peerKey;
      onPeers?.(peers,now+serverOffset);
    }
    const snapshot={online:connected?online:0,selfId,connected,selfName,peers};
    const statusKey=JSON.stringify([snapshot.online,selfId,connected,selfName,peerKey]);
    if(statusKey!==previousStatus) {
      previousStatus=statusKey;
      onStatus?.(snapshot);
    }
    if(records.length&&!destroyed&&!suspended&&!document.hidden){
      const next=Math.min(...records.map(entry=>entry.expires));
      expiryTimer=setTimeout(()=>report(),Math.max(1,next-now+1));
    }
  }
  function forget() {
    records=[];connected=false;online=0;selfId=null;selfName=null;serverOffset=0;
    report();
  }
  function syncRoom() {
    const next=roomFrom(getRoom);
    if(next?.key===room?.key)return;
    const sameHarbor=Boolean(room&&next);
    generation++;
    inFlight?.abort();
    room=next;lastPose=null;lastRequestAt=-Infinity;
    if(!sameHarbor)forget();
  }
  function accept(data) {
    if(!Array.isArray(data?.peers))throw new Error('Missing presence list');
    const now=Date.now();
    const serverTime=Number.isFinite(data.serverTime)?data.serverTime:now;
    serverOffset=serverTime-now;
    selfId=data.selfId??null;selfName=data.selfName??null;
    const seen=new Set();
    records=[];
    for(const peer of data.peers){
      if(!peer?.id || peer.id===selfId || seen.has(peer.id) || !validPose(peer))continue;
      seen.add(peer.id);
      const age=Number.isFinite(peer.updatedAt)?Math.max(0,serverTime-peer.updatedAt):0;
      if(age>=TTL_MS)continue;
      records.push({peer,expires:now+TTL_MS-age});
    }
    connected=true;
    online=Number.isSafeInteger(data.online)&&data.online>=0?data.online:records.length+Number(Boolean(selfId));
    report(true);
  }
  function queue(waitMs) {
    if(destroyed||suspended||document.hidden||inFlight)return;
    const delay=Math.max(0,waitMs), due=Date.now()+delay;
    if(timer!==null && deadline<=due)return;
    clearPoll();deadline=due;timer=setTimeout(poll,delay);
  }
  function nextDelay(pose) {
    const active=isMoving(pose,lastPose);
    return (active?MOVING_MS:REST_MS) - (Date.now()-lastRequestAt);
  }
  async function poll() {
    clearPoll();
    if(destroyed||suspended||document.hidden||inFlight)return;
    syncRoom();
    const pose=getPose();
    if(!room||!validPose(pose)){queue(REST_MS);return;}
    const scope=room, version=generation;
    const controller=new AbortController();
    inFlight=controller;
    const payload={
      terminal:scope.terminal,callId:scope.callId,instanceId,seq:++seq,
      x:pose.x,z:pose.z,yaw:pose.yaw,motion:pose.motion||'idle',actionId:pose.actionId||null,
    };
    lastRequestAt=Date.now();lastPose={x:pose.x,z:pose.z,yaw:pose.yaw,motion:pose.motion,actionId:pose.actionId};
    departureSent=false;
    const timeout=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS);
    try {
      const response=await fetch('/api/presence',{
        method:'POST',credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',
        headers:{'content-type':'application/json',...sessionHeaders()},body:JSON.stringify(payload),signal:controller.signal,
      });
      if(!response.ok)throw new Error(String(response.status));
      const data=await response.json();
      if(!destroyed&&!suspended&&!document.hidden&&version===generation&&scope.key===roomFrom(getRoom)?.key)accept(data);
    } catch {
      if(!destroyed&&!suspended&&!document.hidden&&version===generation){
        connected=false;report();
      }
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeout);inFlight=null;
      if(!destroyed&&!suspended&&!document.hidden) {
        syncRoom();
        // A slow/failed request should not immediately start another full timeout cycle.
        const wait=version===generation?(connected?nextDelay(getPose()):REST_MS):0;
        queue(Math.max(0,wait));
      }
    }
  }
  function wake() {
    if(destroyed||suspended||document.hidden)return;
    syncRoom();
    if(!inFlight) {
      const pose=getPose();
      const changedAction=pose?.actionId && pose.actionId!==lastPose?.actionId;
      queue(changedAction?0:nextDelay(pose));
    }
  }
  function stopPolling(){
    generation++;clearPoll();
    inFlight?.abort();
    records=[];online=0;connected=false;report();
  }
  function leave(){
    if(departureSent||!room||!lastPose)return;
    departureSent=true;
    const body=JSON.stringify({terminal:room.terminal,callId:room.callId,instanceId,seq:++seq,...lastPose,leaving:true});
    // Keep the private session header on departure, even when cookies are blocked.
    fetch('/api/presence',{method:'POST',credentials:'same-origin',
      headers:{'content-type':'application/json',...sessionHeaders()},body,keepalive:true}).catch(()=>{});
  }
  function onVisibility(){
    if(document.hidden){stopPolling();return;}
    lastRequestAt=-Infinity;wake();
  }
  function onPageHide(){suspended=true;stopPolling();leave();}
  function onPageShow(){
    if(!suspended)return;
    suspended=false;lastRequestAt=-Infinity;wake();
  }
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',onVisibility);
  window.addEventListener('pagehide',onPageHide);
  window.addEventListener('pageshow',onPageShow);
  wake();

  return {wake,dispose(){
    if(destroyed)return;
    destroyed=true;
    document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange',onVisibility);
    window.removeEventListener('pagehide',onPageHide);
    window.removeEventListener('pageshow',onPageShow);
    stopPolling();leave();
  }};
}


/* ===== FILE: /shrine.js ===== */
export const guardians = [
  { id: 'poseidon', name: '포세이돈', icon: 'wave', theme: '평온한 바다' },
  { id: 'yi', name: '이순신', title: '이순신 장군', icon: 'shield', theme: '안전한 항로' },
  { id: 'jang', name: '장보고', title: '해상왕 장보고', icon: 'ship', theme: '무역의 번영' },
  { id: 'njord', name: '뇨르드', icon: 'wind', theme: '항해의 순풍' },
];

const kindLabel = {wish: '기원문', bl: '게임 B/L'};
const kstTime = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ko-KR', {
  timeZone: 'Asia/Seoul', month: 'numeric', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false,
});

function node(tag, className, text) {
  const item = document.createElement(tag);
  if (className) item.className = className;
  if (text != null) item.textContent = text;
  return item;
}

export function renderShrine(container, ctx, {wallSide = 'left', initialGuardian} = {}) {
  const root = node('section', 'atd-shrine');
  root.innerHTML = `
    <header class="shrine-heading">
      <div class="shrine-wings" role="group" aria-label="컨테이너 선택">
        <button type="button" data-side="left">청록 컨테이너</button>
        <button type="button" data-side="right">회청 컨테이너</button>
      </div>
      <strong class="shrine-ship"></strong>
    </header>
    <div class="shrine-choose" hidden>
      <p>기원문을 남길 모선을 먼저 골라 주세요.</p>
      <button type="button" class="shrine-action">모선 찾기</button>
    </div>
    <form class="shrine-compose">
      <div class="shrine-forms" role="group" aria-label="게시물 양식">
        <button type="button" data-kind="wish">기원문</button>
        <button type="button" data-kind="bl">게임 B/L</button>
      </div>
      <div class="shrine-bl" hidden>
        <strong>게임 B/L</strong><small>출항 기념용</small>
        <p class="shrine-bl-vessel"></p><p class="shrine-bl-author"></p>
      </div>
      <label class="shrine-field"><span></span><textarea rows="2" maxlength="160"></textarea></label>
      <div class="shrine-submit"><span class="shrine-byline"></span><button type="submit" class="shrine-action"></button></div>
    </form>
    <section class="shrine-log">
      <h3>이 컨테이너에 붙은 기록</h3><p class="shrine-empty"></p><ul></ul>
    </section>`;
  container.replaceChildren(root);
  const find = selector => root.querySelector(selector);
  const field = find('textarea'), form = find('form'), submit = find('[type="submit"]');
  const fieldLabel = find('.shrine-field span'), noVessel = find('.shrine-choose');
  const log = find('.shrine-log'), list = find('ul'), empty = find('.shrine-empty');
  const sides = [...root.querySelectorAll('[data-side]')], kinds = [...root.querySelectorAll('[data-kind]')];
  const guardian = guardians.find(item => [item.id,item.name,item.title].some(v => v && v === initialGuardian))?.name || '';
  const drafts = new Map();
  let side = wallSide === 'right' ? 'right' : 'left', kind = 'wish';
  let draftKey = '', logKey = '', closed = false, busy = false;
  let lastBlankCard = null;

  function refresh() {
    if (closed) return;
    const {selected: call, profile, wishes} = ctx.getState() || {};
    const callId = call?.id || '';
    const vessel = callId ? [call.name,call.voy].filter(Boolean).join(' ') : '';
    const departed = Boolean(call?.atd || call?.berthPhase === 'departed');
    const nextKey = JSON.stringify([callId,kind]);
    if (nextKey !== draftKey) {
      draftKey = nextKey;
      field.value = drafts.get(draftKey) || '';
    }
    noVessel.hidden = Boolean(callId);
    form.hidden = !callId || departed;
    log.hidden = !callId;
    find('.shrine-ship').textContent = departed ? vessel+' · 출항 완료' : vessel;
    for (const button of sides) {
      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(button.dataset.side === side));
      button.disabled = busy;
    }
    for (const button of kinds) {
      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(button.dataset.kind === kind));
      button.disabled = busy;
    }
    const nickname = profile?.name || '';
    const isBL = kind === 'bl';
    find('.shrine-bl').hidden = !isBL;
    find('.shrine-bl-vessel').textContent = vessel;
    const blAuthor = find('.shrine-bl-author');
    blAuthor.hidden = !nickname;
    blAuthor.textContent = nickname;
    fieldLabel.textContent = isBL ? '기념 메모 (선택)' : '나의 기원문';
    field.placeholder = isBL ? '기억하고 싶은 한 줄을 남겨 주세요.' : '같은 배를 기다리는 사람들에게 한마디…';
    field.required = !isBL;
    field.disabled = busy || form.hidden;
    const byline = find('.shrine-byline');
    byline.textContent = isBL || !nickname ? '' : nickname;
    submit.textContent = busy ? '붙이는 중…' : kindLabel[kind]+' 붙이기';
    submit.disabled = field.disabled || (!isBL && !field.value.trim());

    const notes = callId && Array.isArray(wishes) ? wishes.filter(note => {
      if (!note) return false;
      const noteCall = note.callId ?? note.call_id;
      const noteSide = note.side ?? note.wallSide ?? note.wall_side ?? 'left';
      return (noteCall == null || noteCall === callId) && noteSide === side;
    }) : [];
    const nextLog = JSON.stringify([side,callId,vessel,notes]);
    if (nextLog === logKey) return;
    logKey = nextLog;
    empty.hidden = Boolean(notes.length);
    empty.textContent = '아직 붙은 기록이 없어요.';
    const items = document.createDocumentFragment();
    for (const note of notes) {
      const noteKind = (note.kind ?? note.noteKind ?? note.note_kind) === 'bl' ? 'bl' : 'wish';
      const item = node('li','shrine-note'), meta = node('div','shrine-meta');
      item.dataset.kind = noteKind;
      meta.append(node('span','shrine-tag',kindLabel[noteKind]));
      if (note.name) meta.append(node('b','',note.name));
      const at = note.createdAt ?? note.created_at;
      const stamp = new Date(at);
      if (at && Number.isFinite(stamp.getTime())) {
        const time = node('time','',kstTime.format(stamp));
        time.dateTime = stamp.toISOString();
        meta.append(time);
      }
      item.append(meta);
      if (noteKind === 'bl') item.append(node('small','shrine-note-vessel',vessel+' · 출항 기념용'));
      if (note.text) item.append(node('p','',note.text));
      items.append(item);
    }
    list.replaceChildren(items);
  }

  for (const button of sides) button.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (closed || busy) return;
    side = button.dataset.side;
    refresh();
  });
  for (const button of kinds) button.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (closed || busy) return;
    kind = button.dataset.kind;
    refresh();
  });
  noVessel.querySelector('button').addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (!closed && !ctx.getState()?.selected?.id) ctx.open('control');
  });
  field.addEventListener('input', () => {
    if (closed || field.disabled) return;
    drafts.set(draftKey,field.value);
    refresh();
  });
  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (closed || busy) return;
    // Recheck the same room at press time, even between state refreshes.
    const call = ctx.getState()?.selected;
    if (!call?.id || call.atd || call.berthPhase === 'departed' || draftKey !== JSON.stringify([call.id,kind])) {
      refresh();
      return;
    }
    const memo = field.value.trim(), blankCard = !memo && kind === 'bl';
    if (!memo && !blankCard) return;
    const cardKey = JSON.stringify([call.id,side]);
    if (blankCard && lastBlankCard?.key === cardKey && Date.now() - lastBlankCard.at < 650) return;
    const submittedKey = draftKey;
    busy = true;
    refresh();
    try {
      if (await ctx.postWish(memo || '기념 B/L',guardian,side,kind)) {
        if (closed) return;
        if (blankCard) lastBlankCard = {key:cardKey,at:Date.now()};
        drafts.delete(submittedKey);
        if (draftKey === submittedKey) field.value = '';
        ctx.playSound?.('wish');
      }
    } catch {
      if (!closed) ctx.toast?.('컨테이너에 붙이지 못했어요. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
    } finally {
      busy = false;
      refresh();
    }
  });
  refresh();
  const cleanup = () => {closed = true; drafts.clear();};
  cleanup.refresh = refresh;
  return cleanup;
}


/* ===== FILE: /terminal-panel.js ===== */
import {boardData,loadingState,quantity} from './terminal-marquee.js';
const phases={scheduled:'접안 예정',berthed:'접안 중',departed:'출항',unknown:'미확인'};
const make=(tag,value,cls)=>{
  const item=document.createElement(tag);
  if(value!=null)item.textContent=value;
  if(cls)item.className=cls;
  return item;
};
function link(label,url){
  if(typeof url!=='string'||!url.startsWith('https://'))return make('span',label);
  const a=make('a',label);Object.assign(a,{href:url,target:'_blank',rel:'noopener noreferrer'});return a;
}
export function renderTerminalPanel(host,ctx){
  const section=make('section',null,'terminal-detail'),top=make('div',null,'terminal-boardbar'),
    name=make('strong'),terminal=make('select'),meta=make('p',null,'terminal-meta'),
    list=make('div',null,'terminal-roster'),sourceLine=make('p',null,'terminal-meta');
  terminal.setAttribute('aria-label','선적 현황 터미널');
  for(const port of ctx.getState().terminals||[])terminal.add(new Option(port.name,port.id));
  terminal.onchange=async()=>{try{await ctx.setTerminal(terminal.value);paint();}catch(error){ctx.toast(error.message);paint();}};
  top.append(name,terminal);section.append(top,meta,list,sourceLine);host.replaceChildren(section);
  let signature='';
  function paint(){
    const state=ctx.getBoardState(),board=boardData(state),data=state.terminalStatus,source=data?.source||{};
    const key=JSON.stringify([state.terminal,source,data?.yardTrucks,board.rows.map(c=>[c.id,c.berth,c.berthPhase,c.etd,c.loading])]);
    if(signature===key)return;signature=key;
    if(!terminal.options.length)for(const port of state.terminals||[])terminal.add(new Option(port.name,port.id));
    name.textContent='선적 현황 · '+board.statusText;terminal.value=state.terminal;
    const stamp=source.checkedAt||source.updatedAt,trucks=data?.yardTrucks;
    meta.textContent=[stamp?'시간표 확인 '+ctx.fmt(stamp)+' KST':'공식 자료 연결 대기',
      Number.isFinite(trucks?.inbound)&&Number.isFinite(trucks?.outbound)?'야드 반입 '+trucks.inbound+'대 · 반출 '+trucks.outbound+'대':null].filter(Boolean).join('  ·  ');
    list.replaceChildren();
    if(board.rows.length){
      const table=make('table'),head=table.createTHead().insertRow(),body=table.createTBody();
      for(const title of ['모선 / 수출 항차','적하 계획','작업물량','적하 완료','잔량 (VAN)','잔여 작업 (MOVES)','선적 상태','부두 / 일정']){
        const th=make('th',title);th.scope='col';head.append(th);
      }
      for(const c of board.rows){
        const tr=body.insertRow(),v=tr.insertCell(),w=c.loading||{};
        v.append(make('b',c.name),make('small',c.voy||c.terminalVoy||'—'));
        tr.insertCell().textContent=quantity(w.plan);
        tr.insertCell().textContent=quantity(w.workTotal)+(w.workUnit?' '+w.workUnit:'');
        tr.insertCell().textContent=quantity(w.completed)+(w.completedUnit&&w.completed!=null?' '+w.completedUnit:'');
        tr.insertCell().textContent=quantity(w.remainingVan);
        tr.insertCell().textContent=quantity(w.remainingMoves);
        const stateCell=tr.insertCell();stateCell.append(make('b',loadingState(c)));
        if(w.workSourceUrl && w.workCheckedAt){
          const detail=make('small');detail.append(link('원문 · '+ctx.fmt(w.workCheckedAt)+' KST',w.workSourceUrl));stateCell.append(detail);
        }
        const berth=tr.insertCell();berth.append(make('span',[c.berth,phases[c.berthPhase]].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')),
          make('small','ETB '+ctx.fmt(c.etb)+' / ETD '+ctx.fmt(c.etd)));
      }
      list.append(table);
    }else{list.append(make('p',source.kind==='official'?'표시할 선적 자료가 없습니다.':'터미널 공식 선적 자료 수신을 기다립니다.'));}
    sourceLine.replaceChildren();
    if(board.rows.length)sourceLine.append(document.createTextNode('— 미수신 · VAN은 컨테이너 수, MOVES는 작업 횟수입니다. 단위 표기가 없는 수치는 원문 그대로입니다. '));
    if(source.url)sourceLine.append(link('선적 계획 원문',source.url));
    if(source.loadingUrl)sourceLine.append(document.createTextNode(' · '),link('본선작업현황 원문',source.loadingUrl));
  }
  paint();const dispose=()=>{};dispose.refresh=paint;return dispose;
}


/* ===== FILE: /control.js ===== */
import {quantity,loadingState} from './terminal-marquee.js';
const node = (tag, cls, text) => {
  const element = document.createElement(tag);
  if (cls) element.className = cls;
  if (text !== undefined) element.textContent = String(text);
  return element;
};
const button = (label, cls, click) => {
  const item = node('button', cls, label);
  item.type = 'button';
  if (click) item.addEventListener('click', click);
  return item;
};
function external(label, url) {
  let target; try { target = new URL(url); } catch { return node('span', '', label); }
  if (target.protocol !== 'https:') return node('span', '', label);
  const link = node('a', '', label);
  Object.assign(link, {href:target.href, target:'_blank', rel:'noopener noreferrer'});
  return link;
}
const terminalLabel = (id, state) => state.terminals?.find(t => t.id === id)?.name || String(id || '').toUpperCase();
const voyage = call => [call?.name, call?.voy].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
function kstDate(iso) {
  const stamp = Date.parse(iso);
  return Number.isFinite(stamp) ? new Date(stamp + 32400000).toISOString().slice(0,10) : '';
}
const slots = {eta:'입항 예정',etb:'접안 예정',atb:'실제 접안',etd:'출항 예정',atd:'실제 출항'};
const fail = (ctx, e) => ctx.toast?.(e?.message || '불러오지 못했어요. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
function metric(label, value, title) {
  const el=node('div');
  el.append(node('small','',label),node('b','',value));
  if(title) el.title=title;
  return el;
}
function sourceLine(state, ctx) {
  const el = node('p','sourceline'), source = state.source || {};
  let line = source.kind==='demo' ? '체험 시간표 · 표시한 일정은 예시입니다.' : (source.label || '운항 정보');
  if(source.updatedAt) line += ' · 원문 갱신 '+ctx.fmt(source.updatedAt)+' KST';
  if(source.checkedAt) line += ' · 확인 '+ctx.fmt(source.checkedAt)+' KST';
  if(source.stale) line += ' · 최근 수신 데이터';
  el.append(document.createTextNode(line));
  const official = state.officialSource || (source.url ? source : null);
  if(official?.url) el.append(document.createTextNode(' · '),external('터미널 공식 시간표',official.url));
  const ws = state.weather;
  el.append(node('br'),document.createTextNode(ws? ('부산신항 '+ws.label+' '+ws.temperature+'°C · '+ctx.fmt(ws.validAt)+' 예보 · '):'기상청 예보 미연결'));
  if(ws?.sourceUrl) el.append(external(ws.source || '기상청',ws.sourceUrl));
  return el;
}
function scheduleTable(cb, sort) {
  const table=node('table','schedules'), head=table.createTHead().insertRow();
  ['모선 / 항차',sort==='eta'?'ETA':'ETB','ETD'].forEach(label=>{const th=node('th','',label);th.scope='col';head.append(th);});
  const body=table.createTBody();
  return {table, add(call,selected,ctx) {
    const row=body.insertRow(); if(selected) row.className='selected';
    const cell=row.insertCell(), choose=button(call.name,'',()=>cb(call));
    choose.setAttribute('aria-label',voyage(call)+' 선택');
    cell.append(choose,node('small','',[call.voy,call.terminal.toUpperCase(),call.isDemo?'예시':''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')));
    for (const key of [sort==='eta'?'eta':'etb','etd']) row.insertCell().textContent=ctx.fmt(call[key]);
  }};
}
async function action(ctx, btn, fn) {
  if(btn.disabled) return;
  btn.disabled=true;
  try { return await fn(); }
  catch(e) { fail(ctx,e); }
  finally { if(btn.isConnected) btn.disabled=false; }
}

export function renderControl(container,ctx) {
  const root=node('section','panel-pad');
  const search=node('form','searchline'), input=node('input','searchbox');
  Object.assign(input,{type:'search',placeholder:'선박명 또는 선박명 + 항차',maxLength:120,autocomplete:'off'});
  input.setAttribute('aria-label','부산항 전체 터미널에서 모선 찾기');
  const submit=node('button','navy-btn','검색');submit.type='submit';search.append(input,submit);
  const filters=node('div','filterbar'), terminal=node('select'), sort=node('select');
  terminal.setAttribute('aria-label','터미널'); sort.setAttribute('aria-label','일정 기준');
  [['etd','출항 예정순 · ETD'],['etb','접안 예정순 · ETB'],['eta','입항 예정순 · ETA']].forEach(([id,title])=>sort.append(new Option(title,id)));
  const bookmarks=button('☆ 즐겨찾기','pick'), more=button('날짜 선택','pick');
  const left=node('div','pick-row'), right=node('div','pick-row');
  left.append(terminal,sort);right.append(bookmarks,more,button('선적 현황판','pick',()=>ctx.open('board')));filters.append(left,right);
  const dates=node('div','pick-row'), date=node('input','searchbox');
  date.type='date';date.setAttribute('aria-label','한국 시간 기준 일정 날짜');
  dates.hidden=true;dates.style.display='none';dates.append(date,button('날짜 해제','pick',()=>{date.value='';paint(true);}));
  const hint=node('p','sourceline');hint.style.margin='7px 0';hint.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
  const schedule=node('div','tablewrap'),details=node('div'),footer=node('div');
  const together=node('p','sourceline','선명과 항차는 내 화면에만 표시됩니다. 모선이 달라도 부산항 공동 부두에서 함께 응원해요.');
  root.append(together,search,filters,dates,hint,schedule,details,footer);container.replaceChildren(root);
  let closed=false, favoritesOnly=false, allResults=[], featured=new Map(), timer=0, request=0, loading=false, error='';
  let prevRows='',prevDetail='',prevSource='',prevTerminals='';
  const missing=new Set();
  function choose(call) {
    if(!call || closed) return;
    Promise.resolve(ctx.selectCall(call.id,call.terminal)).then(()=>paint(true)).catch(e=>!closed && fail(ctx,e));
  }
  function resolveFavorites(state) {
    const ids=state.profile?.favorites || [];
    for(const id of ids) {
      if(featured.has(id) || missing.has(id)) continue;
      missing.add(id);
      ctx.api('state?call='+encodeURIComponent(id)).then(list=>{
        if(closed) return;
        if(list.selected?.id===id) featured.set(id,list.selected);
        paint();
      }).catch(()=>{ if(!closed) missing.delete(id); });
    }
  }
  function showDetail(state) {
    const call=state.selected;
    if(!call) { details.replaceChildren();return; }
    const card=node('section','scheddetail');card.append(node('h3','',voyage(call)));
    const info=node('p','sourceline',[terminalLabel(call.terminal,state),call.berth?call.berth+'번 선석':null,call.atd?'출항 확인':call.status,call.isDemo?'예시 항차':null].filter(Boolean).join(' · '));
    info.style.marginTop='4px';card.append(info);
    const times=node('div','metrics');
    for(const key of ['eta','etb',...(call.atb?['atb']:[]),'etd','atd']) times.append(metric(key.toUpperCase(),ctx.fmt(call[key]),slots[key]));
    card.append(times);
    if(!call.isDemo){
      const w=call.loading||{}, header=node('h4','','선적 현황 · '+loadingState(call)),figures=node('div','metrics');
      for(const [title,value] of [['적하 계획',w.plan],['작업물량',w.workTotal],['완료'+(w.completedUnit?' ('+w.completedUnit+')':''),w.completed],['잔량 (VAN)',w.remainingVan],['잔여 작업 (MOVES)',w.remainingMoves]])figures.append(metric(title,quantity(value)));
      card.append(header,figures);
      if(w.workSourceUrl){
        const note=node('p','sourceline');note.append(external('본선작업현황 원문',w.workSourceUrl));
        if(w.workCheckedAt)note.append(document.createTextNode(' · 확인 '+ctx.fmt(w.workCheckedAt)+' KST'));
        card.append(note);
      }
    }

    if ((call.carrierVoy && call.carrierVoy!==call.voy) || call.terminalVoy) card.append(node('p','sourceline',[call.carrierVoy?'선사 항차 '+call.carrierVoy:'',call.terminalVoy?'터미널 항차 '+call.terminalVoy:''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')));
    const actions=node('div','row-actions');
    if (!call.atd && call.berthPhase!=='departed') {
      const enter=button('내 모선으로 선택하고 함께 밀기','coral');
      enter.addEventListener('click',()=>action(ctx,enter,async()=>{await ctx.selectCall(call.id,call.terminal);if(await ctx.join())ctx.open('departure');}));
      actions.append(enter);
    }
    const favored=state.profile?.favorites?.includes(call.id);
    const favorite=button(favored?'★ 즐겨찾기 해제':'☆ 즐겨찾기','secondary');favorite.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!!favored));
    favorite.addEventListener('click',()=>action(ctx,favorite,async()=>{await ctx.favorite(call.id);paint(true);}));
    actions.append(favorite);card.append(actions);
    if (state.changes?.length) {
      const history=node('details','sourceline'), heading=node('summary','','일정 변경 기록 '+state.changes.length+'건');
      history.append(heading);for (const rev of state.changes) {
        const changes=Object.keys(slots).filter(k=>(rev.previous?.[k] || '')!==(rev.updated?.[k] || ''));
        history.append(node('p','',ctx.fmt(rev.changedAt)+' · '+changes.map(k=>k.toUpperCase()+' '+ctx.fmt(rev.previous[k])+' → '+ctx.fmt(rev.updated[k])).join(' / ')));
      }card.append(history);
    }
    details.replaceChildren(card);
  }
  function paint(force=false) {
    if(closed) return;
    const state=ctx.getState() || {}, q=input.value.trim(), ids=state.profile?.favorites || [];
    const termsKey=JSON.stringify(state.terminals || []);
    if(termsKey!==prevTerminals || !terminal.options.length) {
      prevTerminals=termsKey;terminal.replaceChildren();
      for(const t of (state.terminals || [])) terminal.append(new Option(t.name,t.id));
      terminal.append(new Option('부산항 전체','all'));
    }
    terminal.value=q || favoritesOnly?'all':state.terminal || 'pnc';terminal.disabled=!!q || favoritesOnly;
    const allOption=[...terminal.options].find(opt=>opt.value==='all');if(allOption) allOption.hidden=!terminal.disabled;
    bookmarks.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(favoritesOnly));
    const known=[...(state.calls || []),...allResults,...(state.selected?[state.selected]:[])];
    for(const c of known) featured.set(c.id,c);
    if(favoritesOnly) resolveFavorites(state);
    const currentCalls=new Map((state.calls || []).map(c=>[c.id,c]));
    let calls = q ? allResults.map(c=>currentCalls.get(c.id) || c) : favoritesOnly ? ids.map(id=>currentCalls.get(id) || featured.get(id)).filter(Boolean) : state.calls || [];
    if(favoritesOnly&&q) calls=calls.filter(c=>ids.includes(c.id));
    if(date.value) calls=calls.filter(c=>kstDate(c[sort.value])===date.value);
    calls=[...calls].sort((a,b)=>String(a[sort.value] || '9999').localeCompare(String(b[sort.value] || '9999')));
    hint.textContent=loading?'부산항 터미널을 검색하고 있어요…':error || (q?'부산항 전체 검색':favoritesOnly?'저장한 모선':'앞으로 12일')+' · '+calls.length+'척 · 한국 시간(KST)';
    const rowsKey=JSON.stringify([calls.map(c=>[c.id,c.name,c.voy,c.etb,c.etd,c.eta,c.participants]),sort.value,state.selected?.id,loading,error,favoritesOnly,q]);
    if(force || rowsKey!==prevRows) {
      prevRows=rowsKey;
      if(calls.length) {
        const t=scheduleTable(choose,sort.value);calls.forEach(c=>t.add(c,c.id===state.selected?.id,ctx));schedule.replaceChildren(t.table);
      } else {
        const text=loading?'검색 중':error?'검색을 다시 시도해 주세요.':favoritesOnly?'이 조건에 맞는 즐겨찾기가 없어요.':q?'일치하는 모선이 없어요. 선박명 일부로 다시 검색해 보세요.':'이 날짜에 표시할 항차가 없어요.';
        schedule.replaceChildren(node('div','empty',text));
      }
    }
    const detailKey=JSON.stringify([state.selected,state.profile?.favorites,state.room?.selfJoined,state.changes]);
    if(force || detailKey!==prevDetail) {prevDetail=detailKey;showDetail(state);}
    const sourceKey=JSON.stringify([state.source,state.weather,state.officialSource]);
    if(force || sourceKey!==prevSource) {prevSource=sourceKey;footer.replaceChildren(sourceLine(state,ctx));}
  }
  async function find() {
    const q=input.value.trim(), seq=++request;
    if (!q) {allResults=[];loading=false;error='';paint();return;}
    loading=true;error='';allResults=[];paint();
    try {
      const found=await ctx.api('search?q='+encodeURIComponent(q));
      if(!closed && seq===request) allResults=found.calls || [];
    } catch(e) {if(!closed && seq===request) error=e?.message || '검색 정보를 불러오지 못했어요.';}
    finally {if(!closed && seq===request) {loading=false;paint();}}
  }
  input.addEventListener('input',()=>{
    clearTimeout(timer);++request;error='';allResults=[];loading=!!input.value.trim();paint();timer=setTimeout(find,220);
  });
  search.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();clearTimeout(timer);find();});
  terminal.addEventListener('change',()=>{Promise.resolve(ctx.setTerminal(terminal.value)).then(()=>paint(true)).catch(e=>fail(ctx,e));});
  sort.addEventListener('change',()=>paint(true));
  bookmarks.addEventListener('click',()=>{favoritesOnly=!favoritesOnly;paint(true);});
  more.addEventListener('click',()=>{dates.hidden=!dates.hidden;dates.style.display=dates.hidden?'none':'flex';more.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!dates.hidden));if(!dates.hidden) date.focus();});
  more.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
  date.addEventListener('input',()=>paint());
  paint(true);
  const cleanup=()=>{closed=true;++request;clearTimeout(timer);};
  cleanup.refresh=paint;return cleanup;
}

};
export const ico = key => '<i data-i="'+key+'"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="'+(d[key]||d.compass)+'"/></svg></i>';
export function hydrateIcons(scope=document) {scope.querySelectorAll('i[data-i]:empty').forEach(el=>{el.innerHTML='<svg aria-hidden="true" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="'+(d[el.dataset.i]||d.compass)+'"/></svg>';});}


/* ===== FILE: /crew-profile.js ===== */
const crew = {
  cardigan: Object.freeze({ style: 'cardigan', name: '최대리', department: '영업지원팀', rank: '대리' }),
  vest: Object.freeze({ style: 'vest', name: '김대리', department: '포워더', rank: '대리' })
};

export function crewIdentity(style) {
  return style === 'vest' ? crew.vest : crew.cardigan;
}

export function crewPortrait(style, face = false) {
  return '/assets/crew-' + crewIdentity(style).style + (face ? '-face' : '') + '.webp';
}

function employeeCard(person) {
  return `<label class="crew-card" data-style="${person.style}">
    <input type="radio" name="outfit" value="${person.style}">
    <span class="crew-sleeve" aria-hidden="true"><img alt="" src="${crewPortrait(person.style)}" width="126" height="150"></span>
    <span class="crew-caption"><strong>${person.name}</strong><small>${person.department}</small></span>
  </label>`;
}

export function editCrewProfile(container, { getProfile, save, done, showError }) {
  container.innerHTML = `
    <form class="crew-form" id="profile-form">
      <fieldset class="crew-people"><legend>항구에 나갈 직원</legend>
        <div class="crew-pair">${[crew.cardigan,crew.vest].map(employeeCard).join('')}</div>
      </fieldset>
      <label class="field">채팅 닉네임
        <input name="nickname" id="nick" maxlength="24" autocomplete="nickname" placeholder="예: 마감요정">
      </label>
      <button class="crew-save" type="submit">사원증 저장</button>
    </form>`;
  const form = container.querySelector('form');
  const nickname = form.elements.nickname;
  const outfits = form.elements.outfit;
  const button = form.querySelector('[type=submit]');
  let nameEdited = false, outfitEdited = false, closed = false;
  const refresh = () => {
    const current = getProfile();
    if (!nameEdited) nickname.value = current?.name || '';
    if (!outfitEdited) outfits.value = crewIdentity(current?.style).style;
  };
  nickname.addEventListener('input', () => { nameEdited = true; });
  form.addEventListener('change', event => {
    if (event.target.name === 'outfit') outfitEdited = true;
  });
  refresh();
  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (button.disabled) return;
    button.disabled = true;
    try {
      await save({ name: nameEdited ? nickname.value.trim() : null, style: outfitEdited ? outfits.value : null });
      if (!closed) done();
    } catch (error) {
      if (!closed) showError(error.message);
    } finally { button.disabled = false; }
  });
  const dispose = () => { closed = true; };
  dispose.refresh = refresh;
  return dispose;
}
