    // An orbit toward the hull glides along its exterior instead of parking
    // the camera inside the avatar. The previous side stabilizes head-on turns.
    alternate.copy(to).addScaledVector(normal,offset.subVectors(hit,to).dot(normal));
    tangent.set(normal.z,0,-normal.x).normalize();
    offset.subVectors(alternate,from);
    const along=offset.dot(tangent), minSide=Math.min(3,from.distanceTo(to)*.65);
    if(Math.abs(along)<minSide) {
      const prev=previous ? offset.subVectors(previous,from).dot(tangent) : 0;
      const sign=Math.abs(along)>.9?Math.sign(along):(Math.sign(prev)||Math.sign(along)||1);
      alternate.addScaledVector(tangent,sign*minSide-along);
    }
    return out.copy(firstHit(from,alternate,radius)?hit:alternate);
  }
  update();
  return {update,padding,constrain};
}


/* ===== FILE: /guardian-court.js ===== */
import { makeCourtTide } from './guardian-tide.js';
import { makeHonorHall } from './honor-hall.js';
import { makeWishWall } from './wish-wall.js';

// Facing the plaza, the Korean maritime heroes occupy the two higher central
// seats. The real open sea begins immediately behind this southern quay.
const pi=Math.PI;
const berths=[
  {id:'poseidon',name:'포세이돈',x:4.0,z:48.85,yaw:pi+.12,field:'#356d78',elevation:2.65},
  {id:'yi',name:'이순신 장군',x:-2.1,z:49.55,yaw:pi+.04,field:'#805854',elevation:3.35},
  {id:'jang',name:'해상왕 장보고',x:-8.2,z:49.55,yaw:pi-.04,field:'#546b82',elevation:3.35},
  {id:'njord',name:'뇨르드',x:-14.3,z:48.85,yaw:pi-.12,field:'#527669',elevation:2.65}
];
const stoneTop=1.10, halfWidth=1.62, halfDepth=1.31;
export function buildGuardianCourt(T,scene){
  const root=new T.Group();root.name='Maritime guardians court';scene.add(root);
  const spots=berths.map(({id,x,z,yaw,elevation})=>({
    id,x,y:stoneTop+elevation,z,yaw,front:{x:Math.sin(yaw),z:Math.cos(yaw)},approach:{x,z:z-4.1}
  }));
  const owned=new Set(),mats=new Set(),inscriptions=[];
  const colors=new Map(),get=c=>{if(!colors.has(c))colors.set(c,new T.Color(c));return colors.get(c);};
  // A separately bounded batch for each sculpture avoids submitting the entire
  // court when a player is examining a single emblem in first-person view.
  let batch;
  function begin(){batch={xyz:[],normals:[],colors:[]};}
  function tri(a,b,c,color){
    const u=[b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1],b[2]-a[2]],v=[c[0]-a[0],c[1]-a[1],c[2]-a[2]];
    const n=[u[1]*v[2]-u[2]*v[1],u[2]*v[0]-u[0]*v[2],u[0]*v[1]-u[1]*v[0]];
    const len=Math.hypot(...n)||1,co=get(color);
    for(const p of [a,b,c]){batch.xyz.push(...p);batch.normals.push(n[0]/len,n[1]/len,n[2]/len);batch.colors.push(co.r,co.g,co.b);}
  }
  const quad=(a,b,c,d,col)=>{tri(a,b,c,col);tri(a,c,d,col);};
  const materials=new Map();
  function finish(name,parent=root){
    if(!batch.xyz.length)return;
    const g=new T.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute('position',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(batch.xyz,3));
    g.setAttribute('normal',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(batch.normals,3));g.setAttribute('color',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(batch.colors,3));
    g.userData.preserveTopology=true;g.computeBoundingSphere();owned.add(g);
    if(!materials.has('matte')){const m=new T.MeshStandardMaterial({vertexColors:true,color:0xffffff,roughness:.83,metalness:.03,side:T.DoubleSide});materials.set('matte',m);mats.add(m);}
    const m=new T.Mesh(g,materials.get('matte'));m.name=name;m.castShadow=true;m.receiveShadow=true;parent.add(m);return m;
  }
  const stoneOutline=(width,depth,cut,dy)=>[
    [-width+cut,dy,depth],[width-cut,dy,depth],[width,dy,depth-cut],[width,dy,-depth+cut],
    [width-cut,dy,-depth],[-width+cut,dy,-depth],[-width,dy,-depth+cut],[-width,dy,depth-cut]
  ];
  function plinthLayer(w,d,cut,lo,hi,top,side,bevel=.026){
    const a=stoneOutline(w,d,cut,lo),b=stoneOutline(w,d,cut,hi-bevel),c=stoneOutline(w-bevel,d-bevel,Math.max(.05,cut-bevel*.5),hi);
    for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const j=(i+1)%8;
      quad(a[i],a[j],b[j],b[i],side);quad(b[i],b[j],c[j],c[i],top);tri([0,hi,0],c[i],c[j],top);
    }
  }
  function rod(a,b,r,col,n=6){
    const va=new T.Vector3(...a),vb=new T.Vector3(...b),d=vb.clone().sub(va).normalize();
    const orth=new T.Vector3().crossVectors(d,Math.abs(d.y)<.86?new T.Vector3(0,1,0):new T.Vector3(1,0,0)).normalize(),other=new T.Vector3().crossVectors(d,orth),ring=[];
    for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const th=j*2*Math.PI/n;ring.push(orth.clone().multiplyScalar(r*Math.cos(th)).addScaledVector(other,r*Math.sin(th)));}
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const j=(i+1)%n;
      quad(va.clone().add(ring[i]).toArray(),va.clone().add(ring[j]).toArray(),vb.clone().add(ring[j]).toArray(),vb.clone().add(ring[i]).toArray(),col);}
  }
  function badge(id,field,accent){
    const front=.738;
    const points=[[-.312,.088],[-.35,.124],[-.35,.243],[-.305,.281],[.305,.281],[.35,.243],[.35,.124],[.312,.088]];
    for(let i=1;i<points.length-1;i++)tri([points[0][0],points[0][1],front],[points[i][0],points[i][1],front],[points[i+1][0],points[i+1][1],front],field);
    const y=.184, scale=.76,z=front+.009;
    function trace(path,width=.013){for(let i=1;i<path.length;i++)rod([path[i-1][0]*scale,y+path[i-1][1]*scale,z],[path[i][0]*scale,y+path[i][1]*scale,z],width,accent,5);}
    if(id==='poseidon'){
      trace([[0,-.094],[0,.113]]);
      for(const s of [-1,1])trace([[0,-.018],[s*.071,.003],[s*.102,.053],[s*.102,.103]]);
    }else if(id==='njord'){
      trace([[-.157,.004],[-.073,.002],[.001,.041],[.093,.065],[.124,.091],[.11,.109]]);
      trace([[-.115,-.064],[-.018,-.056],[.056,-.025],[.125,-.017],[.148,.010]]);
    }else if(id==='yi'){
      trace([[-.17,-.022],[-.111,-.070],[.113,-.070],[.17,-.02]]);trace([[-.152,-.012],[-.113,.054],[-.064,.079],[.059,.079],[.12,.045],[.138,-.008]]);
      trace([[-.035,.072],[-.059,.000]],.009);trace([[.055,.074],[.033,.002]],.009);trace([[.135,.017],[.177,.054],[.198,.034]],.011);
    }else{
      trace([[-.19,-.034],[-.118,-.077],[.10,-.078],[.197,-.010]]);trace([[-.047,-.025],[-.047,.115]]);trace([[-.053,.098],[-.14,.014],[-.055,.010]],.010);
      trace([[.066,-.03],[.066,.079]]);trace([[.068,.064],[.126,.013],[.070,.011]],.010);
    }
  }
  // The relief and the surrounding slab share a mineral surface. A distance
  // field from the Korean glyphs carves V-shaped channels into its height map.
  const graniteFaces=[];
  function canvasMap(canvas,display=false){
    const map=new T.CanvasTexture(canvas);map.colorSpace=T.SRGBColorSpace;map.anisotropy=4;
    map.userData.harborLitStone=true;
    if(display)map.userData.harborDisplay=true;
    inscriptions.push(map);return map;
  }
  function makeCanvas(w,h){const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=w;cv.height=h;return cv;}
  const grainSize=192,grainCanvas=makeCanvas(grainSize,grainSize),grain=grainCanvas.getContext('2d');
  let randomState=73175;
  const random=()=>{randomState=(Math.imul(randomState,1664525)+1013904223)>>>0;return randomState/4294967296;};
  if(grain){
    const im=grain.createImageData(grainSize,grainSize);
    for(let y=0;y<grainSize;y++)for(let x=0;x<grainSize;x++){
      const crystal=random(),fleck=crystal<.037?-17:crystal>.963?21:0;
      const mottling=4*Math.sin(x*.082+Math.sin(y*.063))+3*Math.cos(y*.137+x*.043);
      const tone=121+mottling+(random()-.5)*15+fleck,i=(y*grainSize+x)*4;
      im.data[i]=tone-2;im.data[i+1]=tone+1;im.data[i+2]=tone;im.data[i+3]=255;
    }
    grain.putImageData(im,0,0);
    for(let i=0;i<130;i++){
      const x=random()*grainSize,y=random()*grainSize,sz=1+random()*1.9;
      grain.fillStyle=i%3?'rgba(41,48,46,.32)':'rgba(193,193,181,.39)';
      grain.beginPath();grain.moveTo(x,y);grain.lineTo(x+sz,y-.63*sz);
      grain.lineTo(x+1.4*sz,y+.36*sz);grain.lineTo(x+.32*sz,y+.82*sz);grain.fill();
    }
  }
  const stoneMap=canvasMap(grainCanvas), stoneMat=new T.MeshStandardMaterial({map:stoneMap,color:0xffffff,roughness:.96,metalness:0,vertexColors:true});
  mats.add(stoneMat);
  function carve(name,index){
    const w=768,h=219,face=makeCanvas(w,h),ctx=face.getContext('2d'),height=makeCanvas(w,h),bump=height.getContext('2d');
    const mask=makeCanvas(w,h),ink=mask.getContext('2d');
    if(!ctx||!bump||!ink)return null;
    const fontFamily='"Noto Serif KR","Noto Serif CJK KR",Batang,serif';
    let size=139;ink.font='bold '+size+'px '+fontFamily;
    const measured=ink.measureText(name).width;
    if(measured>w-32){size=Math.floor(size*(w-32)/measured);ink.font='bold '+size+'px '+fontFamily;}
    ink.textAlign='center';ink.textBaseline='middle';ink.fillStyle='#fff';ink.fillText(name,w/2,h/2-1);
    const glyph=ink.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data,distance=new Float32Array(w*h);
    for(let i=0;i<distance.length;i++)distance[i]=glyph[i*4+3]>127?30:0;
    for(let y=1;y<h-1;y++)for(let x=1;x<w-1;x++){
      const i=y*w+x;if(distance[i])distance[i]=Math.min(distance[i],distance[i-1]+1,distance[i-w]+1,distance[i-w-1]+1.414,distance[i-w+1]+1.414);
    }
    for(let y=h-2;y>0;y--)for(let x=w-2;x>0;x--){
      const i=y*w+x;if(distance[i])distance[i]=Math.min(distance[i],distance[i+1]+1,distance[i+w]+1,distance[i+w+1]+1.414,distance[i+w-1]+1.414);
    }
    const depth=i=>Math.min(distance[i]/5.5,1);
    const colorMap=canvasMap(face,true);
    const relief=new T.CanvasTexture(height);relief.colorSpace=T.NoColorSpace;relief.anisotropy=4;inscriptions.push(relief);
    function repaint(quarry){
      ctx.fillStyle='#787d7b';ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
      if(quarry){
        const sw=quarry.naturalWidth||quarry.width,sh=quarry.naturalHeight||quarry.height;
        const slice=Math.min(sh,sw*h/w),start=(sh-slice)*(index+.5)/4;
        ctx.drawImage(quarry,0,start,sw,slice,0,0,w,h);
      }else{
        for(let y=-32;y<h;y+=grainSize)for(let x=-23*index;x<w;x+=grainSize)ctx.drawImage(grainCanvas,x,y);
      }
      const source=ctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h),out=source.data,heights=bump.createImageData(w,h);
      for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
        const i=y*w+x,j=i*4,coverage=glyph[j+3]/255,d=depth(i);
        const gray=out[j]*.32+out[j+1]*.53+out[j+2]*.15;
        // Unpainted natural stone stays in the same mid-gray range across all
        // four tablets; the inscription is the shadow inside the cut.
        const field=quarry?Math.max(82,Math.min(150,112+(gray-119)*.43)):gray;
        const dx=x>0&&x<w-1?depth(i+1)-depth(i-1):0,dy=y>0&&y<h-1?depth(i+w)-depth(i-w):0;
        const grazing=(-dx*.62-dy*.80)*174;
        const tone=field+(grazing-(62+12*d)*coverage);
        out[j]=tone-1;out[j+1]=tone+1;out[j+2]=tone;
        const v=Math.round(193+(field-119)*.14-133*d);
        heights.data[j]=heights.data[j+1]=heights.data[j+2]=v;heights.data[j+3]=255;
      }
      ctx.putImageData(source,0,0);bump.putImageData(heights,0,0);
      colorMap.needsUpdate=true;relief.needsUpdate=true;
    }
    repaint(null);graniteFaces.push(repaint);
    return {colorMap,relief};
  }
  function stoneTablet(station,index){
    const tablet=new T.Group();tablet.name=station.name+' 석패';
    tablet.position.set(station.x,.02,46.62);tablet.rotation.y=pi;
    tablet.userData.atdInteract='guardian';tablet.userData.guardianId=station.id;root.add(tablet);
    // The apron, canted shoulders, raised lip and inner reveal have real
    // depth. There is no front face behind the opening until the text bed.
    const points=[],uvs=[],tones=[];
    function panel(a,b,c,tint){
      const u=new T.Vector3(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1],b[2]-a[2]),v=new T.Vector3(c[0]-a[0],c[1]-a[1],c[2]-a[2]);
      const n=u.cross(v),ax=Math.abs(n.x),ay=Math.abs(n.y),az=Math.abs(n.z);
      for(const [x,y,z] of [a,b,c]){
        points.push(x,y,z);
        uvs.push(ax>az&&ax>ay?(z+.53)/1.06:(x+2.29)/4.58,ay>ax&&ay>az?(z+.53)/1.06:y/1.85);
        tones.push(tint,tint,tint);
      }
    }
    function link(left,right,tint){
      for(let i=0;i<left.length;i++){const k=(i+1)%left.length;
        panel(left[i],left[k],right[k],tint);panel(left[i],right[k],right[i],tint);}
    }
    function ring(w,lo,hi,corner,shoulder,z){
      return [[-w+corner,lo],[w-corner,lo],[w,lo+corner],[w,hi-shoulder],[w-shoulder,hi],
        [-w+shoulder,hi],[-w,hi-shoulder],[-w,lo+corner]].map(([x,y])=>[x,y,z-.06*y]);
    }
    const heel=ring(2.20,.02,.27,.08,.10,-.42),toe=ring(2.20,.02,.27,.08,.10,.42);
    link(heel,toe,.62);
    for(let i=1;i<toe.length-1;i++)panel(toe[0],toe[i],toe[i+1],.66);
    const rings=[
      ring(2.04,.19,1.80,.12,.25,-.37),
      ring(2.09,.20,1.81,.12,.25,.20),
      ring(2.03,.27,1.74,.11,.22,.37),
      ring(1.94,.37,1.64,.084,.15,.368),
      ring(1.88,.43,1.58,.062,.10,.287)
    ];
    for(let i=0;i<rings.length-1;i++)link(rings[i],rings[i+1],[.74,.89,.96,.59][i]);
    for(let i=1;i<rings[0].length-1;i++)panel(rings[0][0],rings[0][i+1],rings[0][i],.70);
    const bodyGeometry=new T.BufferGeometry();
    bodyGeometry.setAttribute('position',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(points,3));bodyGeometry.setAttribute('uv',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(uvs,2));
    bodyGeometry.setAttribute('color',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(tones,3));bodyGeometry.computeVertexNormals();bodyGeometry.userData.preserveTopology=true;owned.add(bodyGeometry);
    const body=new T.Mesh(bodyGeometry,stoneMat);body.name=station.name+' 화강석 몸돌';body.castShadow=true;body.receiveShadow=true;tablet.add(body);
    const cut=carve(station.name,index);if(!cut)return;
    const bed=rings[rings.length-1],vertices=[],coords=[],indices=[];
    const centerY=1.005,centerZ=.287-.06*centerY;
    for(const [x,y,z] of bed){vertices.push(x,y-centerY,z-centerZ);coords.push((x+1.88)/3.76,(y-.43)/1.15);}
    for(let i=1;i<bed.length-1;i++)indices.push(0,i,i+1);
    const g=new T.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute('position',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(vertices,3));g.setAttribute('uv',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(coords,2));g.setIndex(indices);g.computeVertexNormals();owned.add(g);
    const material=new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xffffff,map:cut.colorMap,bumpMap:cut.relief,bumpScale:.043,roughness:.96,metalness:0});
    mats.add(material);
    const face=new T.Mesh(g,material);face.name=station.name+' 음각';face.position.set(0,centerY,centerZ);face.receiveShadow=true;
    face.userData.atdInteract='guardian';face.userData.guardianId=station.id;tablet.add(face);
  }
  for(const [index,station] of berths.entries()){
    stoneTablet(station,index);
    const plinth=new T.Group();
    plinth.name=station.id+' honor plinth';
    plinth.userData.atdInteract='guardian';plinth.userData.guardianId=station.id;
    plinth.position.set(station.x,station.elevation,station.z);plinth.rotation.y=station.yaw;root.add(plinth);
    begin();
    {
      plinthLayer(1.93,1.52,.36,-.05,.018,'#9bafa7','#7b9392');
      plinthLayer(1.80,1.43,.32,.018,.067,'#c8c6b4','#a3aba0');
      plinthLayer(1.68,1.35,.30,.067,.105,'#d8d4c0','#b6bead');
    }
    plinthLayer(halfWidth,halfDepth,.34,.105,.20,'#c4c5b5','#94a49d',.04);
    plinthLayer(1.53,1.22,.32,.20,.32,'#e0d7c1','#b6bbaa',.035);
    plinthLayer(1.41,1.08,.25,.32,.39,'#729a95','#466e70');
    plinthLayer(1.37,1.05,.23,.39,.86,'#e2dfcc','#c5c4af',.035);
    plinthLayer(1.42,1.10,.26,.86,.94,'#aeaa91','#91a29a');
    plinthLayer(1.48,1.13,.28,.94,stoneTop,'#efead9','#ded8c1',.045);
    finish(station.id+' layered limestone',plinth);
    const relief=new T.Group();relief.position.set(0,.207,.748);relief.scale.set(1.44,1.64,.44);plinth.add(relief);
    begin();badge(station.id,station.field,'#fff0bb');finish(station.id+' enamel seal',relief);
    begin();
    for(const d of [-1,1]){
      const x=d*.91;
      rod([d*.62,.553,1.061],[d*1.20,.553,1.061],.014,'#a29670');
      quad([x-.02,.51,1.068],[x+.02,.51,1.068],[x+.02,.595,1.068],[x-.02,.595,1.068],'#af9c71');
    }
    finish(station.id+' bronze inlay',plinth);
  }
  // Higgsfield quarry sample. Only its mineral surface is used; the border,
  // inscription, cavity height and chisel shading are generated per statue.
  let quarryImage=null;
  if(typeof Image==='function'){
    quarryImage=new Image();
    quarryImage.onload=()=>{
      if(grain){
        const size=Math.min(quarryImage.naturalWidth||quarryImage.width,quarryImage.naturalHeight||quarryImage.height);
        grain.drawImage(quarryImage,0,0,size,size,0,0,grainSize,grainSize);
        const data=grain.getImageData(0,0,grainSize,grainSize);
        for(let p=0;p<data.data.length;p+=4){
          const d=data.data,shade=Math.max(82,Math.min(150,112+(d[p]*.32+d[p+1]*.53+d[p+2]*.15-119)*.43));
          d[p]=shade-1;d[p+1]=shade+1;d[p+2]=shade;
        }
        grain.putImageData(data,0,0);stoneMap.needsUpdate=true;
      }
      for(const paint of graniteFaces)paint(quarryImage);
      graniteFaces.length=0;
    };
    quarryImage.onerror=()=>{graniteFaces.length=0;};
    quarryImage.src='/assets/guardian-granite.png';
  }else graniteFaces.length=0;
  // Rotate the original tidal sculpture into the new shore frame; this keeps
  // each crest in the physical gap between its two neighbouring statues.
  const seaFrame=new T.Group();seaFrame.position.set(-5.15,2.58,49.20);seaFrame.rotation.y=pi;root.add(seaFrame);
  const surfSpots=spots.map(s=>({x:-(s.x+5.15),z:49.20-s.z}));
  const tide=makeCourtTide(T,seaFrame,surfSpots);
  const hall=makeHonorHall(T,root);
  const walls=[
    makeWishWall(T,root,{x:13,z:47.4,yaw:-pi*.5,side:'left'}),
    makeWishWall(T,root,{x:-23.3,z:47.4,yaw:pi*.5,side:'right'})
  ];
  const wishEntries={left:{x:10.2,z:45.9,radius:3.3},right:{x:-20.5,z:45.9,radius:3.3}};
  function blocks(x,z){return hall.blocks(x,z)||walls.some(w=>w.blocks(x,z));}
  function setWishes(call,wishes){for(const wall of walls)wall.setWishes(call,wishes);}
  function destroy(){
    if(quarryImage){quarryImage.onload=null;quarryImage.onerror=null;}graniteFaces.length=0;
    tide.destroy();hall.destroy();for(const wall of walls)wall.destroy();
    scene.remove(root);owned.forEach(g=>g.dispose());mats.forEach(m=>m.dispose());inscriptions.forEach(map=>map.dispose());
  }
  return {group:root,spots,blocks,wishEntries,setWishes,update:tide.update,destroy};
}


/* ===== FILE: /canvas-harbor.js ===== */
/* Perspective software renderer for devices that deny a WebGL context.
   It rasterizes the same Three scene and articulated meshes into a color and
   reciprocal-depth buffer. It never substitutes a backdrop for a 3D view. */
import {Matrix4,SphereGeometry,CylinderGeometry,ConeGeometry,TorusGeometry} from './vendor/three.module.js';
const clamp=(a,l,h)=>Math.max(l,Math.min(h,a));
const toByte=Array.from({length:4097},(_,i)=>Math.round(255*(i/4096<=.0031308?12.92*i/4096:1.055*(i/4096)**(1/2.4)-.055)));
const srgb=x=>toByte[clamp((x*4096)|0,0,4096)];
const rgb=(r,g,b)=>0xff000000|(clamp(b,0,255)<<16)|(clamp(g,0,255)<<8)|clamp(r,0,255);
const geoCache=new WeakMap(),materialMaps=new WeakMap(),replacements=new WeakMap();
function textureData(tex){
  if(!tex?.image)return null;let cached=materialMaps.get(tex);
  if(cached?.version===tex.version)return cached;
  const cv=tex.image;if(!cv.width||!cv.height)return null;
  try{
    const fit=tex.userData?.harborDisplay?Math.min(1,1024/cv.width,512/cv.height):tex.userData?.harborPaving?Math.min(1,768/cv.width,1152/cv.height):Math.min(1,512/cv.width,256/cv.height);let source=cv;
    if(!cv.getContext||fit<1){source=document.createElement('canvas');source.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(cv.width*fit));source.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(cv.height*fit));source.getContext('2d').drawImage(cv,0,0,source.width,source.height);}
    const pixels=source.getContext('2d').getImageData(0,0,source.width,source.height).data;
    cached={buf:new Uint32Array(pixels.buffer,pixels.byteOffset,pixels.length/4),w:source.width,h:source.height,version:tex.version};materialMaps.set(tex,cached);return cached;
  }catch{return null;}
}
function coarse(g){
  if(replacements.has(g))return replacements.get(g);
  let other=g;const a=g.parameters||{};
  if(g.type==='SphereGeometry')other=new SphereGeometry(a.radius,7,5);
  if(g.type==='CylinderGeometry'&&a.radialSegments>6)other=new CylinderGeometry(a.radiusTop,a.radiusBottom,a.height,7,a.heightSegments,a.openEnded);
  if(g.type==='ConeGeometry'&&a.radialSegments>7)other=new ConeGeometry(a.radius,a.height,7);
  if(g.type==='TorusGeometry')other=new TorusGeometry(a.radius,a.tube,5,10);
  replacements.set(g,other);return other;
}
/* Rigid cloth, hair, and props have close-spaced vertices from their modeling
   meshes. Clustering only within a color preserves the face, badge and ID. */
function geometry(g,quality=0){
  if(!geoCache.has(g))geoCache.set(g,[]);
  const list=geoCache.get(g);if(list[quality])return list[quality];
  const p=g.attributes.position, n=g.attributes.normal, color=g.attributes.color, uv=g.attributes.uv;
  const raw=p.array, inds=g.index?.array||Uint32Array.from({length:p.count},(_,k)=>k);
  let data={p:raw,n:n?.array,col:color?.array,uv:uv?.array,tri:inds};
  if(quality&&color&&p.count>110){
    if(!g.boundingBox)g.computeBoundingBox();const bounds=g.boundingBox,bb=bounds.min;
    const maxExtent=Math.max(bounds.max.x-bb.x,bounds.max.y-bb.y,bounds.max.z-bb.z,.02), step=maxExtent/(quality===1?22:quality===2?13:7);
    const buckets=new Map(),mp=new Uint32Array(p.count),vp=[],vn=[],vc=[],ns=[];
    for(let i=0,k=0;i<p.count;i++,k+=3){
      const x=Math.round((raw[k]-bb.x)/step),y=Math.round((raw[k+1]-bb.y)/step),z=Math.round((raw[k+2]-bb.z)/step);
      const key=(x*64+y)*64+z+':'+Math.round(color.array[k]*510)+','+Math.round(color.array[k+1]*510)+','+Math.round(color.array[k+2]*510);
      let id=buckets.get(key);if(id===undefined){id=ns.length;buckets.set(key,id);ns.push(0);vp.push(0,0,0);vn.push(0,0,0);vc.push(color.array[k],color.array[k+1],color.array[k+2]);}mp[i]=id;
      ns[id]++;for(let j=0;j<3;j++){vp[id*3+j]+=raw[k+j];vn[id*3+j]+=n.array[k+j];}
    }
    for(let i=0;i<ns.length;i++){const k=i*3,l=Math.hypot(vn[k],vn[k+1],vn[k+2])||1;for(let j=0;j<3;j++){vp[k+j]/=ns[i];vn[k+j]/=l;}}
    const ix=[],seen=new Set();for(let i=0;i<inds.length;i+=3){const a=mp[inds[i]],b=mp[inds[i+1]],c=mp[inds[i+2]];if(a===b||a===c||b===c)continue;const code=(a*8192+b)*8192+c;
      if(!seen.has(code)){seen.add(code);ix.push(a,b,c);}}
    data={p:new Float32Array(vp),n:new Float32Array(vn),col:new Float32Array(vc),uv:null,tri:new Uint32Array(ix)};
  }
  const cnt=data.p.length/3;
  for(const k of ['cx','cy','cz','px','py','inv','red','green','blue'])data[k]=new Float32Array(cnt);
  list[quality]=data;return data;
}

export class CanvasHarborRenderer {
  constructor(){
    this.domElement=document.createElement('canvas');this.ctx=this.domElement.getContext('2d',{alpha:false,desynchronized:true});
    if(!this.ctx)throw Error('Canvas 2D unavailable');
    this.shadowMap={enabled:false};this.isCanvasHarborRenderer=true;
    this.w=0;this.h=0;this.cssW=0;this.cssH=0;this.quality=1;this.limit=800;this.last=-10000;this.avg=32;this.samples=0;
    this.vw=new Matrix4();this.mw=new Matrix4();this.im=new Matrix4();
    this.transparent=[];this.extras=[];this._counts={instances:0,triangles:0};
  }
  setPixelRatio(v){this.ratio=clamp(Number(v)||1,.5,1.4);}
  setSize(width,height,updateStyle=true){
    this.cssW=Math.max(1,Math.round(width));this.cssH=Math.max(1,Math.round(height));this.allocate();
    if(updateStyle){this.domElement.style.width=width+'px';this.domElement.style.height=height+'px';}
  }
  allocate(){
    const scale=Math.min(this.ratio||1,Math.min(this.limit/Math.max(this.cssW,this.cssH),Math.sqrt(350000/(this.cssW*this.cssH))));
    this.w=Math.max(1,Math.round(this.cssW*scale));this.h=Math.max(1,Math.round(this.cssH*scale));
    this.domElement.width=this.w;this.domElement.height=this.h;
    this.frame=this.ctx.createImageData(this.w,this.h);this.out=new Uint32Array(this.frame.data.buffer);this.depth=new Float32Array(this.w*this.h);
  }
  dispose(){this.frame=this.depth=this.out=null;this.transparent.length=this.extras.length=0;}
  render(scene,camera){
    if(!this.out)return;const start=performance.now();if(start-this.last<30)return;this.last=start;
    scene.updateMatrixWorld();camera.updateMatrixWorld();this.view=camera.matrixWorldInverse;
    const pm=camera.projectionMatrix.elements;
    this.fx=pm[0]*this.w*.5;this.fy=pm[5]*this.h*.5;this.ox=this.w*.5*(1-pm[8]);this.oy=this.h*.5*(1+pm[9]);this.near=Math.max(.075,camera.near);this.far=camera.far;
    const sky=scene.background, fog=scene.fog;
    this.fogRGB=fog?[srgb(fog.color.r),srgb(fog.color.g),srgb(fog.color.b)]:[190,213,211];
    this.fogNear=fog?.near??65;this.fogInv=1/((fog?.far??170)-this.fogNear);
    const skyR=srgb(sky?.r??.53),skyG=srgb(sky?.g??.75),skyB=srgb(sky?.b??.82);
    this.night=scene.userData.atdSky?.night??clamp((188-skyR)/65,0,1);
    for(let y=0;y<this.h;y++){const f=Math.min(1,y/(this.h*.70));this.out.fill(rgb(skyR+7*f-(8+12*this.night)*(1-f),skyG+5*f-(4+12*this.night)*(1-f),skyB+f),y*this.w,(y+1)*this.w);}
    this.depth.fill(0);this.transparent.length=this.extras.length=0;this._counts={instances:0,triangles:0};
    scene.traverseVisible(o=>{
      if(o.isMesh&&o.geometry?.attributes.position && !Array.isArray(o.material) && o.material.visible!==false){
        if(o.material.transparent){if(o.material.opacity>.009)this.transparent.push(o);}else this.drawObject(o);
      }else if(o.isLine||o.isPoints||o.isSprite)this.extras.push(o);
    });
    for(const o of this.transparent)this.drawObject(o);
    this.ctx.putImageData(this.frame,0,0);this.overlays();
    const elapsed=performance.now()-start;this.avg=this.avg*.89+elapsed*.11;this.samples++;
    if(this.samples>22&&this.samples%30===0&&this.avg>48&&this.limit>480){this.limit=Math.max(480,this.limit-96);this.allocate();}
  }
  drawObject(o){
    const g=o.geometry;if(!g.boundingSphere)g.computeBoundingSphere();const bounds=g.boundingSphere;
    if(o.isInstancedMesh){
      const color=o.instanceColor?.array;
      for(let i=0;i<o.count;i++){
        this.im.fromArray(o.instanceMatrix.array,i*16);this.mw.multiplyMatrices(o.matrixWorld,this.im);
        this.drawMesh(g,bounds,this.mw,o.material,color,i*3,true);
      }
    }else this.drawMesh(g,bounds,o.matrixWorld,o.material,null,0,false);
  }
  drawMesh(g,bs,world,mat,instance,colorOffset,instanced){
    this.vw.multiplyMatrices(this.view,world);const m=this.vw.elements,t=world.elements;
    const bx=bs.center.x,by=bs.center.y,bz=bs.center.z;
    const xx=m[0]*bx+m[4]*by+m[8]*bz+m[12],yy=m[1]*bx+m[5]*by+m[9]*bz+m[13],zz=-(m[2]*bx+m[6]*by+m[10]*bz+m[14]);
    const normX=t[0]*t[0]+t[1]*t[1]+t[2]*t[2],normY=t[4]*t[4]+t[5]*t[5]+t[6]*t[6],normZ=t[8]*t[8]+t[9]*t[9]+t[10]*t[10], radius=bs.radius*Math.sqrt(Math.max(normX,normY,normZ));
    if(zz+radius<this.near||zz-radius>this.far)return;
    const d=Math.max(zz,0);
    if((xx-radius)*this.fx>(this.w-this.ox)*(d+radius)||(-xx-radius)*this.fx>this.ox*(d+radius)||(yy-radius)*this.fy>this.oy*(d+radius)||(-yy-radius)*this.fy>(this.h-this.oy)*(d+radius))return;
    const projected=radius*this.fy/Math.max(.10,zz);
    if(projected<.68)return;
    // The cluster mesh drops UVs. Preserve the carving and grain on stone faces.
    const litStone=mat.map?.userData?.harborLitStone===true;
    const cluster=g.attributes.color && !g.userData.preserveTopology && !litStone?(projected<13?3:projected<43?2:1):0;
    const shape=geometry(instanced&&projected<140?coarse(g):g,cluster);
    const {p,n,col,cx,cy,cz,px,py,inv,red,green,blue}=shape,tris=shape.tri;
    const cr=mat.color.r*(instance?instance[colorOffset]:1),cg=mat.color.g*(instance?instance[colorOffset+1]:1),cb=mat.color.b*(instance?instance[colorOffset+2]:1);
    const singleR=srgb(cr),singleG=srgb(cg),singleB=srgb(cb);
    const ambient=mat.isMeshBasicMaterial?1:.73-.14*this.night,sun=mat.isMeshBasicMaterial?0:.36-.03*this.night;
    // Transform light direction to local coordinates; reciprocal axis scale
    // accounts for elongated beams, oval trees and flattened ripples.
    const nx=Math.sqrt(normX)||1,ny=Math.sqrt(normY)||1,nz=Math.sqrt(normZ)||1;
    const lx=(-.41*t[0]+.79*t[1]+.45*t[2])/nx,ly=(-.41*t[4]+.79*t[5]+.45*t[6])/ny,lz=(-.41*t[8]+.79*t[9]+.45*t[10])/nz;
    const emission=mat.emissiveIntensity>0&&mat.emissive?(mat.emissive.r+mat.emissive.g+mat.emissive.b)*mat.emissiveIntensity*.042:0;
    const fogR=this.fogRGB[0],fogG=this.fogRGB[1],fogB=this.fogRGB[2],texture=textureData(mat.map);
    const invX=1/nx,invY=1/ny,invZ=1/nz;
    for(let i=0,j=0;j<p.length;i++,j+=3){
      const x=p[j],y=p[j+1],z=p[j+2],vx=m[0]*x+m[4]*y+m[8]*z+m[12],vy=m[1]*x+m[5]*y+m[9]*z+m[13],vz=-(m[2]*x+m[6]*y+m[10]*z+m[14]);
      cx[i]=vx;cy[i]=vy;cz[i]=vz;const rz=vz>0?1/vz:0;inv[i]=rz;px[i]=this.ox+vx*this.fx*rz;py[i]=this.oy-vy*this.fy*rz;
      let light=ambient+emission;
      if(n&&sun){const a=n[j]*invX,b=n[j+1]*invY,c=n[j+2]*invZ;const den=Math.sqrt(a*a+b*b+c*c)||1;light+=sun*Math.max(0,(a*lx+b*ly+c*lz)/den);}
      const fog=clamp((vz-this.fogNear)*this.fogInv,0,.89);const lit=light*(1-fog);
      red[i]=(col?srgb(cr*col[j]):singleR)*lit+fogR*fog;green[i]=(col?srgb(cg*col[j+1]):singleG)*lit+fogG*fog;blue[i]=(col?srgb(cb*col[j+2]):singleB)*lit+fogB*fog;
    }
    this._counts.instances++;
    this.tex=texture;this.litStone=litStone;this.alpha=mat.opacity??1;this.writeDepth=mat.depthWrite!==false;this.bias=mat.polygonOffset?0.00014:0;
    for(let t=0;t<tris.length;t+=3){
      const a=tris[t],b=tris[t+1],c=tris[t+2],za=cz[a],zb=cz[b],zc=cz[c];
      if(Math.max(za,zb,zc)<this.near||Math.min(za,zb,zc)>this.far)continue;
      const tint=rgb((red[a]+red[b]+red[c])*.3333,(green[a]+green[b]+green[c])*.3333,(blue[a]+blue[b]+blue[c])*.3333);
      if(Math.min(za,zb,zc)<this.near){this.clipped(shape,a,b,c,tint,mat.side);continue;}
      const xa=px[a],xb=px[b],xc=px[c],ya=py[a],yb=py[b],yc=py[c];
      if((xa<0&&xb<0&&xc<0)||(xa>this.w&&xb>this.w&&xc>this.w)||(ya<0&&yb<0&&yc<0)||(ya>this.h&&yb>this.h&&yc>this.h))continue;
      const A=(xb-xa)*(yc-ya)-(yb-ya)*(xc-xa);
      if(Math.abs(A)<.10||(mat.side===0&&A>0)||(mat.side===1&&A<0))continue;
      this.fill(xa,ya,inv[a],xb,yb,inv[b],xc,yc,inv[c],tint,shape.uv,a,b,c);
    }
  }
  clipped(s,a,b,c,tint,side){
    const points=[a,b,c].map(i=>[s.cx[i],s.cy[i],s.cz[i],s.uv?.[i*2]||0,s.uv?.[i*2+1]||0]),out=[];
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const p=points[i],q=points[(i+1)%3],pin=p[2]>=this.near,qin=q[2]>=this.near;
      if(pin)out.push(p);
      if(pin!==qin){const t=(this.near-p[2])/(q[2]-p[2]);out.push(p.map((val,k)=>val+(q[k]-val)*t));}
    }
    if(out.length<3)return;
    const v=out.map(([x,y,z,u,k])=>[this.ox+this.fx*x/z,this.oy-this.fy*y/z,1/z,u,k]);
    const uv=v.flatMap(p=>p.slice(3));
    for(let i=1;i+1<v.length;i++){const [p,q,r]=[v[0],v[i],v[i+1]],area=(q[0]-p[0])*(r[1]-p[1])-(q[1]-p[1])*(r[0]-p[0]);
      if((side===0&&area>0)||(side===1&&area<0))continue;
      this.fill(p[0],p[1],p[2],q[0],q[1],q[2],r[0],r[1],r[2],tint,uv,0,i,i+1);}
  }
  fill(xa,ya,za,xb,yb,zb,xc,yc,zc,tint,uv,ia,ib,ic){
    const w=this.w,h=this.h,out=this.out,depth=this.depth;
    const det=(xb-xa)*(yc-ya)-(xc-xa)*(yb-ya);
    if(!Number.isFinite(det)||Math.abs(det)<.095)return;
    const zdx=((zb-za)*(yc-ya)-(zc-za)*(yb-ya))/det,zdy=((xb-xa)*(zc-za)-(xc-xa)*(zb-za))/det;
    const tex=this.tex,opacity=this.alpha,opaque=!tex&&opacity>.998,write=this.writeDepth;
    const shadeStone=tex&&this.litStone;
    const lightR=(tint&255)/255,lightG=((tint>>>8)&255)/255,lightB=((tint>>>16)&255)/255;
    let u0=0,v0=0,udx=0,udy=0,vdx=0,vdy=0;
    if(tex&&uv){
      u0=uv[ia*2]*za;v0=uv[ia*2+1]*za;
      const ub=uv[ib*2]*zb-u0,uc=uv[ic*2]*zc-u0,vb=uv[ib*2+1]*zb-v0,vc=uv[ic*2+1]*zc-v0;
      udx=(ub*(yc-ya)-uc*(yb-ya))/det;udy=((xb-xa)*uc-(xc-xa)*ub)/det;
      vdx=(vb*(yc-ya)-vc*(yb-ya))/det;vdy=((xb-xa)*vc-(xc-xa)*vb)/det;
    }
    let topX=xa,topY=ya,midX=xb,midY=yb,botX=xc,botY=yc,swap;
    if(topY>midY){swap=topY;topY=midY;midY=swap;swap=topX;topX=midX;midX=swap;}
    if(midY>botY){swap=botY;botY=midY;midY=swap;swap=botX;botX=midX;midX=swap;}
    if(topY>midY){swap=topY;topY=midY;midY=swap;swap=topX;topX=midX;midX=swap;}
    const long=(botX-topX)/(botY-topY||1),upper=(midX-topX)/(midY-topY||1),lower=(botX-midX)/(botY-midY||1);
    const first=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(topY-.5)),last=Math.min(h,Math.ceil(botY-.5));
    if(first>=last)return;this._counts.triangles++;
    const eps=this.bias+(tex&&!write?.000009:0);
    for(let y=first;y<last;y++){
      const sy=y+.5,e1=topX+(sy-topY)*long,e2=sy<midY?topX+(sy-topY)*upper:midX+(sy-midY)*lower;
      const left=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(Math.min(e1,e2)-.5)),right=Math.min(w,Math.ceil(Math.max(e1,e2)-.5));
      if(left>=right)continue;
      const dx=left+.5-xa,dy=sy-ya;
      let iz=za+dx*zdx+dy*zdy;
      let px=y*w+left;const end=y*w+right;
      if(opaque&&write){
        for(;px<end;px++,iz+=zdx){if(iz+eps>depth[px]){depth[px]=iz;out[px]=tint;}}continue;
      }
      let u=u0+dx*udx+dy*udy,v=v0+dx*vdx+dy*vdy;
      for(;px<end;px++,iz+=zdx,u+=udx,v+=vdx){
        if(iz+eps<=depth[px])continue;
        let ink=tint,a=opacity;
        if(tex){
          const tx=clamp((u/iz*(tex.w-1)+.5)|0,0,tex.w-1), ty=clamp(((1-v/iz)*(tex.h-1)+.5)|0,0,tex.h-1);
          ink=tex.buf[ty*tex.w+tx];a*=((ink>>>24)&255)/255;
          if(shadeStone)ink=rgb((ink&255)*lightR,((ink>>>8)&255)*lightG,((ink>>>16)&255)*lightB);
        }
        if(a<.02)continue;
        if(write)depth[px]=iz;
        if(a>.99){out[px]=ink|0xff000000;continue;}
        const back=out[px],q=1-a;
        out[px]=0xff000000|((((ink>>>16)&255)*a+((back>>>16)&255)*q)<<16)|((((ink>>>8)&255)*a+((back>>>8)&255)*q)<<8)|(((ink&255)*a+(back&255)*q)|0);
      }
    }
  }
  project(x,y,z,m){
    const vx=m[0]*x+m[4]*y+m[8]*z+m[12],vy=m[1]*x+m[5]*y+m[9]*z+m[13],vz=-(m[2]*x+m[6]*y+m[10]*z+m[14]);
    if(vz<this.near||vz>this.far)return null;return [this.ox+vx*this.fx/vz,this.oy-vy*this.fy/vz,vz];
  }
  overlays(){
    const ctx=this.ctx;ctx.save();
    for(const o of this.extras){
      const m=this.vw.multiplyMatrices(this.view,o.matrixWorld).elements,mat=o.material;
      ctx.globalAlpha=mat.opacity??1;
      if(o.isSprite){
        const p=this.project(0,0,0,m),im=mat.map?.image;if(!p||!im)continue;
        const wi=Math.abs(o.scale.x*this.fx/p[2]),he=Math.abs(o.scale.y*this.fy/p[2]);ctx.drawImage(im,p[0]-wi*.5,p[1]-he*.5,wi,he);continue;
      }
      const a=o.geometry.attributes.position.array,s=mat.color;ctx.fillStyle=ctx.strokeStyle='rgb('+srgb(s.r)+','+srgb(s.g)+','+srgb(s.b)+')';
      ctx.lineWidth=1.4;ctx.beginPath();let cont=false;
      for(let i=0;i<a.length;i+=3){
        const p=this.project(a[i],a[i+1],a[i+2],m);if(!p||p[0]<0||p[0]>=this.w||p[1]<0||p[1]>=this.h){cont=false;continue;}
        const behind=1/p[2]+.00025<this.depth[(p[1]|0)*this.w+(p[0]|0)];
        if(behind){cont=false;continue;}
        if(o.isPoints){const sz=clamp((mat.size||.06)*this.fy/p[2],.65,4);ctx.fillRect(p[0],p[1],sz,sz);continue;}
        if(cont)ctx.lineTo(p[0],p[1]);else ctx.moveTo(p[0],p[1]);cont=true;
      }
      if(o.isLine)ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
}


/* ===== FILE: /palm-beats.js ===== */
/** Two distinct open-palm contacts in one communal push. The timestamps also
 * drive the ship, splash, sound and gesture labels in the shared scene. */
export const PALM_DURATION=1.06;
export const PALM_CONTACTS=Object.freeze([.30,.74]);
const keys=[
  // Seconds, forward reach, backswing, body recoil
  [0,0,0,0],[.14,0,1,0],[.201,.10,.74,0],
  [.30,1,0,0],[.327,1,0,0],[.424,.13,.09,.88],
  [.551,0,.98,.20],[.615,.08,.78,0],
  [.74,1,0,0],[.768,1,0,0],[.870,.20,.03,1],
  [.98,0,0,.28],[PALM_DURATION,0,0,0]
];
const smooth=t=>t*t*(3-2*t);
export function palmPose(elapsed){
  if(!(elapsed>0&&elapsed<PALM_DURATION))return {push:0,backswing:0,recoil:0};
  let i=1;while(i<keys.length-1&&elapsed>keys[i][0])i++;
  const a=keys[i-1],b=keys[i],t=smooth((elapsed-a[0])/(b[0]-a[0]));
  return {push:a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*t,backswing:a[2]+(b[2]-a[2])*t,recoil:a[3]+(b[3]-a[3])*t};
}


/* ===== FILE: /shrine-gesture.js ===== */
// Each of the three raised-arm peaks belongs to the same recorded cheer.
export const CHEER_PEAKS = [.93, 1.95, 2.97];
export const CHEER_DURATION = 3.86;
const sat=x=>Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=sat(x);return x*x*(3-2*x);};

export function cheerPose(age) {
  const kneel=smooth(age/.40)*(1-smooth((age-3.45)/.41));
  let raise=0;
  for(const peak of CHEER_PEAKS) {
    const incoming=smooth((age-peak+.39)/.39);
    const outgoing=1-smooth((age-peak-.10)/.34);
    raise=Math.max(raise,incoming*outgoing);
  }
  return {prayKneel:kneel,prayRaise:raise};
}

// A message starts its reading time on the first frame in which its speaker
// is visible. Keep unresolved speakers through one full presence timeout.
export const CHAT_VISIBLE_MS=5600;
const PRESENCE_TIMEOUT_MS=22000;

export function mountCheers(THREE,layer,clock=()=>performance.now()) {
  const speakers=new Map(),point=new THREE.Vector3();
  function node(className,style) {
    const tag=document.createElement('div');
    tag.className=className;tag.style.cssText=style;return tag;
  }
  function speaker(key,anchor) {
    let row=speakers.get(key);
    if(!row){
      const tag=node('harbor-speech-stack','position:absolute;left:0;top:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:4px;width:max-content;max-width:min(82%,21rem);pointer-events:none;');
      // visibility keeps a newly inserted stack measurable without painting
      // it at (0,0) while its speaker's position is still in transit.
      tag.style.visibility='hidden';layer.append(tag);
      row={tag,anchor,messages:[],ritual:null};speakers.set(key,row);
    }
    return row;
  }
  function prune(row,now,sceneTime) {
    row.messages=row.messages.filter(m=>{
      const until=m.shownAt===null?m.created+PRESENCE_TIMEOUT_MS:m.shownAt+CHAT_VISIBLE_MS;
      if(now<until)return true;
      m.tag.remove();return false;
    });
    if(row.ritual && sceneTime>=row.ritual.until){
      row.ritual.tag.remove();
      row.ritual=null;
    }
  }
  function say(key,anchor,message) {
    const words=String(message??'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
    if(!key||!words)return false;
    const row=speaker(key,anchor),now=clock();
    prune(row,now,-Infinity);
    const tag=node('harbor-chat-bubble','box-sizing:border-box;max-width:100%;width:max-content;padding:6px 11px;border:1px solid #a8b3b0;border-radius:13px 13px 13px 4px;color:#23383e;background:#fffffcfa;box-shadow:0 2px 6px #19303829;font:600 clamp(12px,1.3vw,15px)/1.43 system-ui;text-align:left;overflow-wrap:anywhere;');
    tag.textContent=words;
    if(row.ritual)row.tag.insertBefore(tag,row.ritual.tag);
    else row.tag.append(tag);
    row.messages.push({tag,created:now,shownAt:null});return true;
  }
  function acclamation(text,beat) {
    const intro=beat===0;
    const style='box-sizing:border-box;width:max-content;max-width:100%;border:1px solid #a4bdb5;border-radius:13px 13px 13px 4px;box-shadow:0 1px 3px #25453924;text-align:center;overflow-wrap:anywhere;';
    const tag=node('harbor-ritual-bubble',style+(intro
      ? 'padding:4px 9px;background:#fff8e4;color:#415449;font:700 12px/1.3 system-ui;'
      : 'padding:4px 7px;background:#f3fff7;color:#28584e;font:750 13px/1.2 system-ui;'));
    tag.dataset.beat=String(beat);
    tag.textContent=text;
    if(!globalThis.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches){
      tag.animate?.([{opacity:.45,transform:'translateY(3px) scale(.92)'},{opacity:1,transform:'none'}],{duration:140,easing:'ease-out'});
    }
    return tag;
  }
  function exclaim(key,anchor,call,beat,sceneTime) {
    if(!key||!call?.name||!Number.isInteger(beat)||beat<0||beat>3||!Number.isFinite(sceneTime))return;
    const row=speaker(key,anchor);
    prune(row,clock(),sceneTime);
    if(beat===0 || !row.ritual){
      if(beat===0 && row.ritual?.call===call && row.ritual.lastBeat===0)return;
      row.ritual?.tag.remove();
      const tag=node('harbor-ritual','display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;max-width:min(100%,17rem);gap:5px;');
      const responses=node('harbor-ritual-responses','display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:6px;');
      const vessel=[call.name,call.voy].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
      tag.append(acclamation(vessel,0),responses);
      row.tag.append(tag);
      row.ritual={tag,responses,call,lastBeat:0,until:sceneTime+CHEER_DURATION};
    }
    const ritual=row.ritual;
    if(beat<=ritual.lastBeat)return;
    ritual.responses.append(acclamation(call.direction==='import'?'입항!':'출항!',beat));
    ritual.lastBeat=beat;
    ritual.until=Math.max(ritual.until,sceneTime+1.42);
  }
  function paint(camera,player,w,h,sceneTime,local) {
    const now=clock();
    for(const [key,row] of speakers){
      prune(row,now,sceneTime);
      if(!row.messages.length&&!row.ritual){row.tag.remove();speakers.delete(key);continue;}
      const person=row.anchor();
      let x,y,visible=Boolean(person?.parent);
      if(visible){
        point.copy(person.position);point.y+=person===local?2.75:2.99;
        visible=point.distanceToSquared(player)<35*35;
        point.project(camera);
        visible=visible&&point.z>=-1&&point.z<1&&Math.abs(point.x)<1.05&&Math.abs(point.y)<1.05;
        x=(1+point.x)*w/2;y=(1-point.y)*h/2;
      }
      row.tag.style.visibility=visible?'visible':'hidden';
      if(!visible)continue;
      // Flex lays out wrapped messages at their actual height. Clamp the
      // complete stack to the viewport when a speaker is near an edge.
      const half=row.tag.offsetWidth/2, tall=row.tag.offsetHeight;
      x=Math.max(half+6,Math.min(w-half-6,x));
      y=Math.max(Math.min(tall+8,h-8),Math.min(h-8,y));
      row.tag.style.transform='translate('+Math.round(x)+'px,'+Math.round(y)+'px) translate(-50%,-100%)';
      for(const m of row.messages)if(m.shownAt===null)m.shownAt=now;
    }
  }
  function clear(){for(const row of speakers.values())row.tag.remove();speakers.clear();}
  return {exclaim,say,paint,clear};
}


/* ===== FILE: /terminal-marquee.js ===== */
// The central quay ends at z=-31.35. This wall stands beyond that edge in
// the wider northern forecourt, on the same X axis as the four guardians.
export const TERMINAL_WALL=Object.freeze({x:-5.15,z:-36.4,width:24.4,height:8.4,screenY:5.1,footDepth:1.46});
const wallHalfWidth=(TERMINAL_WALL.width+1.02)/2;
export const TERMINAL_WALL_BOUNDS=Object.freeze({
  minX:TERMINAL_WALL.x-wallHalfWidth,maxX:TERMINAL_WALL.x+wallHalfWidth,
  minZ:TERMINAL_WALL.z-TERMINAL_WALL.footDepth/2,maxZ:TERMINAL_WALL.z+TERMINAL_WALL.footDepth/2,
  top:TERMINAL_WALL.screenY+TERMINAL_WALL.height/2+.38
});
const kst=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',hourCycle:'h23'});
function timeLabel(input){
  if(!input || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(input)))return '—';
  const part=Object.fromEntries(kst.formatToParts(new Date(input)).map(p=>[p.type,p.value]));
  return part.month+'/'+part.day+' '+part.hour+':'+part.minute;
}
const digits=new Intl.NumberFormat('ko-KR');
export const quantity=value=>Number.isSafeInteger(value)&&value>=0?digits.format(value):'—';
export function loadingState(call){
  const w=call.loading||{};
  if(w.remainingVan===0 && (w.workTotal>0 || w.completed>0))return '잔량 0';
  if(w.completed>0 && w.remainingVan>0)return '선적 진행';
  if(w.completed===0 && w.remainingVan>0)return '작업 대기';
  if(Number.isSafeInteger(w.completed)||Number.isSafeInteger(w.remainingVan))return '실적 확인';
  if(Number.isSafeInteger(w.workTotal))return '작업량 확인';
  return Number.isSafeInteger(w.plan)?'계획 확인':'미수신';
}
export function boardData(state={}){
  const terminal=state.terminal||'pnc', status=state.terminalStatus||{},source=status.source||{};
  const official=source.kind==='official',live=official&&!source.stale;
  const statusText=source.partial?'확인본 · 일부 모선':source.stale?'최근 확인본':live?'운영사 공식':'연결 대기';
  const priority=c=>c.berthPhase==='berthed'?0:c.berthPhase==='departed'?2:1;
  const rows=(official?(status.calls||[]):[]).filter(c=>c && c.terminal===terminal && !c.isDemo &&
    (c.berthPhase!=='departed'||c.loading?.workCheckedAt))
    .sort((a,b)=>priority(a)-priority(b)||String(a.etb||'~').localeCompare(String(b.etb||'~')));
  return {terminal,source:source.label||'',last:timeLabel(source.checkedAt||source.updatedAt),statusText,live,
    rows,trucks:status.yardTrucks||null};
}
export function mountTerminalMarquee(T,scene,renderer){
  const group=new T.Group();group.name='항만 운영 전광판';scene.add(group);
  group.position.set(TERMINAL_WALL.x,0,TERMINAL_WALL.z);group.userData.atdInteract='board';
  const resources=[],keep=obj=>(resources.push(obj),obj);
  const steel=keep(new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x343f46,metalness:.46,roughness:.52}));
  const liner=keep(new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x506673,metalness:.42,roughness:.6}));
  const accent=keep(new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xf6a747,metalness:.22,roughness:.6}));
  const masonry=keep(new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xa3aaa4,metalness:.02,roughness:.94}));
  const unit=keep(new T.BoxGeometry());
  function box(x,y,z,w,h,d,mat){
    const mesh=new T.Mesh(unit,mat);mesh.scale.set(w,h,d);mesh.position.set(x,y,z);group.add(mesh);return mesh;
  }
  const {width,height,screenY:cy,footDepth}=TERMINAL_WALL;
  const lowerEdge=cy-height/2,upperEdge=cy+height/2,faceDepth=.505;
  // Back the entire screen with a continuous wall from slab to coping. The
  // screen's bottom edge is 90 cm above the paving, in view from the approach.
  box(0,.14,0,width+1.02,.28,footDepth,masonry);
  box(0,(upperEdge+.22)/2,0,width+.78,upperEdge+.22,.88,masonry);
  box(0,cy,0,width+.26,height+.27,.98,steel);
  box(0,upperEdge+.28,0,width+.96,.20,1.19,liner);
  for(const side of [-1,1]){
    box(0,lowerEdge-.115,side*.495,width+.28,.105,.095,liner);
    box(-width/2+1.25,lowerEdge-.18,side*.49,2.2,.05,.09,accent);
    for(let i=1;i<8;i++)box(-width/2+i*width/8,.47,side*.445,.020,.31,.015,liner);
  }
  const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=1440;canvas.height=536;
  const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
  const tex=keep(new T.CanvasTexture(canvas));tex.colorSpace=T.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.userData.harborDisplay=true;tex.anisotropy=4;
  const screenMat=keep(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:tex,toneMapped:false,fog:false}));
  const surface=keep(new T.PlaneGeometry(width,height));
  for(const sign of [1,-1]){
    const face=new T.Mesh(surface,screenMat);face.position.set(0,cy,faceDepth*sign);face.rotation.y=sign<0?Math.PI:0;
    face.userData.atdInteract='board';group.add(face);
  }
  let board, signature='',page=-1,clock=0;
  function words(txt,x,y,size,color,weight=600,cap){
    ctx.font=weight+' '+size+'px system-ui, sans-serif';ctx.fillStyle=color;
    let str=String(txt??'');
    if(cap && ctx.measureText(str).width>cap){
      let lo=0,hi=str.length;
      while(lo<hi){const mid=Math.ceil((lo+hi)/2);if(ctx.measureText(str.slice(0,mid)+'…').width<=cap)lo=mid;else hi=mid-1;}
      str=str.slice(0,lo)+'…';
    }
    ctx.fillText(str,x,y);
  }
  function paint(){
    if(!ctx||!board)return;
    const W=canvas.width,H=canvas.height;
    ctx.fillStyle='#102934';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);ctx.fillStyle='#173541';ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,110);
    ctx.fillStyle='#ffa83e';ctx.fillRect(0,0,10,110);
    words(board.terminal.toUpperCase()+'   선적 현황',46,78,59,'#fff8e8',750,775);
    ctx.textAlign='right';words(board.statusText,W-42,48,31,board.live?'#a2e4bd':'#ffc17e',700);
    words('BUSAN NEW PORT',W-42,87,24,'#accaCE',600);ctx.textAlign='left';
    const X=[46,601,792,987,1200], label='#9fbfc6';
    ['모선 / 수출 항차','적하 계획','적하 완료','잔량(VAN)','선적 상태'].forEach((v,i)=>words(v,X[i],169,30,label,600));
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const y=187+i*85,row=board.rows[page*3+i];
      ctx.fillStyle=i%2?'#142f3a':'#193641';ctx.fillRect(20,y,W-40,80);
      if(!row)continue;
      const w=row.loading||{};
      words(row.name,X[0],y+37,34,'#fff9e9',715,540);
      words([row.voy,row.berth].filter(Boolean).join('  ·  '),X[0],y+67,23,'#b6c9c8',600,540);
      words(quantity(w.plan),X[1],y+53,35,'#f2d598',650,165);
      words(quantity(w.completed),X[2],y+53,35,'#a2e4bd',700,171);
      words(quantity(w.remainingVan),X[3],y+53,35,'#edf5ef',650,170);
      words(loadingState(row),X[4],y+53,29,'#f2d598',620,210);
    }
    if(!board.rows.length)words(board.statusText==='연결 대기'?'공식 선적 자료 연결 대기':'표시할 선적 자료가 없습니다',X[0],240,36,'#c6d5d5');
    ctx.fillStyle='#274650';ctx.fillRect(20,455,W-40,2);
    const trucks=board.trucks;
    const truckLabel=trucks && Number.isFinite(trucks.inbound)&&Number.isFinite(trucks.outbound)?'  ·  반입 '+trucks.inbound+'대 / 반출 '+trucks.outbound+'대':'';
    words((board.source?board.terminal.toUpperCase()+' 운영사 공개자료':'부산항')+truckLabel,46,507,26,'#bed1cf',520,805);
    ctx.textAlign='right';words((board.last==='—'?'':'확인 '+board.last+' KST   ')+(board.rows.length>3?(page+1)+'/'+Math.ceil(board.rows.length/3):''),W-43,507,25,'#b6c9c8',550,540);ctx.textAlign='left';
    tex.needsUpdate=true;
  }
  function setBoard(state){
    const next=boardData(state);
    const key=JSON.stringify([next.terminal,next.statusText,next.source,next.last,next.trucks,next.rows.map(r=>[r.id,r.name,r.voy,r.berth,r.berthPhase,r.etb,r.etd,r.loading])]);
    if(signature===key)return;
    const reset=next.terminal!==board?.terminal;board=next;signature=key;
    page=reset?0:Math.max(0,Math.min(page,Math.ceil(board.rows.length/3)-1));paint();
  }
  function update(time){
    if(!board || !Number.isFinite(time))return;
    if(time-clock<9)return;clock=time;
    const pages=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(board.rows.length/3));
    if(pages>1){page=(page+1)%pages;paint();}
  }
  // On the software renderer the world buffer can shrink on a slow device.
  // Reproject the same live face at display resolution, behind the game's HUD.
  const holder=renderer?.isCanvasHarborRenderer && renderer.domElement.parentElement;
  if(holder){
    canvas.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    canvas.style.cssText='position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:1440px;height:536px;max-width:none;transform-origin:0 0;pointer-events:none;';
    canvas.hidden=true;holder.append(canvas);
  }
  const clipMatrix=new T.Matrix4(),corners=Array.from({length:3},()=>new T.Vector4()),sample=new T.Vector4(),localCamera=new T.Vector3();
  function paintView(camera,w,h){
    if(!holder)return;
    const side=group.worldToLocal(localCamera.copy(camera.position)).z>=0?1:-1;
    clipMatrix.multiplyMatrices(camera.projectionMatrix,camera.matrixWorldInverse).multiply(group.matrixWorld);
    const at=(v,x,y)=>v.set(x*side,y+cy,(faceDepth+.004)*side,1).applyMatrix4(clipMatrix);
    const [tl,tr,bl]=corners;
    at(tl,-width/2,height/2);at(tr,width/2,height/2);at(bl,-width/2,-height/2);
    canvas.hidden=true;
    if(corners.some(c=>c.w<.5)||Math.abs(tr.x/tr.w-tl.x/tl.w)*w<150)return;
    let visible=0;
    for(const fx of [-.46,-.23,0,.23,.46])for(const fy of [-.43,0,.43]){
      at(sample,width*fx,height*fy);
      const x=Math.floor((sample.x/sample.w+1)*renderer.w/2),y=Math.floor((1-sample.y/sample.w)*renderer.h/2);
      if(x<0||y<0||x>=renderer.w||y>=renderer.h)continue;
      if(renderer.depth[y*renderer.w+x]>1/Math.max(.1,sample.w-.28))return;
      visible++;
    }
    if(!visible)return;
    for(const c of corners){c.x=(c.x+c.w)*w/2;c.y=(c.w-c.y)*h/2;}
    const dx=canvas.width,dy=canvas.height,scale=1/tl.w;
    const m=[(tr.x-tl.x)/dx,(tr.y-tl.y)/dx,0,(tr.w-tl.w)/dx,
      (bl.x-tl.x)/dy,(bl.y-tl.y)/dy,0,(bl.w-tl.w)/dy,0,0,1,0,tl.x,tl.y,0,tl.w];
    canvas.style.transform='matrix3d('+m.map(v=>+(v*scale).toFixed(9)).join(',')+')';
    canvas.hidden=false;
  }
  setBoard({});
  return {setBoard,update,paintView,destroy(){if(holder)canvas.remove();scene.remove(group);resources.forEach(x=>x.dispose());}};
}


/* ===== FILE: /harbor-life.js ===== */
// The approach is also used by the presence service to validate eating at the counter.
export const EOMUK_ZONE = Object.freeze({x:-13.75, z:24.7, yaw:-Math.PI / 2, radius:2.45});
export const SHARK_WATCH = Object.freeze({x:-17.75, z:30.35, yaw:-Math.PI / 2, radius:2.8});

const SEA_LEVEL = -.85;
const STALL = Object.freeze({x:-16.15, z:24.7, yaw:Math.PI / 2});
const WATCH_SIGN = Object.freeze({x:-18.42, z:28.15, yaw:Math.PI*.61});

export function buildHarborLife(T, scene) {
  const group=new T.Group();group.name='부산항의 일상';scene.add(group);
  const booth=new T.Group();booth.name='부산 어묵 포장마차';booth.position.set(STALL.x,0,STALL.z);
  booth.rotation.y=STALL.yaw;booth.userData.atdInteract='eomuk';group.add(booth);
  const resources=new Set(),colors=new Map();
  const keep=resource=>{resources.add(resource);return resource;};
  function material(color) {
    if(!colors.has(color))colors.set(color,keep(new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color,roughness:.80,side:T.DoubleSide})));
    return colors.get(color);
  }
  const cube=keep(new T.BoxGeometry()),ball=keep(new T.SphereGeometry(1,12,9)),tube=keep(new T.CylinderGeometry(1,1,1,10));
  const up=new T.Vector3(0,1,0),vector=new T.Vector3();
  function mesh(parent,g,m,x=0,y=0,z=0){
    const o=new T.Mesh(g,typeof m==='string'?material(m):m);o.position.set(x,y,z);parent.add(o);return o;
  }
  function box(parent,m,x,y,z,w,h,d){const o=mesh(parent,cube,m,x,y,z);o.scale.set(w,h,d);return o;}
  function ellipsoid(parent,m,x,y,z,rx,ry,rz){const o=mesh(parent,ball,m,x,y,z);o.scale.set(rx,ry,rz);return o;}
  function cyl(parent,m,x,y,z,r,h){const o=mesh(parent,tube,m,x,y,z);o.scale.set(r,h,r);return o;}
  function bar(parent,m,a,b,r) {
    const dir=vector.set(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1],b[2]-a[2]);const length=dir.length();
    const o=mesh(parent,tube,m,(a[0]+b[0])*.5,(a[1]+b[1])*.5,(a[2]+b[2])*.5);
    o.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(up,dir.normalize());o.scale.set(r,length,r);return o;
  }
  function curve(parent,m,points,r,segments=12){
    const path=new T.CatmullRomCurve3(points.map(p=>new T.Vector3(...p)));
    return mesh(parent,keep(new T.TubeGeometry(path,segments,r,5,false)),m);
  }
  function geometry(vertices,index,vertexColors){
    const g=keep(new T.BufferGeometry());g.setAttribute('position',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(vertices,3));g.setIndex(index);
    if(vertexColors)g.setAttribute('color',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(vertexColors,3));
    g.computeVertexNormals();g.computeBoundingSphere();g.userData.preserveTopology=true;return g;
  }
  function paintedSign(text,w,h,x,y,z) {
    const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=768;cv.height=150;
    const c=cv.getContext('2d');c.fillStyle='#f3dfb8';c.fillRect(0,0,768,150);
    c.strokeStyle='#a9623d';c.lineWidth=3;c.strokeRect(10,10,748,130);
    c.textAlign='center';c.textBaseline='middle';c.fillStyle='#643e33';c.font='bold 106px "Noto Serif KR",serif';c.fillText(text,384,78,620);
    for(const s of [24,744]){c.fillStyle='#927a5f';c.beginPath();c.arc(s,76,3.5,0,Math.PI*2);c.fill();}
    const map=keep(new T.CanvasTexture(cv));map.colorSpace=T.SRGBColorSpace;map.userData.harborDisplay=true;map.userData.harborLitStone=true;
    const face=mesh(booth,keep(new T.PlaneGeometry(w,h)),keep(new T.MeshStandardMaterial({map,roughness:.89})),x,y,z);face.name=text;
  }
  const steel='#8d9b96',chrome='#cbd0ba',charcoal='#394b49',timber='#996b49',cream='#f6dcaa',apron='#536f60';
  // A pushcart base, worn timber service counter and a lidded back shelf.
  for(const x of [-1.35,1.32])for(const z of [-.49,.49]){
    const wheel=cyl(booth,'#4c524d',x,.245,z,.245,.11);wheel.rotation.x=Math.PI*.5;
    const hub=cyl(booth,'#a5a391',x,.245,z+Math.sign(z)*.058,.10,.014);hub.rotation.x=Math.PI*.5;
  }
  box(booth,'#426258',0,.72,0,3.30,.76,1.34);
  const slats=['#b78154','#b07a50','#c28d5d','#aa744c','#bf8957'];
  for(let n=0;n<14;n++)box(booth,slats[n%slats.length],-1.54+n*.237,.72,.683,.218,.64,.027);
  box(booth,timber,0,1.11,.02,3.55,.15,1.58);
  box(booth,'#cda678',0,1.15,.807,3.55,.065,.045);
  box(booth,'#8e5c43',0,.395,.71,3.38,.12,.095);
  for(const x of [-1.56,1.56])for(const z of [-.57,.59]){
    bar(booth,chrome,[x,1.16,z],[x,2.65,z],.033);cyl(booth,charcoal,x,1.17,z,.063,.066);
  }
  // A stretched canvas awning: ridge, sagging front valance, stitched panels.
  const stripes=['#c95e3d','#d5714c','#c55b3a','#db8054','#cf6843'];
  const eaves=[[-.98,2.59],[-.56,2.84],[.02,2.99],[.47,2.92],[.95,2.71],[.96,2.48]];
  for(let i=0;i<9;i++){
    const x0=-1.90+i*(3.8/9),x1=x0+3.8/9,mid=(x0+x1)*.5,xyz=[],ix=[];
    for(let j=0;j<eaves.length;j++){
      const [z,y]=eaves[j];xyz.push(x0,y,z,mid,y-(j===5?.045:.012),z+.008,x1,y,z);
      if(j<eaves.length-1)for(let k=0;k<2;k++){const a=j*3+k;ix.push(a,a+3,a+1,a+1,a+3,a+4);}
    }
    mesh(booth,geometry(xyz,ix),stripes[i%stripes.length]);
  }
  for(const x of [-1.90,1.90]){
    mesh(booth,geometry([x,2.57,-.98,x,2.84,-.56,x,2.99,.02,x,2.92,.47,x,2.71,.95,x,2.57,.95],[0,1,2,0,2,3,0,3,4,0,4,5]),'#ce714d');
    for(let j=0;j<4;j++)bar(booth,cream,[x,eaves[j][1]-.01,eaves[j][0]],[x,eaves[j+1][1]-.01,eaves[j+1][0]],.017);
  }
  bar(booth,cream,[-1.90,2.70,.956],[1.90,2.70,.956],.019);
  box(booth,timber,0,2.49,.979,2.31,.42,.071);paintedSign('부산 어묵',2.26,.385,0,2.495,1.018);
  // Divided stainless pan, amber broth, folded flat fish cakes and round skewers.
  box(booth,charcoal,.15,1.21,.32,1.73,.14,.80);
  box(booth,steel,.15,1.285,.32,1.68,.065,.77);box(booth,'#ad753c',.15,1.319,.32,1.53,.02,.62);
  for(const x of [-.66,.15,.96])box(booth,chrome,x,1.35,.32,.032,.082,.715);
  for(const z of [-.043,.683])box(booth,chrome,.15,1.35,z,1.66,.082,.032);
  const folded=geometry([-.070,-.165,-.033,.073,-.095,-.033,-.064,-.01,-.033,.094,.066,-.033,-.046,.139,-.033,
    -.070,-.165,.033,.073,-.095,.033,-.064,-.01,.033,.094,.066,.033,-.046,.139,.033],[0,1,5,1,6,5,1,2,6,2,7,6,2,3,7,3,8,7,3,4,8,4,9,8]);
  for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
    const x=-.49+(i%6)*.249,z=.12+Math.floor(i/6)*.36,lean=(i%3-1)*.045;
    bar(booth,'#b08a53',[x,1.33,z],[x+lean,1.80+(i%4)*.043,z-.105],.011);
    if(i%3===1){for(let p=0;p<2;p++)ellipsoid(booth,p?'#e2b969':'#ce9855',x+lean*.3,1.40+p*.103,z-.031,.070,.075,.059);}
    else {const fish=mesh(booth,folded,i%2?'#ebca8a':'#d6a064',x+lean*.3,1.49,z-.036);fish.rotation.y=(i%4)*.24;}
  }
  const urn=cyl(booth,'#9faaa0',1.32,1.38,-.01,.218,.41);urn.name='육수통';
  cyl(booth,chrome,1.32,1.604,-.01,.24,.047);ellipsoid(booth,charcoal,1.32,1.65,-.01,.07,.037,.05);
  for(let n=0;n<4;n++){const cup=mesh(booth,keep(new T.CylinderGeometry(.102,.070,.155,12)),cream,-1.29+n*.08,1.27,-.02+n*.065);cup.rotation.z=(n-2)*.03;}
  for(const x of [-1.3,1.3]){
    bar(booth,'#665344',[x,2.81,.32],[x,2.58,.32],.011);
    ellipsoid(booth,'#e98153',x,2.43,.32,.131,.163,.133);cyl(booth,cream,x,2.41,.32,.135,.043);cyl(booth,charcoal,x,2.58,.32,.064,.035);
  }
  // A distinct, short-haired vendor in a kerchief and apron offers a skewer.
  const vendor=new T.Group();vendor.name='어묵집 아주머니';vendor.position.set(-.55,0,-.50);booth.add(vendor);
  const skin='#e5ac8f',hair='#493b39';
  ellipsoid(vendor,'#e6cc9f',0,1.29,0,.304,.48,.212);box(vendor,apron,0,1.28,.162,.403,.53,.095);
  for(const s of [-1,1])bar(vendor,apron,[s*.105,1.63,.119],[s*.165,1.44,.214],.030);
  const vendorHead=new T.Group();vendorHead.position.set(0,1.87,0);vendor.add(vendorHead);
  ellipsoid(vendorHead,skin,0,0,.017,.27,.293,.238);ellipsoid(vendorHead,hair,0,.065,-.058,.283,.26,.211);
  ellipsoid(vendorHead,'#ba654d',0,.169,-.029,.293,.158,.240);
  curve(vendorHead,cream,[[-.27,.121,.03],[-.178,.157,.153],[.015,.147,.202],[.17,.135,.153],[.26,.113,.064]],.017);
  for(const s of [-1,1]){
    ellipsoid(vendorHead,skin,s*.252,-.018,.035,.054,.069,.045);ellipsoid(vendorHead,hair,s*.099,.015,.230,.022,.029,.011);

  }

  ellipsoid(vendorHead,skin,.006,-.029,.249,.040,.031,.032);
  for(const s of [-1,1]){
    const end=[s*(s<0?.38:.31),s<0?1.35:1.45,s<0?.25:.36];
    bar(vendor,'#dcb68a',[s*.232,1.51,.01],[s*.365,1.38,.123],.083);bar(vendor,skin,[s*.365,1.38,.123],end,.059);ellipsoid(vendor,skin,...end,.069,.073,.061);
  }
  bar(vendor,'#bd914f',[.30,1.43,.33],[.49,1.79,.49],.011);
  for(let n=0;n<3;n++)ellipsoid(vendor,'#e3b777',.385+n*.024,1.62+n*.05,.42+n*.022,.048,.041,.044);
  // The cart and the vendor's head each move as a unit. Batch their small
  // pieces by painting the original material colour into the merged vertices.
  const boothPaint=keep(new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xffffff,vertexColors:true,roughness:.80,side:T.DoubleSide}));
  function batchWoodAndFittings(parent,excluded) {
    parent.updateWorldMatrix(true,true);
    const inv=parent.matrixWorld.clone().invert(),transform=new T.Matrix4(),normalMatrix=new T.Matrix3();
    const xyz=[],normal=[],color=[],index=[],pieces=[];
    function collect(item){
      if(item===excluded)return;
      if(item.isMesh&&item.material.isMeshStandardMaterial&&!item.material.map){
        const g=item.geometry,p=g.getAttribute('position'),n=g.getAttribute('normal'),offset=xyz.length/3,rgb=item.material.color;
        transform.multiplyMatrices(inv,item.matrixWorld);normalMatrix.getNormalMatrix(transform);
        for(let v=0;v<p.count;v++){
          vector.fromBufferAttribute(p,v).applyMatrix4(transform);xyz.push(vector.x,vector.y,vector.z);
          vector.fromBufferAttribute(n,v).applyNormalMatrix(normalMatrix);normal.push(vector.x,vector.y,vector.z);color.push(rgb.r,rgb.g,rgb.b);
        }
        const ix=g.getIndex();for(let k=0;k<(ix?.count??p.count);k++)index.push(offset+(ix?ix.getX(k):k));
        pieces.push(item);
      }
      item.children.forEach(collect);
    }
    parent.children.forEach(collect);
    const combined=geometry(xyz,index,color);combined.setAttribute('normal',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(normal,3));
    pieces.forEach(o=>o.removeFromParent());const model=mesh(parent,combined,boothPaint);model.name=parent.name+' 조형';
  }
  batchWoodAndFittings(booth,vendorHead);batchWoodAndFittings(vendorHead);
  // A waist-high harbour marker sits between the cart and the observation
  // spot, well below the sightline from the walkway to the dorsal fin.
  const marker=new T.Group();marker.name='상어 표지판';marker.userData.atdInteract='sharkWatch';
  marker.position.set(WATCH_SIGN.x,0,WATCH_SIGN.z);marker.rotation.y=WATCH_SIGN.yaw;group.add(marker);
  box(marker,'#a7aaa0',0,.043,0,.36,.084,.29);
  box(marker,'#5d6a62',0,.28,0,.16,.44,.13);
  box(marker,'#9e7752',0,.75,0,.146,.96,.113);
  box(marker,'#876b4d',0,1.16,0,.90,.70,.13);
  box(marker,'#b49b78',0,1.532,0,.94,.058,.169);
  batchWoodAndFittings(marker);
  const markerCv=document.createElement('canvas');markerCv.width=352;markerCv.height=272;
  const ink=markerCv.getContext('2d');
  ink.fillStyle='#2e6069';ink.fillRect(0,0,352,272);
  ink.strokeStyle='#8caaa3';ink.lineWidth=3;ink.strokeRect(9,9,334,254);
  // A simple round snout, swept tail and dorsal fin echo the animal below.
  ink.save();ink.translate(176,136);ink.scale(1.42,1.42);ink.translate(-122,-99);ink.fillStyle='#e2ebdc';
  ink.beginPath();ink.moveTo(26,110);ink.quadraticCurveTo(77,71,120,79);ink.lineTo(155,39);
  ink.quadraticCurveTo(158,60,148,82);ink.quadraticCurveTo(176,86,194,98);ink.lineTo(220,74);
  ink.quadraticCurveTo(220,98,209,111);ink.lineTo(221,132);ink.lineTo(196,123);
  ink.quadraticCurveTo(165,141,114,143);ink.lineTo(133,158);ink.quadraticCurveTo(103,159,83,142);
  ink.quadraticCurveTo(43,141,28,125);ink.quadraticCurveTo(18,119,26,110);ink.fill();
  ink.fillStyle='#335e65';ink.beginPath();ink.arc(61,108,4.5,0,Math.PI*2);ink.fill();
  ink.restore();
  const signMap=keep(new T.CanvasTexture(markerCv));signMap.colorSpace=T.SRGBColorSpace;signMap.userData.harborDisplay=true;
  const face=keep(new T.PlaneGeometry(.84,.645));
  const signInk=keep(new T.MeshStandardMaterial({map:signMap,roughness:.78}));
  const front=mesh(marker,face,signInk,0,1.165,.066);front.name=marker.name;
  const back=mesh(marker,face,signInk,0,1.165,-.066);back.rotation.y=Math.PI;
  // Swirls of steam above the pot. A soft alpha gradient leaves the broth and skewers in view.
  const vapourCanvas=document.createElement('canvas');vapourCanvas.width=64;vapourCanvas.height=80;
  const vc=vapourCanvas.getContext('2d');const mist=vc.createRadialGradient(32,40,1,32,40,30);
  mist.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,247,222,.85)');mist.addColorStop(.42,'rgba(255,247,222,.46)');mist.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,247,222,0)');vc.fillStyle=mist;vc.fillRect(0,0,64,80);
  const vapourTexture=keep(new T.CanvasTexture(vapourCanvas));vapourTexture.colorSpace=T.SRGBColorSpace;
  const steamGeo=keep(new T.PlaneGeometry(.40,.65));
  const steam=Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=>{
    const m=keep(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map:vapourTexture,transparent:true,opacity:.35,depthWrite:false,side:T.DoubleSide}));
    const wisp=mesh(booth,steamGeo,m);wisp.name='어묵 솥의 김';wisp.rotation.y=(i%3-1)*.40;return wisp;
  });

  // A round-nosed requiem shark. The body, asymmetric caudal fin and paired
  // pectorals have real thickness and can be viewed from either shore angle.
  const shark=new T.Group();shark.name='북항이';shark.userData.atdInteract='shark';group.add(shark);
  const animal=new T.Group();shark.add(animal);
  const rings=[[-1.77,.085,.10,.035],[-1.58,.114,.12,.015],[-1.24,.185,.174,.012],[-.84,.342,.278,.025],
    [-.30,.49,.377,.045],[.27,.55,.403,.056],[.76,.52,.368,.064],[1.19,.428,.288,.090],
    [1.52,.326,.192,.11],[1.72,.22,.121,.124],[1.83,.10,.066,.132],[1.858,.014,.018,.137]];
  const bodyPos=[],bodyIx=[],bodyColor=[],segments=24,dorsalPaint=new T.Color('#75939e'),flankPaint=new T.Color('#9db7b8'),bellyPaint=new T.Color('#d8dfd0');
  rings.forEach(([z,width,height,offset],j)=>{
    for(let i=0;i<segments;i++){
      const a=i/segments*Math.PI*2,yn=Math.sin(a),xn=Math.cos(a);
      bodyPos.push(xn*width,yn*height+offset,z);
      const color=yn>-.23?flankPaint.clone().lerp(dorsalPaint,Math.min(1,(yn+.23)*1.10)):flankPaint.clone().lerp(bellyPaint,Math.min(1,(-.23-yn)*4.2));
      bodyColor.push(color.r,color.g,color.b);
      if(j<rings.length-1){const p=j*segments+i,q=j*segments+(i+1)%segments;bodyIx.push(p,q,p+segments,q,q+segments,p+segments);}
    }
  });
  const sharkSkin=keep(new T.MeshStandardMaterial({vertexColors:true,color:0xffffff,side:T.DoubleSide,roughness:.54,metalness:.015}));
  const body=mesh(animal,geometry(bodyPos,bodyIx,bodyColor),sharkSkin);body.name='둥근 주둥이와 유선형 몸통';
  // Closed tapered blades are thicker along their leading edge. Each fin uses
  // an internal ridge; silhouettes remain readable even when seen edge-on.
  function fin(parent,name,outline,ridge,width,color,axis='x'){
    const points=outline.flat(),v=axis==='x'?[width,0,0]:[0,width,0];
    points.push(...ridge.map((d,i)=>d+v[i]),...ridge.map((d,i)=>d-v[i]));
    const a=outline.length,b=a+1,index=[];for(let j=0;j<a;j++){const k=(j+1)%a;index.push(j,k,a,k,j,b);}
    const o=mesh(parent,geometry(points,index),color);o.name=name;return o;
  }
  fin(animal,'등지느러미',[[0,.34,.35],[0,.59,.18],[0,.88,-.015],[0,1.03,-.24],[0,1.055,-.36],
    [0,.91,-.337],[0,.76,-.32],[0,.58,-.38],[0,.47,-.50],[0,.35,-.65]],[0,.57,-.095],.077,'#445d71');
  curve(animal,'#a4b8bc',[[0,.49,.263],[0,.65,.145],[0,.854,.010],[0,1.013,-.216]],.017,12);
  fin(animal,'두 번째 등지느러미',[[0,.18,-1.13],[0,.37,-1.35],[0,.41,-1.42],[0,.24,-1.43],[0,.17,-1.51]],[0,.25,-1.33],.04,'#546b79');
  const pectorals=[];
  for(const s of [-1,1]){
    const verts=[[s*.42,.047,.62],[s*.74,.021,.40],[s*1.02,-.021,.09],[s*1.25,-.077,-.17],
      [s*1.31,-.102,-.31],[s*1.22,-.099,-.36],[s*.83,-.050,-.25],[s*.52,-.018,-.15],[s*.39,-.063,.12]];
    pectorals.push(fin(animal,'가슴지느러미',verts,[s*.70,.008,.13],.045,'#71949c','y'));
    fin(animal,'배지느러미',[[s*.19,-.08,-.71],[s*.42,-.15,-.87],[s*.49,-.17,-1.10],[s*.28,-.12,-1.04],[s*.15,-.10,-.95]],
      [s*.28,-.12,-.91],.022,'#7e9fa2','y');
  }
  const tailPivot=new T.Group();tailPivot.position.set(0,.038,-1.58);animal.add(tailPivot);
  const tail=fin(tailPivot,'초승달 꼬리지느러미',[[0,.0,.073],[0,.23,-.10],[0,.47,-.30],[0,.73,-.59],[0,.84,-.78],
    [0,.78,-.88],[0,.50,-.65],[0,.28,-.47],[0,.07,-.34],[0,-.01,-.35],[0,-.26,-.50],[0,-.42,-.63],
    [0,-.46,-.59],[0,-.30,-.32],[0,-.13,-.13]],[0,.095,-.18],.073,'#527481');
  for(const s of [-1,1]){
    ellipsoid(animal,'#364c58',s*.436,.175,1.13,.038,.049,.056);
    ellipsoid(animal,'#f7efda',s*.461,.196,1.146,.014,.014,.017);
    ellipsoid(animal,'#3e5961',s*.186,.166,1.74,.016,.012,.021);
    curve(animal,'#556e71',[[s*.248,.047,1.66],[s*.332,.050,1.49],[s*.392,.067,1.32]],.009,6);
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++){
      const z=.82-i*.111,x=.51+i*.011;
      curve(animal,'#476773',[[s*(x-.026),.212,z],[s*x,.136,z-.035],[s*(x+.01),.060,z-.028]],.011,5);
    }
  }
  // The harbor's opaque ocean clips submerged geometry. Reproject only the
  // underwater silhouettes just below the apparent wet back, tinted through
  // the water. This preserves the full fins in the software and WebGL views.
  const below=new T.Group();below.name='수면에 비치는 북항이';shark.add(below);
  const refract=keep(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:'#7ea5b1',transparent:true,opacity:.69,side:T.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false}));
  const detail=keep(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:'#82a6ad',transparent:true,opacity:.76,side:T.DoubleSide,depthWrite:false}));
  const echo=mesh(below,body.geometry,refract);echo.renderOrder=1;
  for(const source of pectorals){const f=mesh(below,source.geometry,detail);f.renderOrder=1;}
  const echoTail=new T.Group();echoTail.position.copy(tailPivot.position);below.add(echoTail);const tailShadow=mesh(echoTail,tail.geometry,detail);tailShadow.renderOrder=1;
  below.scale.y=.009; below.position.y=.017;
  // The wake is broken up into short arcs so both the nose and the turning
  // tail leave a gentle line, without a permanent oval surrounding the fish.
  const wake=new T.Group();shark.add(wake);const wakePieces=[];
  for(const s of [-1,1])for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
    const start=[1.52,.19,-1.36][j],end=[.49,-.98,-2.96][j],spread=[.31,.66,.84][j];
    const w=curve(wake,keep(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({color:j===0?'#e1eee3':'#b0cfcd',transparent:true,opacity:.52,depthWrite:false})),
      [[s*spread,.035,start],[s*(spread+.19),.035,start*.54+end*.46],[s*(spread+.21+.10*j),.035,end]],j===0?.029:.023,10);
    w.renderOrder=2;wakePieces.push(w);
  }
  const dayTransmission=new T.Color('#84acb4'),nightTransmission=new T.Color('#507e91');
  function update(time=0,night=0,reduced=false){
    const t=reduced?7:Number.isFinite(time)?time:0,n=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(night)||0));
    for(let i=0;i<steam.length;i++){
      const s=steam[i],life=(t*.32+i*.173)%1;
      s.position.set(-.38+(i%3)*.39+.12*Math.sin(t*1.4+i)+life*.065,1.46+life*.76,.13+(i%2)*.25);
      s.scale.set(.53+.82*life,.63+.59*life,1);s.rotation.z=.28*Math.sin(life*3+i);s.material.opacity=.53*Math.sin(life*Math.PI);
    }
    vendorHead.rotation.y=.10*Math.sin(t*.51)-.055;vendorHead.rotation.z=.019*Math.sin(t*.66);
    // The west side of the quay is clear from the stall to the new shoreline
    // return. A short patrol keeps both the bow and tail in this open water.
    const a=t*.18+.66,rx=1.48,rz=3.20;
    shark.position.set(-24.8+rx*Math.sin(a),SEA_LEVEL,30.55+rz*Math.cos(a));
    shark.rotation.y=Math.atan2(rx*Math.cos(a),-rz*Math.sin(a));
    animal.position.y=-.083+.048*Math.sin(a*1.8);animal.rotation.z=.038*Math.sin(a);animal.rotation.x=.025*Math.sin(t*1.12);
    tailPivot.rotation.y=.27*Math.sin(t*1.95);echoTail.rotation.y=tailPivot.rotation.y;
    for(let i=0;i<wakePieces.length;i++){const w=wakePieces[i];w.material.opacity=(.60+.13*Math.sin(t*.90-i*.86))*(1-.16*n);w.position.z=.065*Math.sin(t*1.28-i*.46);}
    refract.color.copy(dayTransmission).lerp(nightTransmission,n);detail.color.copy(refract.color).multiplyScalar(1.08);
  }
  function blocks(x,z){
    if(Math.abs(x-STALL.x)<1.09 && Math.abs(z-STALL.z)<1.93)return true;
    const dx=x-WATCH_SIGN.x,dz=z-WATCH_SIGN.z,c=Math.cos(WATCH_SIGN.yaw),s=Math.sin(WATCH_SIGN.yaw);
    return Math.abs(dx*c-dz*s)<.58&&Math.abs(dx*s+dz*c)<.29;
  }
  function dispose(){scene.remove(group);resources.forEach(resource=>resource.dispose());resources.clear();}
  update();return {group,booth,shark,sharkWatch:SHARK_WATCH,approach:EOMUK_ZONE,blocks,update,dispose};
}

import {mountOfficeSpeech} from './office-speech.js?v=22';

// The plaza desk faces the visitor as they arrive alongside the export quay.
export const DEPARTURE_OFFICE=Object.freeze({x:2.7,z:7.4});
const officeScale=1.18;
const chorusLines=[
  ['What if we just','surrender, surrender?'],
  ['Baby, we could make it','last forever'],
  ['But only if we','surrender, surrender'],
  ['Baby, give me something','to remember'],
  ['So what if we just surrender?'],
];
const chorusStart=2.50,chorusBeat=2.6,chorusCount=chorusLines.length*2;
const chorusEnd=chorusStart+chorusBeat*chorusCount;

const kst=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ko-KR',{timeZone:'Asia/Seoul',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',hourCycle:'h23'});
function actualTime(iso){
  const parts=Object.fromEntries(kst.formatToParts(new Date(iso)).map(p=>[p.type,p.value]));
  return parts.month+'.'+parts.day+'   '+parts.hour+':'+parts.minute+' KST';
}
const limit=v=>Math.max(0,Math.min(1,v));
const ease=v=>{const t=limit(v);return t*t*(3-2*t);};
const mix=(a,b,t)=>a+(b-a)*t;

export function buildDepartureOffice(T,scene,{onAnnouncement}={}){
  let speechOverlay=null;
  const group=new T.Group();group.name='출항 공표소';group.position.set(DEPARTURE_OFFICE.x,0,DEPARTURE_OFFICE.z);
  group.scale.setScalar(officeScale);group.userData.atdInteract='departureOffice';scene.add(group);
  const resources=new Set(),materials=new Map();
  function keep(value){resources.add(value);return value;}
  const cube=keep(new T.BoxGeometry()),sphere=keep(new T.SphereGeometry(1,18,14)),rod=keep(new T.CylinderGeometry(1,1,1,12));
  function mat(color){
    if(!materials.has(color))materials.set(color,keep(new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color,roughness:.76})));
    return materials.get(color);
  }
  function child(parent,x=0,y=0,z=0){const g=new T.Group();g.position.set(x,y,z);parent.add(g);return g;}
  function mesh(parent,geo,color,x=0,y=0,z=0){
    const o=new T.Mesh(geo,typeof color==='string'?mat(color):color);o.position.set(x,y,z);
    o.castShadow=true;o.receiveShadow=true;parent.add(o);return o;
  }
  function box(parent,color,x,y,z,w,h,d){const o=mesh(parent,cube,color,x,y,z);o.scale.set(w,h,d);return o;}
  function oval(parent,color,x,y,z,w,h,d){const o=mesh(parent,sphere,color,x,y,z);o.scale.set(w,h,d);return o;}
  const vec=new T.Vector3(),axis=new T.Vector3(0,1,0);
  function stretch(o,a,b,r,depth=r){
    vec.set(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1],b[2]-a[2]);const length=vec.length();
    o.position.set((a[0]+b[0])*.5,(a[1]+b[1])*.5,(a[2]+b[2])*.5);
    o.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(axis,vec.normalize());o.scale.set(r,length,depth);
  }
  function bar(parent,color,a,b,r,depth=r){const o=mesh(parent,rod,color);stretch(o,a,b,r,depth);return o;}
  function curve(parent,color,coords,radius){
    const path=new T.CatmullRomCurve3(coords.map(p=>new T.Vector3(...p)));
    return mesh(parent,keep(new T.TubeGeometry(path,Math.max(5,coords.length*2),radius,6,false)),color);
  }
  function facelet(parent,color,points,z){
    const s=new T.Shape(points.map(([x,y])=>new T.Vector2(x,y)));
    return mesh(parent,keep(new T.ShapeGeometry(s)),color,0,0,z);
  }
  function surface(parent,w,h,px,py,x,y,z,alpha=false){
    const cv=document.createElement('canvas');cv.width=px;cv.height=py;
    const map=keep(new T.CanvasTexture(cv));map.colorSpace=T.SRGBColorSpace;map.userData.harborDisplay=true;map.anisotropy=4;
    const paint=keep(new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map,toneMapped:false,transparent:alpha,depthWrite:!alpha,side:T.DoubleSide}));
    const item=mesh(parent,keep(new T.PlaneGeometry(w,h)),paint,x,y,z);item.castShadow=false;
    return {item,map,ctx:cv.getContext('2d'),width:px,height:py};
  }
  function text(ctx,word,x,y,size,color,bold,maxWidth){
    ctx.fillStyle=color;ctx.font=(bold?'700 ':'500 ')+size+'px system-ui, sans-serif';
    ctx.fillText(word,x,y,maxWidth);
  }
  const ink='#263a45',teal='#26494b',brass='#ba9a5e',wood='#aa7250',darkWood='#78503c';
  const ivory='#f5ebd6',slacks='#40475a',skin='#efbda1',silver='#a6a9a7',hairlight='#d9d9cf',navy='#37455d',shirt='#f1eadf';
  // Sloped writing surface makes the seal and the lower half of the clerk visible from the promenade.

  const clip=box(group,brass,-.64,1.16,.27,.36,.045,.065);clip.rotation.x=.37;
  // The copper-edged sign stays visible above other visitors even when no ship has left.
  for(const x of [-1.51,2.00]){
    box(group,teal,x,1.52,-.50,.084,3.03,.092);

  }
  box(group,darkWood,.245,3.09,-.50,3.75,.58,.137);

  }

  const clerk=child(group,-.49,0,-.16);clerk.name='삼각 안경 출항 안내원';
  for(const side of [-1,1]){
    oval(clerk,slacks,side*.133,.46,0,.112,.37,.116);
    oval(clerk,'#393b41',side*.14,.12,.06,.131,.081,.183);
  }
  oval(clerk,navy,0,1.02,0,.326,.42,.230);

    facelet(clerk,shirt,[[side*.035,1.25],[side*.143,1.386],[side*.041,1.405],[0,1.355]],.145);
    oval(clerk,'#28364b',0,.99+side*.082,.222,.021,.021,.009);
  }
  oval(clerk,skin,0,1.433,-.007,.116,.158,.11);
  const head=child(clerk,0,1.756,0);head.name='공표원 얼굴';
  const face=oval(head,skin,0,-.018,.0,.391,.393,.319);
  face.geometry=keep(new T.BufferGeometry().copy(sphere));
  oval(head,skin,0,-.104,.304,.047,.047,.037);
  for(const side of [-1,1]){
    oval(head,skin,side*.366,-.062,-.001,.060,.081,.04);

    oval(head,'#d7927f',side*.240,-.134,.223,.051,.018,.012);
  }
  // A continuous sculpted cap runs below the ears at the back and opens at the forehead.
  const vertices=[],indices=[],rings=12,segments=34;
  for(let j=0;j<=rings;j++)for(let i=0;i<=segments;i++){
    const a=i/segments*Math.PI*2,fore=(1+Math.cos(a))*.5;
    const edge=2.16-.98*fore**3+.14*Math.sin(2*a)*fore**3;

  }

  }

  mesh(head,cap,silver);oval(head,silver,0,.105,-.305,.236,.186,.156);
  for(const side of [-1,1]){
    const temple=oval(head,silver,side*.333,.07,-.009,.076,.197,.104);temple.rotation.z=-side*.10;
    curve(head,hairlight,[[side*.055,.391,.108],[side*.17,.337,.193],[side*.275,.226,.215],[side*.348,.110,.155]],.011);

  }

  for(const side of [-1,1]){

  // One hand keeps the form in place. The other follows the stamp's handle.

  const grasp=oval(stamp,skin,-.074,.285,-.015,.084,.10,.081);

  // Framed announcement board. Both faces display the same genuine record.
  const notice=child(group,1.08,0,-.33);
  for(const x of [-.875,.875]){box(notice,teal,x,.85,0,.087,1.7,.087);box(notice,brass,x,.082,0,.174,.09,.18);}
  box(notice,teal,0,1.67,0,2.10,1.26,.126);
