  const board=surface(notice,1.99,1.15,870,504,0,1.673,.068);
  board.item.name='공식 출항 게시판';
  const backBoard=mesh(notice,board.item.geometry,board.item.material,0,1.673,-.068);backBoard.rotation.y=Math.PI;
  const chat=surface(group,2.50,.74,900,274,-.55,2.59,.05,true);chat.item.name='공표원 말풍선';chat.item.visible=false;
  // Reusable camera-facing bubbles keep each sung line readable while walking.
  const chorus=Array.from({length:2},(_,i)=>{
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=900;canvas.height=270;
    const map=keep(new T.CanvasTexture(canvas));map.colorSpace=T.SRGBColorSpace;
    const material=keep(new T.SpriteMaterial({map,transparent:true,depthWrite:false,toneMapped:false}));
    const item=new T.Sprite(material);item.name='공표원 노래 말풍선 '+(i+1);item.visible=false;group.add(item);
    return {item,material,map,ctx:canvas.getContext('2d'),beat:-1,shown:false,words:''};
  });
  function hideChorus(){for(const bubble of chorus){bubble.shown=false;bubble.item.visible=false;bubble.beat=-1;}}
  function chorusPaint(bubble,lines){
    const c=bubble.ctx;if(!c)return;
    c.clearRect(0,0,900,270);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(48,12);c.lineTo(852,12);c.quadraticCurveTo(884,12,884,44);
    c.lineTo(884,194);c.quadraticCurveTo(884,226,852,226);c.lineTo(480,226);c.lineTo(428,257);c.lineTo(433,226);
    c.lineTo(48,226);c.quadraticCurveTo(16,226,16,194);c.lineTo(16,44);c.quadraticCurveTo(16,12,48,12);c.closePath();
    c.fillStyle='#fff8e7';c.fill();c.strokeStyle='#8d7257';c.lineWidth=5;c.stroke();
    c.textAlign='center';
    text(c,'♪',63,69,42,'#a86754',true,50);text(c,'♫',837,196,40,'#a86754',true,50);
    lines.forEach((line,i)=>text(c,line,450,lines.length===1?142:103+i*67,54,ink,true,749));
    bubble.map.needsUpdate=true;
  }
  function chorusUpdate(age,enabled,loop=false){
    const beat=Math.floor((age-chorusStart)/chorusBeat);
    for(let slot=0;slot<chorus.length;slot++){
      const bubble=chorus[slot],index=beat-((beat-slot+2)%2);
      const elapsed=age-chorusStart-index*chorusBeat;
      bubble.shown=enabled&&index>=0&&(loop||index<chorusCount)&&elapsed>=0&&elapsed<chorusBeat+.22;
      bubble.item.visible=bubble.shown&&!speechOverlay;
      if(!bubble.shown)continue;
      if(bubble.beat!==index){
        bubble.beat=index;const lines=chorusLines[index%chorusLines.length];
        bubble.words='♪ '+lines.join(' ')+' ♫';chorusPaint(bubble,lines);
      }
      const enter=ease(elapsed/.16),exit=1-ease((elapsed-chorusBeat)/.22);
      bubble.material.opacity=enter*exit;
      bubble.item.position.set(-.55+(slot?-.075:.075),2.48+.025*Math.sin(elapsed*3)+.045*(1-exit),.23);
      bubble.item.scale.set(2.28*(.95+.05*enter),.684*(.95+.05*enter),1);
    }
  }
  function boardPaint(call){
    const c=board.ctx;if(!c)return;
    c.fillStyle='#f1eddf';c.fillRect(0,0,870,504);c.fillStyle='#314d4e';c.fillRect(0,0,870,116);
    text(c,'출항 확인 기록',41,82,58,'#fff7e1',true,544);c.textAlign='right';text(c,'ATD',823,79,49,'#e6c488',true,172);c.textAlign='left';
    c.fillStyle='#c4b590';c.fillRect(40,143,790,3);
    text(c,call?'공식 출항 확인':'관제소 공식 기록',43,207,40,'#5e6c65',false,776);
    text(c,call?call.name:'출항 확인 대기',39,310,82,ink,true,785);
    text(c,call?'항차 '+call.voy:'출항한 모선의 이름과 항차를 공표합니다.',42,388,call?47:38,teal,true,777);
    text(c,call?'ATD  '+actualTime(call.atd):'부산신항  ·  관제소',43,460,35,'#7a6758',false,775);
    board.map.needsUpdate=true;
  }
  function paperPaint(call,sealed){
    const c=paper.ctx;if(!c)return;
    c.fillStyle='#fff4d9';c.fillRect(0,0,768,510);c.strokeStyle='#b7a47c';c.lineWidth=5;c.strokeRect(15,15,738,480);
    text(c,'BILL OF LADING',42,95,64,ink,true,675);
    text(c,'출항 기념증',45,140,31,'#836e56',false,660);
    c.fillStyle='#bbac89';c.fillRect(45,163,674,2);
    if(call){text(c,call.name+'   '+call.voy,43,214,35,ink,true,677);text(c,'ATD  '+actualTime(call.atd),44,254,29,ink,false,672);}
    for(const y of [278,320,360,405,449]){c.fillStyle='#c2b496';c.fillRect(46,y,673,2);}
    if(sealed){
      c.save();c.translate(389,350);c.rotate(-.075);c.strokeStyle='#a83f35';c.lineWidth=7;c.strokeRect(-254,-57,508,113);
      c.textAlign='center';text(c,'SURRENDERED',0,28,68,'#a83f35',true,471);c.restore();
    }
    paper.map.needsUpdate=true;
  }
  function bubblePaint(call){
    const c=chat.ctx;if(!c)return;
    c.clearRect(0,0,900,274);c.beginPath();c.moveTo(51,9);c.lineTo(849,9);c.quadraticCurveTo(886,9,886,46);c.lineTo(886,186);
    c.quadraticCurveTo(886,223,849,223);c.lineTo(397,223);c.lineTo(296,261);c.lineTo(332,223);
    c.lineTo(51,223);c.quadraticCurveTo(14,223,14,186);c.lineTo(14,46);c.quadraticCurveTo(14,9,51,9);c.closePath();
    c.fillStyle='#fff4d6';c.fill();c.strokeStyle='#725c45';c.lineWidth=6;c.stroke();
    c.textAlign='center';text(c,call.name,450,92,61,ink,true,805);
    text(c,call.voy+' 항차, 출항을 공표합니다!',450,167,45,teal,true,807);
    chat.map.needsUpdate=true;
  }
  let currentTerminal='',known=null,latest=null,active=null,armed=false,startTime=null,sealed=false;
  let displayAge=0,idleStart=null,manualUntil=null,manualRequested=false;
  const queue=[],terminalHistory=new Map();
  function present(call){
    active=call;startTime=null;displayAge=0;idleStart=null;manualUntil=null;manualRequested=false;sealed=false;hideChorus();boardPaint(call);paperPaint(call,false);
    bubblePaint(call);chat.item.visible=!speechOverlay;onAnnouncement?.(call);
  }
  function showLatest(){
    boardPaint(latest);
    paperPaint(latest,Boolean(latest));
  }
  function reset(){
    active=null;startTime=null;idleStart=null;manualUntil=null;manualRequested=false;sealed=false;chat.item.visible=false;hideChorus();queue.length=0;known=null;
  }
  function setDepartures(calls,terminal){
    if(!['pnc','pnit'].includes(terminal)||!Array.isArray(calls))return;
    if(terminal!==currentTerminal){reset();currentTerminal=terminal;latest=null;armed=false;known=terminalHistory.get(terminal)||new Map();terminalHistory.set(terminal,known);}
    const now=Date.now();
    const authorized=calls.filter(c=>c?.atdVerified===true&&c.isDemo===false&&c.terminal===terminal&&
      typeof c.id==='string'&&c.id&&typeof c.name==='string'&&c.name.trim()&&typeof c.voy==='string'&&c.voy.trim()&&
      Number.isFinite(Date.parse(c.atd))&&Date.parse(c.atd)<=now&&Date.parse(c.atd)>=now-7*86400000);
    const sorted=[...new Map(authorized.map(c=>[c.id,c])).values()].sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(b.atd)-Date.parse(a.atd));
    const ids=new Set(sorted.map(c=>c.id)),previous=latest;latest=sorted[0]||null;
    if(known&&armed){
      for(const c of [...sorted].reverse())if(!known.has(c.id))queue.push(c);
    }
    for(const c of sorted)known.set(c.id,Date.parse(c.atd));
    for(const [id,when] of known)if(when<now-7*86400000)known.delete(id);
    for(let i=queue.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(!ids.has(queue[i].id))queue.splice(i,1);
    if(active&&!ids.has(active.id)){active=null;startTime=null;chat.item.visible=false;hideChorus();}
    if(!active){
      if(queue.length)present(queue.shift());
      else if(!armed||previous?.id!==latest?.id||previous?.atd!==latest?.atd)showLatest();
    }
    armed=true;
  }
  function replay(){
    if(active)return true;
    // A visitor can hear her hum without inventing a departure or stamping an
    // unverified document. Official records alone may start the stamp ceremony.
    if(!latest){manualRequested=true;idleStart=null;hideChorus();return true;}
    present(latest);return true;
  }
  function update(time,nearby=false){
    if(!Number.isFinite(time))return;
    if(active&&startTime===null)startTime=time;
    const age=active?Math.max(0,time-startTime):0;
    displayAge=age;
    if(manualRequested){manualUntil=time+chorusCount*chorusBeat;manualRequested=false;}
    const humming=!active&&(nearby||(manualUntil!==null&&time<manualUntil));
    if(humming&&idleStart===null)idleStart=time;
    if(!humming)idleStart=null;
    const songAge=humming?chorusStart+Math.max(0,time-idleStart):age;
    const singing=humming||(!!active&&age>=chorusStart&&age<chorusEnd);
    const rhythm=singing?Math.sin((songAge-chorusStart)*Math.PI*4/chorusBeat):0;
    const read=ease(age/.6)*(1-ease((age-1.3)/.7));
    const rise=ease((age-1.60)/.73),impact=ease((age-2.33)/.17),bounce=ease((age-2.50)/.26),back=ease((age-3.04)/.78);
    const emphasis=active?(1-back):0;
    // At impact the bottom of the rubber stamp is parallel to the writing plane.
    const tilt=Math.PI/2+paper.item.rotation.x,hitZ=.72;
    const hitY=paper.item.position.y-Math.tan(tilt)*(hitZ-paper.item.position.z);
    stamp.position.set(mix(-1.195,-.64,rise*emphasis),mix(1.14,hitY,impact*emphasis)+(.72*(rise-impact)+.16*bounce)*emphasis,mix(.54,hitZ,rise*emphasis));
    stamp.rotation.set(tilt-.31*(rise-impact)*emphasis,0,-.08*(1-impact*emphasis)-.12*(rise-impact)*emphasis);
    const wr=[stamp.position.x-.11,stamp.position.y+.258,stamp.position.z+.072];
    const lifting=(rise-impact)*emphasis;
    const bend=[-1.005-.065*lifting,1.141+.41*lifting,.11+.075*lifting];
    stretch(armTop,[-.721,1.267,-.10],bend,.104,.097);elbow.position.set(...bend);
    stretch(armLower,bend,wr,.084,.078);
    const breath=Math.sin(time*1.52)*.009;
    const idleGlance=active?0:ease((Math.sin(time*.56)-.15)/.85);
    head.position.y=1.756+breath+.009*rhythm;head.rotation.x=.03+.052*idleGlance+.11*read+.13*(impact-bounce)*emphasis+.025*rhythm;
    head.rotation.y=active?0:.17*idleGlance;
    head.rotation.z=.027+.05*(rise-impact)*emphasis+.035*rhythm;
    mouth.scale.y=singing?.010+.020*Math.abs(Math.sin((songAge-chorusStart)*9)):active&&age<2.25?.008+.012*Math.abs(Math.sin(age*12)):.006;
    const blink=Math.abs((time+.2)%4.7-2.0);for(const eye of eyes)eye.scale.y=.049*(blink<.12?.22+.78*blink/.12:1);
    if(active&&age>=2.50&&!sealed){sealed=true;paperPaint(active,true);}
    if(active&&age>=chorusStart)chat.item.visible=false;
    chorusUpdate(songAge,!!active||humming,humming);
    if(active&&age>=chorusEnd+.30){active=null;startTime=null;hideChorus();if(queue.length)present(queue.shift());else showLatest();}
  }
  function speechMessages(){
    if(active&&displayAge<chorusStart)return [{id:'announcement:'+active.id,text:active.name+' '+active.voy+' 항차, 출항을 공표합니다!',opacity:1}];
    return chorus.filter(b=>b.shown).sort((a,b)=>a.beat-b.beat).map(b=>({id:'song:'+b.beat,text:b.words,opacity:b.material.opacity}));
  }
  function mountSpeech(layer){
    speechOverlay?.dispose();speechOverlay=mountOfficeSpeech(T,layer,()=>head);
    chat.item.visible=false;for(const bubble of chorus)bubble.item.visible=false;
  }
  function paintSpeech(camera,player,width,height){speechOverlay?.paint(camera,player,width,height,speechMessages());}
  boardPaint(null);paperPaint(null,false);update(0);
  const approach={x:DEPARTURE_OFFICE.x,z:DEPARTURE_OFFICE.z+2.5*officeScale,yaw:Math.PI,radius:2.45};
  function blocks(x,z){
    const dx=(x-DEPARTURE_OFFICE.x)/officeScale,dz=(z-DEPARTURE_OFFICE.z)/officeScale;
    // Include the visitor's shoulder clearance around the writing desk and narrow board.
    return (dx>-1.72&&dx<.78&&dz>-.75&&dz<1.21)||(dx>-.17&&dx<2.33&&dz>-.62&&dz<-.04);
  }
  return {group,approach,blocks,setDepartures,update,replay,mountSpeech,paintSpeech,dispose(){speechOverlay?.dispose();scene.remove(group);resources.forEach(o=>o.dispose());resources.clear();}};
}


/* ===== FILE: /guardian-tide.js ===== */
// Sculpted water uses ordinary indexed meshes. Both renderers see the same
// changing silhouette, colored glassy folds and droplets, including on mobile.
export function makeCourtTide(T,parent,spots) {
  const group=new T.Group();group.name='Sea passage between the guardians';parent.add(group);
  const openWater=new T.Group();group.add(openWater);
  const centerX=spots.reduce((n,p)=>n+p.x,0)/spots.length;
  const centerZ=spots.reduce((n,p)=>n+p.z,0)/spots.length;
  group.position.set(centerX,0,centerZ);
  const geometries=new Set(),materials=new Set(),palette=[];
  const tau=Math.PI*2, len=13.28;
  const color=c=>new T.Color(c);
  function paint(day,moon,{alpha=1,flat=false,vertices=false,lit=true}={}) {
    const opts={color:day,side:T.DoubleSide,vertexColors:vertices,transparent:alpha<1,opacity:alpha,depthWrite:alpha>=1};
    const m=lit?new T.MeshStandardMaterial({...opts,roughness:.34,metalness:.035,flatShading:flat}):new T.MeshBasicMaterial(opts);
    materials.add(m);palette.push({m,day:color(day),moon:color(moon)});return m;
  }
  function geometry(xyz,index,colors) {
    const g=new T.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute('position',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(xyz,3));
    if(colors)g.setAttribute('color',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(colors,3));
    g.setIndex(index);g.userData.preserveTopology=true;geometries.add(g);return g;
  }
  function add(name,g,m) {
    if(!g.attributes.normal)g.computeVertexNormals();
    const o=new T.Mesh(g,m);o.name=name;group.add(o);return o;
  }
  const rows=104,columns=8;
  const tones=['#4ea7af','#287d8e','#479e9d','#92d3bf','#b5e6cf','#63c6c6','#388d9c','#77c7c3'];
  const riverColors=[],riverIx=[];
  for(let i=0;i<=rows;i++)for(let j=0;j<columns;j++){
    const c=color(tones[j]);riverColors.push(c.r,c.g,c.b);
    if(i<rows&&j<columns-1){const a=i*columns+j,b=a+columns;riverIx.push(a,a+1,b,b,a+1,b+1);}
  }
  const riverG=geometry(new Float32Array((rows+1)*columns*3),riverIx,riverColors);
  riverG.attributes.position.setUsage(T.DynamicDrawUsage);
  const river=add('Continuous folded tidal stream',riverG,paint('#ffffff','#9bbee2',{vertices:true,alpha:.82,lit:false}));
  river.renderOrder=2;
  riverG.boundingSphere=new T.Sphere(new T.Vector3(0,.4,0),len+.3);
  const median=spots[1].x-spots[0].x;
  const firstGap=(spots[0].x+spots[1].x)*.5-centerX;
  const cycle=9.4;
  function smooth(low,high,value){
    const f=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(value-low)/(high-low)));return f*f*(3-2*f);
  }
  function tidePhase(x,t){
    const p=t/cycle-(x-firstGap)/(3*median)+.44;
    return p-Math.floor(p);
  }
  function swellAt(phase){return Math.pow(.5-.5*Math.cos(tau*phase),1.38);}
  // Main channel curls in front of one plinth, behind the next, then
  // resurfaces in each open gap. Higher water stays in the three gaps.
  function surface(x,v,time,out) {
    const u=(x+len)/(2*len);
    const taper=Math.pow(Math.max(0,Math.sin(Math.PI*u)),.38);
    const along=(x+len)/median;
    const swell=Math.sin(x*1.03-time*1.12+v*1.35);
    const angle=(x-(spots[0].x-centerX))/median*Math.PI;
    const gap=Math.pow(Math.abs(Math.sin(angle)),2.6);
    const cross=v*2-1;
    const pulse=swellAt(tidePhase(x,time));
    const bank=.62*Math.min(1,Math.max(0,(Math.abs(x)-3)/6));
    out[0]=x;
    out[2]=taper*(1.05+bank+.81*Math.sin(angle+.26*Math.sin(time*.34))+.17*Math.sin(time*.62-along*2)) + cross*taper*(.99+.38*gap);
    out[1]=.134+taper*(.048+.12*(1-cross*cross)+gap*(.14+.48*pulse+.11*swell)+.033*Math.sin(along*4.3-time*1.3+v*3.7));
  }

  // Front-to-back sections of a real curling breaker. The foam edge folds
  // back over a concave hollow before the face joins the next stretch of water.
  const outline=[
    [-1.62,.07],[-1.20,.21],[-.92,.49],[-.63,.93],[-.42,1.47],[-.20,1.94],
    [.17,2.25],[.55,2.34],[.87,2.22],[1.01,1.98],[.92,1.77],[.78,1.71],
    [.81,1.91],[.68,2.02],[.51,2.02],[.34,1.89],[.21,1.62],[.15,1.22],
    [.22,.85],[.44,.56],[.83,.29],[1.51,.095]
  ];
  const surfColors=['#327f8e','#398e97','#4aa6a5','#66c4b7','#9dddc9','#c6eddb','#e1f4df','#f5f6df','#e5f6e4','#b9e6d9','#8fd5d4','#b4e4dc','#8dd1ca','#5db5ba','#398e9e','#267f90','#2f8e98','#319ba3','#46a6a4','#57b0ae','#6dbdb6','#85c5b7'];
  const capFaces=T.ShapeUtils.triangulateShape(outline.map(([x,y])=>new T.Vector2(x,y)),[]);
  const folds=[];
  const bodyPaint=paint('#ffffff','#bad5ed',{alpha:.82,vertices:true,lit:false});
  const finePaint=paint('#d7f2df','#d4efff',{alpha:.90,lit:false});
  const foamPaint=paint('#eaf7e6','#d1f2ff',{lit:false});
  const dropG=new T.IcosahedronGeometry(1,1);geometries.add(dropG);
  const beads=new T.InstancedMesh(dropG,foamPaint,24);beads.name='Windblown seafoam pearls';beads.frustumCulled=false;group.add(beads);
  const bead=new T.Object3D(), scratch=[0,0,0];
  const sections=6, stride=outline.length;
  for(let k=0;k<spots.length-1;k++){
    const xyz=new Float32Array((sections+1)*stride*3), idx=[],colors=[];
    for(let j=0;j<=sections;j++)for(let i=0;i<stride;i++){
      const col=color(surfColors[i]);const shade=.84+.16*j/sections;colors.push(col.r*shade,col.g*shade,col.b*shade);
      if(j<sections){const a=j*stride+i,b=j*stride+(i+1)%stride;idx.push(a,b,a+stride,b,b+stride,a+stride);}
    }
    for(const [a,b,c] of capFaces){idx.push(c,b,a,sections*stride+a,sections*stride+b,sections*stride+c);}
    const g=geometry(xyz,idx,colors);g.attributes.position.setUsage(T.DynamicDrawUsage);
    g.boundingSphere=new T.Sphere(new T.Vector3(0,1.6,0),4.25);
    const mesh=add('Breaking tide '+(k+1),g,bodyPaint);mesh.renderOrder=3;
    mesh.position.set((spots[k].x+spots[k+1].x)/2-centerX,.14,(spots[k].z+spots[k+1].z)/2-centerZ+.77);
    mesh.rotation.y=[-.32,.24,-.14][k];mesh.scale.set([.91,1.12,.97][k],[1.07,.91,.99][k],[1.15,.84,1.02][k]);
    folds.push(mesh);
  }
  // A handful of silver streaks follow the S-curve of the actual channel.
  // Moving each sample along the channel keeps them continuous across gaps.
  const strokes=16, segs=9, trailIx=[];
  for(let k=0;k<strokes;k++)for(let j=0;j<segs;j++){const a=(k*(segs+1)+j)*2;trailIx.push(a,a+1,a+2,a+2,a+1,a+3);}
  const trailsG=geometry(new Float32Array(strokes*(segs+1)*6),trailIx);
  const trail=add('Traveling eddies',trailsG,finePaint);trail.renderOrder=4;
  trailsG.boundingSphere=new T.Sphere(new T.Vector3(0,.4,0),len+.4);
  // A long ocean swell arrives behind the breaking crests. Its ridgeline is
  // continuous across the stone piers; the plaques stand on the dry front.
  const backRows=74;
  const profile=[[.02,-1.40,'#3e8294'],[.19,-1.10,'#3c95a2'],[.56,-.72,'#69b9ba'],
    [1.04,-.34,'#a0d9cb'],[1.12,-.23,'#dae9da'],[.98,-.08,'#8dcac3'],
    [.62,.23,'#398b9c'],[.28,.54,'#2c788e'],[.02,1.02,'#4fa4ab']];
  const backIx=[],backColors=[];
  for(let i=0;i<=backRows;i++)for(let j=0;j<profile.length;j++){
    const shade=color(profile[j][2]);backColors.push(shade.r,shade.g,shade.b);
    if(i<backRows && j<profile.length-1){
      const a=i*profile.length+j,b=a+profile.length;backIx.push(a,a+1,b,b,a+1,b+1);
    }
  }
  const backGeo=geometry(new Float32Array((backRows+1)*profile.length*3),backIx,backColors);
  backGeo.attributes.position.setUsage(T.DynamicDrawUsage);
  backGeo.boundingSphere=new T.Sphere(new T.Vector3(0,1,-1.8),15.7);
  const incoming=add('Outer swell against the sanctuary',backGeo,paint('#ffffff','#b2d0dc',{vertices:true,lit:false,alpha:.83}));
  incoming.renderOrder=1;openWater.add(incoming);

  // Build a soft alpha texture with thin wisps at the edge. Three staggered
  // sheets of spray rise in each gap, behind the lettering and below faces.
  const vapour=document.createElement('canvas');vapour.width=192;vapour.height=112;
  const vapourCtx=vapour.getContext('2d');
  if(vapourCtx){
    const pixels=vapourCtx.createImageData(192,112);
    for(let y=0;y<112;y++)for(let x=0;x<192;x++){
      const u=x/191,v=y/111;
      const band=.13*Math.sin(u*9.4+.6)+.08*Math.sin(u*16.0);
      const qx=(u-.49)*2.08,qy=(v-.52-band)*2.60;
      const edge=Math.max(0,1-qx*qx-qy*qy);
      const veil=.45+.21*Math.sin(5.6*u+7.3*v)+.14*Math.cos(9.4*u-16*v)+.095*Math.sin(34*u+24*v);
      const opacity=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,edge*edge*.90+edge*veil*.36));
      const off=(y*192+x)*4;pixels.data[off]=207+22*edge;pixels.data[off+1]=227+16*edge;
      pixels.data[off+2]=226+17*edge;pixels.data[off+3]=Math.round(255*opacity);
    }
    vapourCtx.putImageData(pixels,0,0);
  }
  const mistMap=new T.CanvasTexture(vapour);mistMap.colorSpace=T.SRGBColorSpace;
  const mistGeo=new T.PlaneGeometry(1,1);geometries.add(mistGeo);
  const sprays=[];
  for(let k=0;k<folds.length;k++)for(let layer=0;layer<3;layer++){
    const mat=paint('#e5efea','#a5cbdf',{alpha:.34,lit:false});
    mat.map=mistMap;mat.needsUpdate=true;
    const mesh=add('Sea spray '+(k+1)+' · '+(layer+1),mistGeo,mat);
    mesh.renderOrder=5+layer;mesh.raycast=()=>{};
    sprays.push({mesh,k,layer});
  }
  const pos=(attribute,i,x,y,z)=>attribute.setXYZ(i,x,y,z);
  let lastTime=NaN,lastNight=NaN;
  function update(time=0,night=0,reduced=false){
    // Reduced motion freezes the current, including droplets, but preserves
    // the moonlit palette and the three-dimensional curl.
    const t=reduced?1.7:Math.max(0,time);
    const dusk=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(night)||0));
    const paletteChanged=!Number.isFinite(lastNight)||Math.abs(dusk-lastNight)>.007;
    if(paletteChanged){for(const {m,day,moon} of palette)m.color.copy(day).lerp(moon,dusk);lastNight=dusk;}
    if(!paletteChanged&&Number.isFinite(lastTime)&&Math.abs(t-lastTime)<1/32)return;lastTime=t;
    const bp=backGeo.attributes.position;
    for(let i=0;i<=backRows;i++){
      const f=i/backRows,x=(f-.5)*27.2,edge=Math.min(1,Math.sin(Math.PI*f)*3.3);
      const wave=Math.sin(.49*x-t*.77),swell=.65+.37*wave+.24*Math.sin(x*.81+t*.45);
      const bend=-1.60+.58*Math.sin(x*.34-t*.45)+.19*Math.cos(.72*x+t*.59);
      for(let j=0;j<profile.length;j++){
        const [crest,stretch]=profile[j];
        bp.setXYZ(i*profile.length+j,x,.13+edge*(.12+crest*swell),bend+stretch);
      }
    }
    bp.needsUpdate=true;
    const rp=riverG.attributes.position;
    for(let i=0;i<=rows;i++){
      const x=(-len+2*len*i/rows)*.963;
      for(let j=0;j<columns;j++){surface(x,j/(columns-1),t,scratch);pos(rp,i*columns+j,...scratch);}
    }
    rp.needsUpdate=true;
    for(let k=0;k<folds.length;k++){
      const mesh=folds[k],wp=mesh.geometry.attributes.position;
      const phase=tidePhase(mesh.position.x,t),power=swellAt(phase);
      const lift=.36+1.22*power, passing=-Math.sin(phase*tau);
      // The shoulder rises on the west side and tips toward the next guardian;
      // on the falling phase the entire lip stretches down into a broad eddy.
      const roll=smooth(.22,.62,phase)*(1-smooth(.72,1,phase));
      const spray=smooth(.28,.45,phase)*(1-smooth(.57,.78,phase));
      const breath=t*.73+k*.89;
      const lip=.14*passing+.27*roll;
      const reach=.13+.12*k;
      for(let j=0;j<=sections;j++){
        const v=j/sections,arc=Math.sin(Math.PI*v);
        for(let i=0;i<stride;i++){
          const [x,y]=outline[i],tip=y/2.34;
          const height=.04+y*(.69+.31*arc)*lift;
          const lean=tip*tip*(lip+.105*Math.sin(breath+v*3)) + passing*.27*tip;
          pos(wp,j*stride+i,x*(1+reach*(1-power))+.30*(v-.5)+.16*arc+lean,height,
            (v-.5)*(2.10-.98*tip)+.23*Math.sin(x*1.7+breath+v)*tip);
        }
      }
      wp.needsUpdate=true;
      // A short fan of droplets rises from the breaking lip, then falls into the trough.
      mesh.updateMatrix();
      for(let j=0;j<8;j++){
        const p=(t*.58+j*.3819+k*.19)%1,spread=(j%3-1)*.21;
        const r=spray*(.052+.047*Math.sin(Math.PI*p))*(j%3===0?1.1:.78);
        bead.position.set(.76+lip+passing*.19+p*.61,1.82*lift+.78*Math.sin(Math.PI*p)-.67*p,spread+.12+p*.37);
        bead.position.applyMatrix4(mesh.matrix);bead.scale.set(Math.max(.0001,r*.85),Math.max(.0001,r*1.18),Math.max(.0001,r*.88));bead.updateMatrix();beads.setMatrixAt(k*8+j,bead.matrix);
      }
    }
    beads.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate=true;
    for(const {mesh,k,layer} of sprays){
      const fold=folds[k],phase=tidePhase(fold.position.x,t),power=swellAt(phase);
      const life=(t*(.22+.025*layer)+layer/3+k*.23)%1;
      const visibility=Math.sin(Math.PI*life);
      const drift=(life-.34)*1.45;
      mesh.position.set(fold.position.x+drift+Math.sin(t*.55+k+layer)*.22,
        .52+(.46+2.0*power)*(.42+.81*life)+layer*.08,
        fold.position.z+.22-.48*layer-.29*life);
      mesh.scale.set(2.35+life*2.10+layer*.18,1.00+1.09*life+.24*power,1);
      mesh.rotation.set(-.08,.14*(layer-1),-.15+.25*Math.sin(t*.26+k*1.8+layer));
      mesh.material.opacity=(.19+.35*power)*visibility*(1-.24*dusk);
    }
    const tp=trailsG.attributes.position;
    for(let k=0;k<strokes;k++){
      const u=(k*.618034+t*(.036+(k%3)*.003))%1, distance=(.65+.20*(k%4));
      const center=(u*2-1)*(len-1.4);
      for(let j=0;j<=segs;j++){
        const f=j/segs,x=center+(f-.5)*distance*2;
        const v=.15+.68*((k*.373)%1)+.06*Math.sin(f*3.4+k+t*.8);
        const band=(.007+.024*(k%3===0?1:.6))*Math.sin(Math.PI*f);
        for(let side=0;side<2;side++){surface(x,v+(side?band:-band),t,scratch);pos(tp,(k*(segs+1)+j)*2+side,scratch[0],scratch[1]+.039,scratch[2]);}
      }
    }
    tp.needsUpdate=true;
  }
  update(0,0,false);
  return{update,destroy(){parent.remove(group);mistMap.dispose();geometries.forEach(g=>g.dispose());materials.forEach(m=>m.dispose());}};
}


/* ===== FILE: /honor-hall.js ===== */
// A continuous granite sea wall supports the four statues. The dry forecourt
// ends at its first course; the seaward edge drops to the real harbour below.
export function makeHonorHall(T,parent,centerX=-5.15) {
  const group=new T.Group();group.name='해문 · 수호 석벽';group.position.set(centerX,0,46.20);parent.add(group);
  const geometries=new Set(),materials=new Map();
  const unit=new T.BoxGeometry();geometries.add(unit);
  function mesh(g,color){
    if(!materials.has(color))materials.set(color,new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color,roughness:.86,metalness:.035}));
    const item=new T.Mesh(g,materials.get(color));group.add(item);item.castShadow=true;item.receiveShadow=true;return item;
  }
  function block(name,x,y,z,w,h,d,c) {
    const item=mesh(unit,c);item.name=name;item.scale.set(w,h,d);item.position.set(x,y,z);return item;
  }
  function section(name,x,y,z,bottom,top,lowDepth,topDepth,height,shade) {
    const vertices=[];
    const bottomRing=[[-bottom/2,0,-lowDepth/2],[bottom/2,0,-lowDepth/2],[bottom/2,0,lowDepth/2],[-bottom/2,0,lowDepth/2]];
    const topRing=[[-top/2,height,-topDepth/2],[top/2,height,-topDepth/2],[top/2,height,topDepth/2],[-top/2,height,topDepth/2]];
    function triangle(a,b,c){vertices.push(...a,...b,...c);}
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      const n=(i+1)%4;triangle(bottomRing[i],topRing[i],topRing[n]);triangle(bottomRing[i],topRing[n],bottomRing[n]);
    }
    triangle(topRing[0],topRing[2],topRing[1]);triangle(topRing[0],topRing[3],topRing[2]);
    const g=new T.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute('position',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(vertices,3));g.computeVertexNormals();geometries.add(g);
    const item=mesh(g,shade);item.name=name;item.position.set(x,y,z);
  }
  const upper=.89;
  const flank='#697971',top='#c2bba4',trim='#9a9a84',dark='#414f51';
  block('안벽 앞 굽돌',0,.13,3.18,27.0,.26,6.36,flank);
  block('해안 석단 하인방',0,.35,3.32,26.93,.18,6.14,trim);
  block('물가까지 이어진 해안 성벽',0,1.40,4.0,26.6,2.05,6.1,'#71827d');
  const ashlar=['#919b8b','#9da18f','#90978a','#879489','#9a9e8c'];
  for(let row=0;row<5;row++){
    const n=row%2?9:10,w=26.45/n;
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++)
      block('장대석',-13.225+(i+.5)*w,.59+row*.42,1.12,w-.022,.399,.44,ashlar[(i+2*row)%ashlar.length]);
  }
  block('안벽 돌 난간',0,1.65+upper,3.71,26.91,.24,5.97,top);
  // Stone ears to either side of the statues frame the water, leaving the
  // entire horizon and the two central silhouettes open between them.
  for(const side of [-1,1]){
    const x=side*12.90;
    section('해문 날개벽',x,.30,2.76,1.92,1.16,3.28,1.61,3.37+upper,flank);
    block('날개벽 머릿돌',x,3.79+upper,2.76,1.47,.19,1.94,top);
    const obelisk=side*12.48;
    section('연안 표석',obelisk,1.73+upper,5.2,1.21,.76,1.42,.87,3.22,'#7e8c83');
    block('표석 청동띠',obelisk,4.80+upper,5.20,.86,.17,.99,dark);
    section('표석 돌갓',obelisk,4.92+upper,5.20,.95,.42,1.07,.53,.47,top);
  }
  // Stepped piers beneath the raised central figures form two distinct ranks.
  for(const x of [-3.05,3.05]){
    section('성벽 중앙 부벽',x,1.64+upper,3.48,4.82,4.20,4.30,3.69,.71,flank);
    block('중앙 화강석 갑석',x,2.39+upper,3.48,4.45,.16,3.88,top);
  }
  // The front has three blind sea arches built in dressed voussoirs and
  // four narrow battered cutwaters. There is no opening to the statues.
  for(const x of [-6.1,0,6.1]){
    block('해문 아치 안쪽',x,1.03,.878,1.48,1.32,.12,'#536668');
    const r=.73, midY=1.53, n=11;
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const theta=(i+.5)*Math.PI/n;
      const stone=block('홍예 쐐기돌',x+Math.cos(theta)*r,midY+Math.sin(theta)*r,.81,.262,.32,.27,i%2?trim:top);
      stone.rotation.z=theta-Math.PI*.5;
    }
    for(const side of [-1,1])for(const h of [.62,1.00,1.38])
      block('홍예 기둥돌',x+side*.72,h,.80,.32,.365,.28,trim);
    block('석벽 물길 음각',x,.97,.795,.78,.055,.09,'#839e93');
    block('석벽 물길 음각',x,.83,.795,.50,.045,.09,'#a9b5a0');
  }
  for(const x of [-9.15,-3.05,3.05,9.15]){
    section('석단 앞 부벽',x,.25,.80,1.36,.95,1.06,.43,2.02,flank);
    block('부벽 돌머리',x,2.33,.80,1.15,.17,.55,top);
  }
  return {group,blocks(x,z){return Math.abs(x-centerX)<=13.59&&z>=46.20&&z<=54.1;},destroy(){
    parent.remove(group);geometries.forEach(g=>g.dispose());materials.forEach(m=>m.dispose());
  }};
}


/* ===== FILE: /wish-wall.js ===== */
// The ground-level long side faces local +Z. The upper box sits on a second
// ground box behind it; the writing surface stays accessible from the plaza.
const W = 6, H = 2.595, REAR_Z = -2.43, MAX_NOTES = 12;
const bounds = Object.freeze({minX:-3.01,maxX:3.01,minZ:REAR_Z-1.19,maxZ:1.24});
const dimensions = Object.freeze({width:bounds.maxX-bounds.minX,height:H+2.60,depth:bounds.maxZ-bounds.minZ,bounds});
const FONT = 'AtdPret,"Apple SD Gothic Neo","Malgun Gothic",sans-serif';

function clean(value, limit) {
  return Array.from(String(value ?? '').replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f\u202a-\u202e\u2066-\u2069]/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()).slice(0, limit).join('');
}

function clipped(ctx, text, width) {
  if (ctx.measureText(text).width <= width) return text;
  const chars = Array.from(text);
  while (chars.length && ctx.measureText(chars.join('') + '…').width > width) chars.pop();
  return chars.join('') + '…';
}

function bodyLines(ctx,text,width,maxLines){
  const chars=Array.from(text),lines=[];
  while(chars.length && lines.length<maxLines-1){
    let line='';
    while(chars.length && ctx.measureText(line+chars[0]).width<=width)line+=chars.shift();
    if(!line && chars.length)line=chars.shift();
    lines.push(line.trim());
  }
  if(chars.length)lines.push(clipped(ctx,chars.join('').trim(),width));
  return lines;
}

function drawBL(ctx,entry,vessel){
  const {width,height}=ctx.canvas;
  ctx.fillStyle='#e6eff0';ctx.fillRect(0,0,width,height);
  ctx.fillStyle='#325968';ctx.fillRect(0,0,width,37);
  ctx.font='bold 22px '+FONT;ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.fillStyle='#fff8e7';
  ctx.fillText('게임 B/L',20,19);
  ctx.font='600 16px '+FONT;ctx.textAlign='right';
  ctx.fillText(clipped(ctx,vessel,width*.59),width-16,19);
  ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.textBaseline='alphabetic';ctx.fillStyle='#26454c';ctx.font='600 24px '+FONT;
  bodyLines(ctx,entry.text,width-52,2).forEach((line,i)=>ctx.fillText(line,22,67+i*30));
  ctx.fillStyle='#86a7ad';ctx.fillRect(18,height-43,width-36,1.5);
  ctx.font='bold 20px '+FONT;ctx.fillStyle='#3c5b61';
  ctx.fillText(clipped(ctx,entry.name,width-130),22,height-14);
  ctx.font='600 16px '+FONT;ctx.textAlign='right';ctx.fillText('출항 기념',width-17,height-15);
}
function drawNote(ctx, entry, accent, paper) {
  const {width, height} = ctx.canvas;
  ctx.fillStyle = paper;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
  ctx.fillStyle = accent;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 7, height);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#363b35';
  ctx.font = '600 29px ' + FONT;
  ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  bodyLines(ctx,entry.text,width-58,2).forEach((line,i)=>ctx.fillText(line,25,52+i*39));
  ctx.fillStyle = accent;
  ctx.fillRect(25, height-44, width-49, 1.5);
  ctx.font = 'bold 22px ' + FONT;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#45584c';
  ctx.fillText(clipped(ctx, entry.name, width-63), 25, height-12);
}

export function makeWishWall(T, parent, {x,z,yaw=0,side='left'}) {
  const wallSide=side==='right'?'right':'left';
  function tag(obj){obj.userData.atdInteract='wish';obj.userData.wishSide=wallSide;}
  const group = new T.Group();
  group.name = 'Harbor wish containers · '+wallSide;
  group.position.set(x, 0, z);
  group.rotation.y = yaw;
  parent.add(group);
  const materials = new Set(), geometries = new Set(), textures = new Set();
  const metal = new T.MeshStandardMaterial({vertexColors:true, roughness:.8, metalness:.13});
  materials.add(metal);
  const positions=[], colors=[], indices=[], normals=[];
  const palette = new Map();
  function face(points, color, direction) {
    let rgb=palette.get(color);
    if (!rgb) {rgb=new T.Color(color);palette.set(color,rgb);}
    const start=positions.length/3;
    for (const point of points) {
      positions.push(...point);colors.push(rgb.r,rgb.g,rgb.b);normals.push(...direction);
    }
    indices.push(start,start+1,start+2,start,start+2,start+3);
  }
  function box(cx, cy, cz, w, h, d, paint) {
    const l=cx-w/2,r=cx+w/2,b=cy-h/2,t=cy+h/2,f=cz+d/2,k=cz-d/2;
    face([[l,b,f],[r,b,f],[r,t,f],[l,t,f]],paint,[0,0,1]);
    face([[r,b,k],[l,b,k],[l,t,k],[r,t,k]],paint,[0,0,-1]);
    face([[r,b,f],[r,b,k],[r,t,k],[r,t,f]],paint,[1,0,0]);
    face([[l,b,k],[l,b,f],[l,t,f],[l,t,k]],paint,[-1,0,0]);
    face([[l,t,f],[r,t,f],[r,t,k],[l,t,k]],paint,[0,1,0]);
    face([[l,b,k],[r,b,k],[r,b,f],[l,b,f]],paint,[0,-1,0]);
  }

  const steel='#b8bdab';
  const paints={
    sage:['#77998a','#91ab9b','#536f67','#355951','#92a99b'],
    sand:['#c8bc9f','#d4c8aa','#8f8e79','#677266','#c4baa2'],
    coral:['#b27e69','#c5977f','#8a695a','#605951','#ad8472'],
    slate:['#7b9299','#96a6a8','#566e76','#3f5662','#81989d']
  };
  function freight(level,cz,paint){
    const [sage,rib,frame,casting,door]=paints[paint];
    const part=(cx,cy,z,w,h,d,color)=>box(cx,cy+level*H,z+cz,w,h,d,color);
    part(0,1.31,0,5.82,2.41,2.16,sage);
    // Stamped sidewalls, roof ribs, two steel corner frames and ISO casting openings.
    for (let i=0;i<34;i++) {
      const xx=-2.79+i*.169;
      part(xx,1.31,-1.092,.059,2.24,.032,rib);
      part(xx,1.31,1.092,.059,2.24,.032,rib);
      part(xx,2.526,0,.063,.031,2.14,rib);
    }
    for (const side of [-1,1]) {
      for (const end of [-1,1]) {
        part(end*2.932,1.30,side*1.116,.115,2.5,.105,frame);
        for (const yy of [.118,2.483]) {
          part(end*2.922,yy,side*1.106,.156,.23,.138,casting);
          part(end*2.922,yy,side*1.177,.082,.044,.012,'#28443e');
        }
      }
      for (const yy of [.10,2.496]) part(0,yy,side*1.116,5.90,.103,.095,frame);
      // Paired end doors, locking rods with lever handles, hinge straps and gaskets.
      for (const j of [-1,1]) {
        part(side*2.933,1.305,j*.527,.045,2.27,1.015,door);
        for (let k=0;k<4;k++) part(side*2.963,.55+k*.47,j*.527,.02,.044,.85,sage);
        part(side*2.968,1.286,j*.362,.045,2.2,.043,steel);
        part(side*2.988,.949,j*.485,.024,.052,.265,casting);
        for (let k=0;k<3;k++) part(side*2.960,.44+k*.82,j*.960,.055,.081,.185,'#627f71');
      }
      part(side*2.956,1.30,0,.026,2.24,.030,casting);
    }
  }
  freight(0,0,wallSide==='left'?'sage':'slate');
  freight(0,REAR_Z,wallSide==='left'?'coral':'sage');
  freight(1,REAR_Z,wallSide==='left'?'sand':'coral');
  const fascia=wallSide==='left'?'#345c57':'#385665';
  // Recessed cork-and-linen mounting field, enamel fascia and contrasting wood trim.
  box(0,1.146,1.122,5.47,1.64,.066,'#4b665c');
  box(0,1.146,1.165,5.36,1.54,.034,'#d5c7a6');
  for (const side of [-1,1]) box(side*2.713,1.146,1.172,.045,1.63,.043,'#b68b66');
  for (const yy of [.342,1.949]) box(0,yy,1.166,5.46,.045,.049,'#b68b66');
  box(0,2.23,1.128,5.49,.442,.081,fascia);
  box(-2.757,2.23,1.165,.05,.421,.035,'#c97860');
  // Metal fasteners sit proud of the frame, not the pinned papers.
  for(const xx of [-2.65,2.65]) for(const yy of [.395,1.902,2.202,2.305]) {
    box(xx,yy,1.194,.034,.034,.016,steel);
  }

  const steelGeometry=new T.BufferGeometry();
  steelGeometry.setAttribute('position',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(positions,3));
  steelGeometry.setAttribute('color',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(colors,3));
  steelGeometry.setAttribute('normal',new T.Float32BufferAttribute(normals,3));
  steelGeometry.setIndex(indices);
  steelGeometry.userData.preserveTopology=true;
  steelGeometry.computeBoundingSphere();
  geometries.add(steelGeometry);
  const shell=new T.Mesh(steelGeometry,metal);
  shell.name='Corrugated container, corner castings and fittings';
  shell.castShadow=true;shell.receiveShadow=true;group.add(shell);

  function canvasFace(name,w,h,width,height,shape) {
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=width;canvas.height=height;
    const context=canvas.getContext('2d');
    const map=new T.CanvasTexture(canvas);
    map.colorSpace=T.SRGBColorSpace;map.minFilter=T.LinearFilter;map.generateMipmaps=false;
    const mat=new T.MeshBasicMaterial({map});
    const geo=shape || new T.PlaneGeometry(w,h);
    textures.add(map);materials.add(mat);geometries.add(geo);
    const mesh=new T.Mesh(geo,mat);mesh.name=name;group.add(mesh);
    return {mesh,context,map};
  }
  const sign=canvasFace('기원함',5.18,.345,1024,78);
  sign.mesh.position.set(0,2.23,1.175);
  if(sign.context){
    const ctx=sign.context;
    ctx.fillStyle=fascia;ctx.fillRect(0,0,1024,78);
    ctx.fillStyle='#fff2d3';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
    ctx.font='bold 54px '+FONT;ctx.fillText('기원함',512,39);
    sign.map.needsUpdate=true;
  }
  const empty=canvasFace('Wall status',3.48,.304,512,48);
  empty.mesh.position.set(0,1.17,1.184);

  // The front face stops at the diagonal fold. A separate angled facet lifts its corner
  // in silhouette, independent of whether the renderer supports normal maps.
  const paperFace=new T.BufferGeometry();
  paperFace.setAttribute('position',new T.Float32BufferAttribute([
    -.5,-.5,0,.5,-.5,0,.5,.30,0,.415,.5,0,-.5,.5,0
  ],3));
  paperFace.setAttribute('uv',new T.Float32BufferAttribute([0,0,1,0,1,.8,.915,1,0,1],2));
  paperFace.setIndex([0,1,2,0,2,3,0,3,4]);paperFace.computeVertexNormals();
  const foldGeo=new T.BufferGeometry();
  foldGeo.setAttribute('position',new T.Float32BufferAttribute([.5,.30,.004,.479,.459,.038,.415,.5,.004],3));
  foldGeo.computeVertexNormals();
  const foldMat=new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:'#ddd2b2',side:T.DoubleSide,roughness:.94});
  const pinGeo=new T.IcosahedronGeometry(1,0),pinMat=new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:'#a34f3d',roughness:.53});
  const billPin=new T.MeshStandardMaterial({color:'#587884',metalness:.38,roughness:.43});
  materials.add(billPin);
  materials.add(foldMat);materials.add(pinMat);geometries.add(foldGeo);geometries.add(pinGeo);geometries.add(paperFace);
  const cards=[];
  const noteColors=[['#a85846','#fff2d5'],['#476e66','#f1f4df'],['#596f83','#f4f1df']];
  function createCard() {
    const card=canvasFace('A pinned departure wish',1,1,384,159,paperFace);
    const fold=new T.Mesh(foldGeo,foldMat);card.mesh.add(fold);
    const pin=new T.Mesh(pinGeo,pinMat);
    pin.scale.set(.023,.052,.022);pin.position.set(-.19,.448,.022);card.mesh.add(pin);
    card.pin=pin;card.mesh.traverse(tag);
    cards.push(card);return card;
  }
  group.traverse(tag);
  let signature, disposed=false;
  function setWishes(call,wishes) {
    if(disposed)return;
    const real=call&&Array.isArray(wishes)?wishes.filter(n=>n&&
      (!n.callId||n.callId===call.id)&&(n.side??n.wallSide??n.wall_side??'left')===wallSide&&clean(n.text,160)
    ).slice(0,MAX_NOTES):[];
    const entries=real.map(n=>({id:n.id,name:clean(n.name,32),text:clean(n.text,160),kind:(n.kind??n.noteKind??n.note_kind)==='bl'?'bl':'wish'}));
    const vessel=call?clean([call.name,call.voy].filter(Boolean).join(' '),72):'';
    const key=JSON.stringify([call?.id,vessel,Boolean(call?.atd),entries]);
    if(key===signature)return;signature=key;

    empty.mesh.visible=!entries.length;
    if(!entries.length && empty.context){
      const ec=empty.context;ec.fillStyle='#d5c7a6';ec.fillRect(0,0,512,48);
      ec.fillStyle='#43594e';ec.font='600 23px '+FONT;ec.textAlign='center';ec.textBaseline='middle';
      ec.fillText(!call?'관제소에서 모선을 선택해 주세요':call.atd?'이곳에 남긴 기념 쪽지가 없어요':'기원문이나 게임 B/L을 붙여 주세요',256,25);
      empty.map.needsUpdate=true;
    }
    const dense=entries.length>6, cols=dense?4:3, pitchX=dense?1.255:1.68, pitchY=dense?.48:.68;
    entries.forEach((entry,i)=>{
      const card=cards[i] || createCard(),row=Math.floor(i/cols),col=i%cols;
      const seed=Number.isFinite(Number(entry.id))?Math.abs(Number(entry.id))%24:i;
      const [accent,paper]=noteColors[seed%noteColors.length];
      const xx=(col-(cols-1)/2)*pitchX, yy=(dense?1.615:1.49)-row*pitchY;
      card.mesh.position.set(xx,yy,1.207+(i%2)*.004);
      card.mesh.rotation.z=((seed%7)-3)*.007;
      card.mesh.scale.set(dense?1.15:1.47,dense?.415:.565,1);
      card.mesh.visible=true;
      const bill=entry.kind==='bl';
      card.pin.material=bill?billPin:pinMat;
      card.mesh.name=bill?'Pinned game B/L keepsake':'A pinned departure wish';
      card.mesh.userData.noteKind=entry.kind;
      const noteKey=JSON.stringify([entry.kind,entry.name,entry.text,accent,paper,bill?vessel:'']);
      if(card.noteKey!==noteKey && card.context){
        if(bill)drawBL(card.context,entry,vessel);else drawNote(card.context,entry,accent,paper);
        card.map.needsUpdate=true;card.noteKey=noteKey;
      }
    });
    for(let i=entries.length;i<cards.length;i++)cards[i].mesh.visible=false;
  }
  const cos=Math.cos(yaw),sin=Math.sin(yaw);
  function blocks(px,pz){
    const dx=px-x,dz=pz-z;
    const localX=dx*cos-dz*sin,localZ=dx*sin+dz*cos;
    return Math.abs(localX)<W/2+.12 && localZ>bounds.minZ-.12 && localZ<bounds.maxZ+.12;
  }
  function destroy(){
    if(disposed)return;disposed=true;parent.remove(group);
    geometries.forEach(g=>g.dispose());materials.forEach(m=>m.dispose());textures.forEach(t=>t.dispose());
  }
  setWishes(null,[]);
  return {group,side:wallSide,dimensions,setWishes,blocks,destroy};
}


/* ===== FILE: /bench-seats.js ===== */
// Shared by the visible benches, seated avatars and terminal presence.
export const smokingSeats=[-1,1].flatMap(side=>[-2.02,2.02].map(along=>{
  const x=-14.85+side*2.30,z=12.2+along;
  return {
    id:(side<0?'west':'east')+'-'+(along<0?'north':'south'),
    x,y:.8275,z,yaw:-side*Math.PI/2,approach:{x:x-side*1.1,z},
  };
}));


/* ===== FILE: /office-speech.js ===== */
// Screen-space text stays readable even when the 3D scene uses a reduced-size
// canvas renderer. Only its anchor is projected into the scene.
export function mountOfficeSpeech(THREE,layer,anchor){
  const point=new THREE.Vector3(),tags=new Map();
  const stack=document.createElement('div');
  stack.className='harbor-office-speech';
  stack.style.cssText='position:absolute;left:0;top:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:3px;width:max-content;max-width:min(82%,19rem);pointer-events:none;visibility:hidden;';
  layer.append(stack);
  function paint(camera,player,width,height,messages=[]){
    const expected=new Set(messages.map(m=>m.id));
    for(const [id,tag] of tags)if(!expected.has(id)){tag.remove();tags.delete(id);}
    for(const message of messages){
      let tag=tags.get(message.id);
      if(!tag){
        tag=document.createElement('div');tag.className='harbor-office-bubble';
        tag.style.cssText='box-sizing:border-box;width:max-content;max-width:100%;padding:5px 9px;border:1px solid #a58c73;border-radius:12px 12px 12px 4px;background:#fff9edfa;color:#263a45;box-shadow:0 2px 7px #19303833;font:650 13px/1.35 system-ui,sans-serif;text-align:center;overflow-wrap:anywhere;';
        tag.textContent=message.text;stack.append(tag);tags.set(message.id,tag);
      }
      tag.style.opacity=String(message.opacity??1);
    }
    const target=anchor();
    let visible=messages.length>0&&Boolean(target?.parent)&&width>0&&height>0;
    if(visible){
      target.getWorldPosition(point);point.y+=.70;
      visible=point.distanceToSquared(player)<22*22;point.project(camera);
      visible=visible&&point.z>=-1&&point.z<1&&Math.abs(point.x)<1.1&&Math.abs(point.y)<1.1;
    }
    stack.style.visibility=visible?'visible':'hidden';
    if(!visible)return;
    const half=stack.offsetWidth/2,tall=stack.offsetHeight;
    const x=Math.max(half+8,Math.min(width-half-8,(1+point.x)*width/2));
    const y=Math.max(Math.min(tall+8,height-8),Math.min(height-8,(1-point.y)*height/2));
    stack.style.transform='translate('+Math.round(x)+'px,'+Math.round(y)+'px) translate(-50%,-100%)';
  }
  return {paint,dispose(){stack.remove();tags.clear();}};
}
